### [1] [Green-function Zeros Encode Competing Mott and Charge-ordering Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00124)
*Peizhi Mai,Philip W. Phillips*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过解析和数值方法研究扩展Hubbard模型，建立了一个新的强关联物质范式：Green函数零点的色散由微观自旋-自旋关联和缺陷运动学共同决定，并且在Mott相与棋盘电荷密度波相之间的一阶相变处发生不连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然关联绝缘体缺乏低能准粒子极点，但其Green函数通过零点保留着清晰的动量结构。然而，关于零点究竟意味着什么尚不清楚，本文旨在阐明Green函数零点色散的物理含义。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法研究扩展Hubbard模型，分析Green函数零点的色散关系，并考察其与自旋关联和缺陷运动学的关系，同时检验对进一步近邻跳跃的鲁棒性。

Result: 发现Green函数零点的色散由微观自旋-自旋关联和缺陷运动学决定；在Mott相与棋盘电荷密度波相转变处，色散发生不连续变化，符合一阶相变预期；该物理对进一步近邻跳跃的引入具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 是Green函数零点的色散而非零点本身编码了由强关联导致的序的物理，这为理解强关联体系中Green函数零点提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While correlated insulators are devoid of low-energy quasiparticle poles, their Green functions retain clean momentum structure through zeros. However, precisely what zeros imply is not clear. By studying the extended Hubbard model both analytically and numerically, we establish a new paradigm for strongly correlated matter: the dispersion of Green function zeros is determined by both microscopic spin-spin correlations and defect kinematics. In fact, we find that the dispersion changes discontinuously across the transition between the Mott and the checkerboard charge-density wave phases as is expected for a first-order transition. This physics is robust to the inclusion of further neighbor hopping which simply fine tunes the spin-correlation or charge-defect kinematics. We conclude that it is the {\it dispersion} of the Green-function zeros that encode the physics of ordering resultant from the strong correlations.

</details>


### [2] [Higher-Winding Fractionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00139)
*Kishore Iyer,Christophe Mora,Daniele Guerci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究高拓扑数的斯格明子（skyrmion）纹理产生的多磁通量子涌现磁场，发现这种设置下，涌现磁场的晶格尺度非均匀性会驱动分数化液体与晶体介电态之间的BKT相变，且该相变具有拓扑特征——通量插入下的多体极化给出量子化绕数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高缠绕数的斯格明子纹理可在每个元胞内产生多磁通量子数的涌现磁场，这可能为分数量子反常霍尔态在微观整数填充下出现提供新途径。理解这类系统中涌现磁场不均匀性如何影响基态性质和相变具有重要意义。

Method: 研究在斯格明子产生的涌现磁场背景下，通过调节晶格尺度的磁场非均匀性，分析系统从分数化液体到晶体介电态的相变行为，并使用通量插入下多体极化的量子化绕数作为拓扑序的判据。

Result: 发现增强涌现磁场的晶格尺度非均匀性会驱动BKT相变，从分数化液体态转变为晶体介电态。该相变具有明确的拓扑特征：在分数化相中多体极化的缠绕数非零，而在介电晶体相中为零。

Conclusion: 涌现磁场的非均匀性可以作为调控分数化量子反常霍尔态与晶体介电态之间BKT相变的控制参数，且多体极化的量子化绕数为该相变提供了可观测的拓扑签名。

Abstract: Higher-winding skyrmion textures can generate emergent magnetic fields with multiple flux quanta per unit cell. This opens an intriguing route toward fractionalization, allowing fractionalized quantum anomalous Hall states to arise even at integer filling of the microscopic unit cell. We show that, in this setting, increasing lattice-scale inhomogeneity of the emergent magnetic field drives a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition between a fractionalized liquid and a crystalline dielectric state. This transition carries a topological signature: under flux insertion, the many-body polarization defines a quantized winding number that is nonzero in the fractionalized phase and vanishes in the dielectric crystal.

</details>


### [3] [Engineering tunable $p$-wave magnetism in antiferromagnetic bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00277)
*Yu-Han Lin,Jin-Wei Dong,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过对称性引导的方法，在由两个AB堆叠反铁磁方形晶格组成的双分子层系统中实现可调谐的p波磁性，无需自旋-轨道耦合即可在完全补偿的磁性态中产生奇宇称自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p波磁性（具有奇宇称自旋劈裂的磁性态）是凝聚态物理中的新兴研究方向，以往的研究主要依赖于自旋-轨道耦合来实现，需要寻找一个无需自旋-轨道耦合、可在平衡态下实现和调控p波磁性的最小平台。

Method: 采用对称性分析驱动的方法：构建由两个AB堆叠反铁磁方形晶格组成的双分子层系统，通过层间面内相对位移选择性破坏保护自旋简并的对称性，同时保留与时间反演相关的约束。并将该系统映射为有效的键调制方形晶格，在平均场框架内研究相应的Hubbard模型。

Result: 面内位移可以连续调控p波自旋劈裂的方向和大小及相关自旋响应；平均场计算得到的相图显示，在广泛参数区域中可自发产生具有共面或手性自旋织构的p波磁性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在二维系统中实现和操控p波磁性的最小平衡态平台，为无自旋-轨道耦合条件下的p波磁学研究提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We propose a symmetry-guided route to engineer tunable $p$-wave magnetism in a bilayer system composed of two AB-stacked antiferromagnetic square lattices with their collinear moments nonparallel to each other. We show that an in-plane relative shift between the two layers selectively breaks the symmetries protecting spin degeneracy while preserving time-reversal-related constraints, thereby generating odd-parity spin splitting in a fully compensated magnetic state without spin-orbit coupling. The direction and the magnitude of the resulting $p$-wave spin-splitting, together with the associated spin responses, can be continuously tuned by the in-plane displacement, providing a potential knob for control. We further map the bilayer system onto an effective bond-modulated square lattice and investigate the corresponding Hubbard model within a mean-field framework. The calculated phase diagram reveals extended regions where $p$-wave magnetism with coplanar or chiral spin textures emerges spontaneously. Our results establish a minimal equilibrium platform for realizing and manipulating $p$-wave magnetism in two-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [4] [Bound states, resonances, and their thermodynamic properties in pseudospin-1 systems with short-range impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00433)
*E. V. Gorbar,Pavlo Sukhachov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分析了在带隙和无带隙赝自旋-1系统中，由短程杂质（圆形势阱）在平带和色散带附近诱导的束缚态和共振态，发现来源于平带的束缚态具有不同于赝自旋-1/2费米子的独特性质，并研究了其对热力学性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解赝自旋-1系统中杂质诱导的束缚态与共振态，特别是与赝自旋-1/2系统相比，平带衍生的束缚态在多分量结构下呈现出的异常特征。

Method: 采用圆形势阱建模短程杂质，解析分析束缚态和共振态；利用Anderson模型描述无序势中的独立电子，计算热力学势、熵密度和热容。

Result: 平带衍生的束缚态存在于任意总角动量j值；能量随|j|增大而降低；波函数局域在势阱边缘且局域化程度随|j|增强。由平带束缚态主导时，熵密度随温度单调增加并饱和，热容呈现单一峰值。

Conclusion: 平带衍生的束缚态在赝自旋-1系统中表现出独特的物理性质，与赝自旋-1/2系统及色散带上方的束缚态有本质区别，并在热力学响应上有可区分的特征标志。

Abstract: Bound states and resonances induced by short-range impurities modeled by circular potential wells are analyzed in the vicinity of flat and dispersive bands in gapped and gapless pseudospin-1 systems. We find that the bound and resonant states derived from the flat band show unusual characteristics originating from the multicomponent structure of pseudospin-1 fermions, which are distinct from those for pseudospin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions. Contrary to gapped Dirac systems and unlike bound states in the vicinity of the upper dispersive band, the bound states derived from the flat band occur for any value of the total angular momentum. The energies of these bound states with higher angular momentum $j$ tend to decrease with $|j|$. In addition, it is found that their wave functions are localized at the potential well edge and the localization increases with $|j|$. The signatures of the impurity states in the local density of states are determined. Using the Anderson model for independent electrons in the disorder potential, the thermodynamic potential, entropy density, and heat capacity are obtained. In the regime dominated by bound states derived from the flat band, the entropy density monotonically increases with temperature and saturates, whereas the heat capacity exhibits a single maximum.

</details>


### [5] [Correlation-Driven Nonlinear Magnetoelectric Response in an Altermagnet: A Dynamical Mean-Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00697)
*Robert Peters,Jun Ōiké*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学平均场论研究了强关联交替磁体中的光学非线性磁电效应（NMEE），发现NMEE在交替磁相有限、在顺磁相为零，其频率依赖可估算交替磁自旋劈裂尺度，且向相互作用驱动的磁相边界调谐可最大化响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对交替磁体中光学非线性磁电效应的描述多基于有效能带模型并强加自旋劈裂，缺乏强关联效应下自洽的第一性原理描述。本文旨在通过DMFT自洽地确定交替磁序、电子谱与非线性光学响应，从而更真实地研究NMEE并探索增强该响应的方法。

Method: 采用动力学平均场论（DMFT）求解强关联交替磁体模型，自洽地确定交替磁序、电子谱和光学非线性响应，而非依赖预先强加自旋劈裂的有效能带描述。通过改变相互作用强度和温度来研究NMEE的行为及其参数依赖性。

Result: NMEE在交替磁相中有限、在顺磁相中为零；其频率依赖反映了自旋分辨的谱结构，可用于估算交替磁自旋劈裂的特征尺度。在低温下减小相互作用向相互作用驱动的磁相边界靠近会增强响应，而升高温度则抑制响应并在临界温度以上使其归零。

Conclusion: 光学NMEE可作为关联交替磁序的有效探针，且通过压力、应变或化学替代等手段将系统调谐至相互作用驱动的相边界附近，是最大化该非线性响应的一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: We investigate the optical nonlinear magnetoelectric effect (NMEE) in a strongly correlated altermagnet using dynamical mean-field theory. Unlike effective band descriptions with an imposed spin splitting, our approach determines the altermagnetic order, electronic spectrum, and optical nonlinear response self-consistently. We find that the NMEE is finite in the altermagnetic phase and vanishes in the paramagnetic phase. Its frequency dependence reflects the spin-resolved spectral structure and provides an estimate of the characteristic altermagnetic spin-splitting scale. Interaction and temperature tuning produce qualitatively different behavior: at low temperature, reducing the interaction strength toward the interaction-driven magnetic phase boundary enhances the response, whereas increasing the temperature suppresses it and drives it to zero above the critical temperature. These results establish the optical NMEE as a probe of correlated altermagnetic order and suggest that tuning parameters such as pressure, strain, or chemical substitution toward an interaction-driven phase boundary may provide a promising route to maximizing the response.

</details>


### [6] [Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo applied to a simple fermionic model: A critical assessment of the method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01301)
*Michel Caffarel,Manon Pinar,Anthony Scemama*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过一维周期边界条件模型研究了自修复扩散蒙特卡洛（SHDMC）方法的收敛行为。模型引入反对称性以模拟费米子波函数性质，但节点结构仅部分受对称性约束。研究发现SHDMC迭代中节点演化可描述为动力学系统，存在吸引和排斥不动点；标准SHDMC在该模型下能量随迭代增加且节点收敛到错误值，表明SHDMC并非总能收敛到正确解。通过修改节点更新准则（更重视节点区域）可部分改善此问题，但一般情况可能需要局域基组才能实现收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验SHDMC方法在非平凡模型中的收敛性。由于真实费米子系统的节点结构仅部分由对称性约束，需要一个能体现这一特征的非平凡测试平台来评估SHDMC的节点优化能力。

Method: 采用一维周期性模型，引入反演对称性区分玻色子（偶本征态）与费米子（奇本征态）扇区。将SHDMC节点演化为动力学系统分析，并测试标准SHDMC及修改节点更新准则（加强节点区域权重）后的变体。

Result: 节点演化表现为具有吸引和排斥不动点的动力学系统；标准SHDMC产生固定节点能量升高且节点收敛到错误值；修改节点更新准则后问题部分缓解。

Conclusion: SHDMC并非总能收敛到正确解，标准公式下存在失败模式；节点区域加权的更新准则可部分改善；通用收敛可能需要局域基组。

Abstract: We investigate the Self-Healing Diffusion Monte Carlo (SHDMC) method using a one-dimensional model with periodic boundary conditions. An inversion symmetry is introduced to mimic the antisymmetry property of fermionic wavefunctions, with the bosonic and fermionic sectors being modeled by the even and odd eigenstates, respectively. As in realistic fermionic systems, the nodal structure is only partially constrained by symmetry, making this model a non-trivial testbed for nodal optimization algorithms such as SHDMC. We show that the nodal evolution under SHDMC iterations can be cast into a dynamical system exhibiting both attractive and repulsive fixed points. In the standard formulation of SHDMC applied to this model, the fixed-node energy is found to increase upon iteration, and the node converges to a wrong value, indicating that SHDMC does not always converge to the correct solution. We further show that this problem is partially cured by modifying the nodal update criterion to give more importance to the nodal region. Achieving convergence in the general case very likely requires the use of a localized basis set, as is the case for this model.

</details>

### [7] [Inverse kinematic solution for generic 3R positional robots using Conformal Geometric Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00311)
*Abhilash Nayak,Durgesh Haribhau Salunkhe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用共形几何代数(CGA)研究通用3R机器人的逆运动学问题，通过将问题重构为两个圆的交点，直接得到关于theta_2的一元多项式，无需消去theta_1和theta_3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3R机器人逆运动学求解主要依赖代数方法，其几何解释（一对二次曲线的交点）局限于关节空间域。作者希望借助CGA的优势来获得对3R机器人运动学性质的更深入理解。

Method: 采用共形几何代数(CGA)框架，将3R机器人的逆运动学模型重新构建为两个圆的交点问题，利用CGA中圆作为基本元素的性质，直接推导出仅含theta_2的一元多项式，无需消去其余两个关节变量。

Result: 成功将3R机器人逆运动学问题转化为两个圆的几何交点问题，并直接获得关于theta_2的一元多项式表达式。

Conclusion: CGA为3R机器人逆运动学提供了新颖的几何视角，通过圆交点的表述方式简化了传统代数求解的复杂性，为机器人运动学性质的深入分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The inverse kinematics of generic 3R robots has been investigated through multiple approaches, mainly algebraic methods involving the solution of certain equation sets. Previous geometric interpretations of the solution, characterized as the intersection of a pair of conics have been confined to the joint-space domain. In this article, we study the Inverse Kinematic Model (IKM) of 3R robots, using the advantages of Conformal Geometric Algebra (CGA) to provide further insights on its kinematic properties. Our approach directly yields a univariate polynomial in terms of theta_2 without the need to eliminate theta_1 and theta_3 by reframing the problem as the intersection of two circles, which are fundamental elements within this algebraic framework.

</details>


### [8] [Geometric analysis of generic 3R robots, and necessary and sufficient conditions for a class of orthogonal robots to have four IKS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00316)
*Durgesh Haribhau Salunkhe,Abhilash Nayak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合代数与几何方法优势的通用框架，基于共形几何代数（CGA）的逆运动学模型，对通用3R机器人的运动学进行分析，研究其具有四个逆运动学解（IKS）的条件及工作空间中IKS的分布，并给出了正交机器人类的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去的代数方法虽已建立具体结果，但局限于特殊机器人类型或架构简化；几何方法虽能推广到通用机器人并提供直观理解，但缺乏严格的代数结论。作者希望结合两者优点，对通用3R机器人进行全面的运动学分析。

Method: 采用共形几何代数（CGA）方法推导通用的逆运动学模型作为基础，在此基础上构建一个通用框架，用于研究3R机器人具有四个逆运动学解（IKS）的条件，并分析IKS在工作空间中的分布情况。针对正交机器人类别，推导出充要条件。

Result: 成功建立了一个通用框架，能够判定通用3R机器人是否有四个逆运动学解，并研究解的分布在工作空间中的情况。同时，针对正交机器人这一类特殊机器人，给出了其存在四个IKS的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了代数方法的严谨性与几何方法的直观性与通用性，为通用3R机器人运动学分析提供了一种更全面的解决方案，并为后续研究更广泛类别机器人的IKS特性奠定了基础。

Abstract: The kinematic analysis of a generic 3R robot has been investigated with multiple approaches in the past. The algebraic approaches have established concrete results but are unfortunately limited to special classes or architectural simplifications. Geometric approaches on the other hand have extended the analysis to generic robots while also providing an intuitive understanding of their kinematic properties. We use the best of both approaches to present the kinematic analysis of a generic 3R robot, using the inverse kinematic model inherited from a method based on conformal geometric algebra. The paper discusses a generic framework to study the conditions for a 3R robot to have four inverse kinematic solutions (IKS) and allows to study the distribution of IKS as seen in workspace. The necessary and sufficient conditions for a class of orthogonal robots are presented using the proposed approach.

</details>


### [9] [Scene Graph-based Driving Scenario Extraction for Automotive Egocentric Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00333)
*Stefan Ramdhan,Kyanna Dagenais,Vera Pantelic,Victor Bandur,Mark Lawford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用场景图（Scene Graphs）和线性时序逻辑（LTL）从无标注的真实世界传感器数据流中自动提取驾驶场景的方法，通过形式化定义感兴趣的驾驶场景并通过现成的模型检查器从场景图序列中提取所有匹配实例，方法可同时适用于仿真和真实世界数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无标注的真实世界传感器数据流中提取场景是自动驾驶系统开发中的关键且具有挑战性的任务。从大规模数据集中自动定位关键场景的时间和空间位置，能够支持基于场景的ADS数据集覆盖分析，但现有方法缺乏有效手段。

Method: 首先处理以自我为中心的传感器数据和HD地图，生成表征驾驶场景的场景图序列；然后使用LTL形式化指定感兴趣的驾驶场景；最后利用现成的模型检查器，将LTL公式作用于场景图序列进行评估，从中提取所有场景实例。该方法同时适用于仿真和真实世界数据集。

Result: 在Argoverse 2的训练和验证数据集（包含850段15秒的真实世界驾驶日志）以及多段行车记录仪视频上进行了评估，并与基于轨迹标注和HD地图的规则基准进行比较，证明了方法在场景提取和查询方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效地从大规模无标注的真实世界驾驶数据中形式化地提取感兴趣的驾驶场景，支持场景覆盖分析，并已在真实数据集上得到验证。

Abstract: Extracting scenarios from unlabelled real-world sensor data streams is a critical but challenging task in the development process of automated driving systems (ADS). Automatically sifting through large datasets to spatially and temporally locate critical scenarios can enable scenario-based coverage analysis of ADS datasets. In this paper, we present a method for extracting scenarios from egocentric datasets using scene graphs and Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). We first process egocentric sensor data and HD maps to generate a sequence of scene graphs representing a driving scenario. Next, we use LTL to formally specify driving scenarios of interest, then extract all instances of the scenarios from the dataset using an off-the-shelf model checker, which evaluates the LTL formula against the sequence of scene graphs. Our approach can be used on both simulated and real world datasets. We evaluate the method on the training and validation datasets from Argoverse 2 consisting of 850 15-second real-world driving logs, and several videos of dashcam footage. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for extracting and querying scenarios by evaluating against a rule-based benchmark based on track annotations and HD maps.

</details>

*Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Jinchang Xu,Jiawei Wang,Yushu Qiu,Chen Lv,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准安全强化学习方法在高速超车场景中通常依赖隐式的基于价值的风险估计，而非显式的动力学建模，难以在多步时域内准确捕捉复杂的风险传播过程，往往导致局部安全但长期潜藏重大风险的行为。

Method: 提出WM-RMoE框架：(1) 学习一个潜在动力学模型以支持并行多步推演，将安全评估从动作层级提升到轨迹层级，通过累积风险评估实现；(2) 设计层次化门控机制，在长时域、短时域和基于规则的安全模块之间动态协调专家，以应对不同交互强度；(3) 引入高斯混合模型保留多模态机动分支，缓解行为模式平均化的弊端。

Result: 实验结果表明WM-RMoE在安全合规性、决策稳定性和泛化能力方面显著优于代表性基线。基于风险感知的公式化设计使框架能够在不同交通密度下生成具有前瞻性和语义上截然不同的超车机动。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模潜在动力学并融合多层级的风险感知专家决策机制，WM-RMoE有效解决了高速超车场景中多步时序风险难以捕捉的难题，同时兼顾了前瞻性决策和机动多样性。

Abstract: Autonomous highway overtaking demands foresighted decision-making to handle complex interactions, stochastic traffic evolution, and temporal risk accumulation. However, standard safe reinforcement learning approaches typically rely on implicit value-based risk estimations rather than explicit dynamics modeling, thereby struggling to accurately capture complex risk propagation over multi-step horizons. This limitation frequently results in behaviors that are locally safe but induce substantial latent risks in the long term. To address this, a World Model-based Risk-aware Mixture-of-Experts (WM-RMoE) framework is proposed. First, a learned latent dynamics model facilitates parallel multi-step rollouts, elevating safety assessment from the action level to the trajectory level via cumulative risk evaluation. Second, to enhance robustness under varying interaction intensities, a hierarchical gating mechanism dynamically coordinates experts across long-horizon, short-horizon, and rule-based safety modules. Furthermore, a Gaussian Mixture Model is integrated to preserve multimodal maneuvering branches, thereby mitigating the issue of behavioral mode averaging. Experimental results demonstrate that WM-RMoE significantly outperforms representative baselines in terms of safety compliance, decision stability, and generalization capability. Furthermore, benefiting from the risk-aware formulation, the proposed framework uniquely exhibits the ability to generate foresighted and semantically distinct overtaking maneuvers across diverse traffic densities.

</details>

*Yuzhi Lai,William Marx,Shenghai Yuan,Peizheng Li,Zhuoyu Ran,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从任务导向视角研究凝视辅助操作中的跨设备空间对齐问题，提出了无标记交互框架、跨设备数据集、基于图的参考选择方法，以及直接衡量凝视指定目标空间误差的Gaze-Surface Intersection Error (GSIE)指标。实验表明传统位姿指标排名高的对齐方法在GSIE上不一定最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝视辅助操作通常只支持物体选择，而任意位置放置需要头显与机器人之间的精确空间对齐。然而对于凝视操作，位姿准确性不一定转化为任务准确性——平移误差和旋转误差共同影响变换后的凝视射线，且可能相互补偿。因此需要从任务导向角度研究跨设备对齐问题。

Method: 提出了无标记交互框架和专门的跨设备数据集；提出基于图的参考选择方法来解决机器人参考点稀疏的问题；在统一协议下开发和基准测试了多个任务特定对齐流水线；引入了Gaze-Surface Intersection Error (GSIE)作为直接衡量凝视指定目标空间误差的指标。

Result: 实验表明，传统位姿指标排名较高的对齐方法在GSIE指标下并不总是最优的，证明了在任务层面评估凝视操作的重要性。

Conclusion: 凝视辅助操作的评估不应仅依赖传统位姿精度指标，而应从任务级视角（如GSIE）进行评估，以更真实地反映系统在实际操作中的性能。

Abstract: Gaze-based assistive manipulation typically supports object selection, while arbitrary-position placement requires accurate spatial alignment between the headset and robot. However, for gaze-based manipulation, pose accuracy does not necessarily translate into task accuracy: translational and rotational errors jointly affect the transformed gaze ray and may compensate for each other. To study cross-device alignment from this task-oriented perspective, we present a markerless interaction framework and a dedicated cross-device dataset. We propose Graph-based Reference Selection to address sparse robot references. We further develop and benchmark multiple task-specific alignment pipelines under a unified protocol. Specifically, we introduce Gaze--Surface Intersection Error (GSIE), which directly measures the spatial error of the gaze-specified target. Experiments show that alignment methods ranked highly by conventional pose metrics are not always optimal in GSIE, demonstrating the importance of evaluating gaze-based manipulation at the task level.

</details>


### [12] [Exploring Nonlinear Body Oscillations for Natural Quadruped Gaits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00539)
*Annika Schmidt,Davide Calzolari,Florian Loeffl,Arne Sachtler,Daniel Seidel,Milan Herrmann,Robert Burger,Thomas Gumpert,Antonin Raffin,Tristan Ehlert,Maximilian Pries,David Wandinger,Florian Schmidt,Manuel Keppler,Jinoh Lee,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用非线性共振实现多步态机器人的框架，通过使机器人的非线性动力学在设计阶段可预测，让多步态从由重力、惯性和弹性塑造的非线性共振中自然涌现。作者在高顺应性四足机器人eBert上，利用新理论工具识别出六个非线性正则模态（NNMs），并通过仿真和硬件验证其存在性。结合黑箱优化确定步长，仿真和硬件实验均表明每个NNM能自然发展成具有不同速度的独特步态，展示了机器人通过利用自身力学产生任务特定动作的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物的身体形态塑造了它们能执行的步态模式，机械共振减少了对主动控制的需求。然而，大多数四足机器人并未专门设计以利用机械共振，因为非线性动力学的复杂性使这类设计需要专用运动控制器。本文旨在提供一种替代方案，使非线性动力学在设计过程中可预测，从而让多步态能从非线性共振中自然涌现。

Method: 本文提出一个概念验证框架：(1) 使用新理论工具表征非线性机械系统的正则模态，使非线性动力学在设计阶段可预测；(2) 设计高顺应性四足机器人eBert，其动力学由重力、惯性和弹性共同塑造；(3) 识别六个非线性正则模态（NNMs）并在仿真和硬件中验证；(4) 使用黑箱优化确定步长，使每个NNM自然发展为不同速度和特征的独特步态。

Result: 在仿真中，每个NNM都自然发展成具有不同速度的独特步态；这些结果在相当大程度上成功迁移到真实机器人硬件上。实验表明eBert能够利用其力学机制产生任务特定的运动序列。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了利用非线性共振涌现多步态的概念可行性，为设计新一代灵活高效、充分利用具身智能（embodied intelligence）的机器人奠定了基础。

Abstract: Animals' body morphology shapes the gait patterns they can perform, where mechanical resonance reduces the need for active control. By tuning posture and muscle stiffness, they leverage their embodied intelligence to achieve effective gaits for different speeds. In contrast, most quadruped robots are not specifically designed to exploit mechanical resonance due to the complexity of nonlinear dynamics and require dedicated locomotion controllers. To provide an alternative, we present a proof of concept framework making the nonlinear dynamics of a robot predictable in the design process and show how this knowledge can be leveraged such that multi-gait locomotion can emerge from nonlinear resonances, shaped by gravity, inertia, and elasticity. We present the highly compliant quadruped robot eBert, on which we identify six nonlinear normal modes (NNMs) using our new theoretical tools and validate their existence in simulation and hardware. With black-box optimization to determine step length, simulations show how each NNM naturally develops into a distinct gait, manifesting different speeds, which also largely transfers to the robotic hardware. Our experiments show that eBert can exploit its mechanics to generate task-specific movements which may serve as foundation for designing a new generation of agile and efficient robots leveraging embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [13] [Potential-Guided Particle Steering for Negation-Constrained Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00555)
*Geonho Kim,SooGon Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需否定性训练数据的推理时框架，通过结合序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）与分类器自由引导（CFG），解决语言驱动的灵巧抓取模型中"指令指定不抓取区域"时的系统性失败问题，并在新基准NegGrasp上将约束违反率从57.9%降至17.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动的灵巧抓取模型（如DextER）在指令同时指定"抓取何处"和"避免何处"时会系统性失败。原因有二：一是现有训练语料（如DexGYSNet）几乎没有包含避免类指令；二是训练中每个提及部件都被视为接触目标，模型可能将禁止部件误解为另一个应抓取区域而非应避免的区域。

Method: 提出一个不依赖否定性训练样本的推理时框架。核心思路是将序贯蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo, SMC）与分类器自由引导（classifier-free guidance）相结合：CFG引导采样朝向被指示的目标部件，同时SMC剪枝掉朝向禁止区域的候选抓取。此外，利用一个冻结的3D部件接地模型（part-grounding model）从语言指令中定位禁止区域。同时构建了NegGrasp基准，包含成对的正面/负面指令以及约束感知指标（仅在抓取既完成任务又遵守约束时才计为成功）。

Result: 在NegGrasp基准上，该方法将最强基线的违反率从57.9%降至17.2%，同时在约束感知成功率与物理成功率两项指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过推理时的方法设计（而非收集海量的否定性训练数据），可以有效地将避免约束整合到灵巧抓取模型中，为语言驱动的抓取建模在更复杂、约束更丰富的真实场景中提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Language-driven dexterous grasp models, such as DextER, perform well when instructions specify where to grasp, but we find they fail systematically when an instruction also specifies where not to grasp (e.g., "grasp the handle but avoid the body"). Existing training corpora, DexGYSNet among them, contain virtually no avoidance instructions, and collecting examples for every possible constraint is impractical. Moreover, because every part mentioned during training denotes a contact target, models may interpret a forbidden part as another region to grasp rather than one to avoid. We therefore introduce an inference-time framework for negation-constrained dexterous grasping that requires no negation-specific training examples. Combining Sequential Monte Carlo with classifier-free guidance, our method guides sampling toward the instructed part while pruning candidates headed for the forbidden region, without any negation examples during training. A frozen 3D part-grounding model localizes the forbidden region from the language instruction. To evaluate this setting, we construct NegGrasp, a benchmark of paired positive/negative instructions with constraint-aware metrics that credit a grasp only if it both accomplishes the task and respects the stated constraint. On NegGrasp, our method reduces the violation rate of the strongest baseline from 57.9% to 17.2% while improving both constraint-aware and physical success.

</details>


### [14] [A Wearable Pneumatic Device for Continuous, Closed-Loop, Bidirectional Tactile Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00612)
*Cosima du Pasquier,Aliyah Smith,Serin Huber,Joshua Phelps,Ilana A. Cohen,Ava Chen,Monroe Kennedy,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套由两个可穿戴气动触觉设备组成的系统，支持在感知相关的力和时间尺度上进行连续、闭环、双向的触觉交互。每个设备包含多达十二个压力传感通道，连接到基于纺织材料的气动囊。每个通道既可作传感器、也可作致动器，或两者兼有。分布式架构支持持续压力输出、快速动态响应以及无线配对。设备性能表征显示力带宽超过30 Hz、快速且阻尼良好的阶跃响应、比以往紧凑型气动平台更持久的压力保持。人类研究表明，基于压力的指尖反馈能够区分力和刚度，在遥操作中将施加压力降低23.1%、任务时长缩短27.4%、主观脑力负荷降低18.8%，尤其适用于视觉受限条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作等应用需要双向触觉交互，即既能感知触摸输入又能提供触觉反馈。然而先前的紧凑型气动设备在压力保持、动态响应和传感/致动集成方面存在局限，难以在感知相关的时间尺度上实现连续、闭环的双向触觉交互。

Method: 采用可穿戴气动触觉设备架构，单设备包含多达十二通道纺织气动囊，每通道基于局部闭环控制实现稳定皮肤压痕（致动模式），也可安装于任意表面测量触摸交互（传感模式）。采用分布式架构支持持续压力输出和快速动态响应，两个相同设备可无线配对实现实时压力信号传输与再现。

Result: 设备级表征显示力带宽超过30 Hz，阶跃响应快速且阻尼良好，压力保持能力优于先前的紧凑气动平台。人类研究表明：基于压力的指尖反馈可区分力和刚度判别；遥操作中施加压力降低23.1%，任务持续时间缩短27.4%；主观脑力负荷降低18.8%，尤其利于视觉受限条件下的表现。

Conclusion: 通过在单一气动模态中统一触觉传感和触觉反馈，该系统为遥操作中的双向触觉交互提供了实用基础，解决了先前景气动平台在传感-致动集成、响应速度和压力保持方面的不足。

Abstract: We present a system of two wearable pneumatic haptic devices that supports continuous, closed-loop, bidirectional tactile interaction at perceptually relevant force and temporal scales. A single device can contain up to twelve pressure sensing channels connected to textile-based pneumatic pouches. Each channel in a device can be used as a sensor, an actuator, or both. As an actuator with integrated sensing, the channel generates stable skin indentation through local closed-loop control. As a sensor, a channel can be mounted (or worn) on any surface, including on a robot gripper or on the human body, and used to measure touch interactions with the environment or a human user. A distributed architecture supports sustained pressure output, rapid dynamic response, and wireless pairing of identical devices in a system to transmit and reproduce tactile pressure signals in real time.
  Device-level characterization demonstrates force bandwidth exceeding 30 Hz, rapid and well-damped step responses, and extended pressure retention compared to prior compact pneumatic platforms. Human studies show that pressure-based fingertip feedback enables discrimination of force and stiffness, improves teleoperated manipulation by reducing applied pressures by up to 23.1% and task duration by up to 27.4%, and lowers subjective mental workload by 18.8%, particularly under visually constrained conditions. By unifying tactile sensing and haptic feedback within a single pneumatic modality, the device provides a practical foundation for bidirectional touch interaction in teleoperation.

</details>


### [15] [DSG: Dynamic 3D Scene Graph Construction for Embodied Agents in Changing Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00619)
*Ming Liao,Chao Ye,Jianing Fei,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DSG框架，用于动态3D场景图构建，通过语义感知的3D高斯场景表示和双视图渲染实现物体变化检测，并结合多粒度视觉上下文的LLM空间关系推理。同时引入AI2-THOR平台上的DynTHOR基准，实验表明在多个数据集上性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内环境中物体位置常因人类活动或具身智能体交互而频繁变化，导致已有场景图与当前场景不一致。现有方法无法有效处理动态环境中的场景图更新问题。

Method: (1) 构建语义感知的3D高斯场景表示，提出基于双视图渲染的物体变化检测方法，实现可靠的场景图节点更新；(2) 提出融入多粒度视觉上下文的空间关系推理方法，使大规模语言模型能够识别更丰富的物体间空间关系；(3) 构建基于AI2-THOR模拟平台的动态室内场景图基准DynTHOR用于评估。

Result: 在DynTHOR、3RScan和真实世界场景上的大量实验表明，DSG在物体节点构建和空间关系推理两方面均优于现有方法，显著提升了动态场景图构建的准确性。

Conclusion: DSG框架有效解决了动态室内场景中场景图与当前场景不一致的问题，通过物体变化检测和基于LLM的空间关系推理，实现了更准确的动态场景图构建。

Abstract: In indoor environments, object positions frequently change due to human activities or embodied-agent interactions, causing previously constructed scene graphs to become inconsistent with the current scene. To address this issue, we propose DSG, a dynamic 3D scene graph construction framework that detects object changes and performs spatial relationship reasoning. First, we construct a semantic-aware 3D Gaussian scene representation and develop a dual-view rendering-based object change detection method to enable reliable scene graph node updates. Second, we propose a spatial relationship reasoning method that incorporates multi-granularity visual context, enabling a large language model to identify a richer set of interobject spatial relationships. Furthermore, we introduce DynTHOR, a dynamic indoor scene graph benchmark built on the AI2-THOR simulation platform for evaluating scene graph construction in dynamic environments. Extensive experiments on Dyn-THOR, 3RScan, and real-world scenes demonstrate that DSG consistently outperforms existing methods in both object node construction and spatial relationship reasoning, significantly improving the accuracy of dynamic scene graph construction.

</details>


### [16] [AM-Bench: A Modular Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Aerial Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00641)
*Yutong Wang,Dongjae Lee,Xiaofeng Guo,Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Bavin Saravanan,Muqing Cao,Jia Xie,Chenyang Mao,Sebastian Scherer,Junyi Geng,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AM-Bench，一个面向多旋翼空中操纵（AM）策略学习的模块化仿真套件与基准测试平台，覆盖欠驱动、全驱动和过驱动等多种机体构型，包含12个跨接触、运输和受限交互的任务，支持可配置气动扰动和致动器饱和，从而实现机体构型、底层控制、高层策略与扰动之间的系统性交互评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操纵学习基准大多聚焦地面支撑系统，难以适用于空中操纵这一动力学关键领域。AM面临环境扰动、操纵器与浮动基座间的耦合动力学及受限自由度等独特挑战，任务性能同时依赖于机体构型、底层控制和策略设计，而现有基准主要强调端到端策略性能，缺乏系统级评估能力。

Method: AM-Bench是一个模块化仿真套件，包含：三种代表性的多旋翼构型（欠驱动、全驱动、过驱动）、12个覆盖接触/运输/受限交互的任务、可配置的气动扰动与致动器饱和模型、标准底层控制器以及基线策略学习算法。其独特之处在于支持系统级评估，考察机体、控制、扰动和策略之间的交互作用。

Result: 通过三项仿真研究（涵盖高层策略、策略-控制接口、机体构型）展示了平台的诊断价值，并进行了真实世界验证以确认建模效应的有效性，同时对学习管线进行了硬件实测。

Conclusion: AM-Bench作为首个面向空中操纵的系统级基准，能够揭示机体构型、底层控制、扰动与高层策略之间的耦合关系，为空中操纵策略学习提供更全面的诊断与评测工具，超越仅关注端到端策略性能的传统操纵基准。

Abstract: Standardized benchmarks have played a central role in advancing robot manipulation learning, yet most focus on ground-supported manipulation systems, which limits their applicability to dynamics-critical domains such as aerial manipulation (AM). AM presents distinct system-level challenges, including environmental disturbances, coupled dynamics between the manipulator and floating base, and constrained degrees of freedom. Consequently, task performance depends jointly on robot embodiment, low-level control, and high-level policy design. We introduce AM-Bench, a modular simulation suite and benchmark for multirotor-based AM policy learning. AM-Bench includes representative embodiments spanning underactuated, fully actuated, and overactuated systems, 12 tasks across contact, transport, and constrained interaction, configurable aerodynamic disturbances and actuator saturation, standard low-level controllers, and baseline policy-learning algorithms. Unlike prior manipulation benchmarks that primarily emphasize end-to-end policy performance, AM-Bench enables system-level evaluation of how embodiment, control, disturbances, and policy choices interact. We demonstrate its diagnostic value through three simulation studies spanning high-level policies, policy--control interfaces, and embodiments, together with real-world validation of modeled effects and a hardware test of the learning pipeline.

</details>

Conclusion: 多通道麦克风阵列结合空间音频处理可以有效支撑嘈杂环境下的多方对话场景，在增强语音清晰度的同时为化身交互提供更沉浸的体验。

Abstract: For noisy real-world environments such as those in open public spaces, spoken dialogue systems for both autonomous robots and avatars should be carefully designed to provide enhanced speech signals. These signals can be used either for speech recognition or, in the case of an avatar system, transmitted as clean speech to a remote operator. This work proposes an audio system that can be used for both these scenarios and was demonstrated as a proof-of-concept at the 2025 World Expo in Osaka. The first scenario is an attentive listening system with the android ERICA, and the second is a conversation support system with mobile Teleco robots, with one of them acting as an avatar for a remote operator. Both systems feature multi-party conversation and use a single multi-channel microphone array. We describe how our audio system not only enhances the speech of multiple speakers in a noisy environment, but provides a form of spatial audio which allows for more immersiveness in avatar-based conversational interactions.

### [18] [Fleets Need a Context Plane: Rethinking Cooperative Perception for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00659)
*Liangkai Liu,Xiaoxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"context plane"（上下文平面），一个轻量级运行时接口，使无人机蜂群协同感知系统能够在飞行中根据任务、带宽、编队几何等多维上下文动态调整特征共享策略，无需重训练感知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知系统通常在设计时固定特征共享策略，或仅适应单一上下文信号，这对无人机蜂群不适用——因为其任务、带宽、编队几何和场景覆盖在飞行中会动态变化。作者量化了上下文盲共享在UAV3D上的代价，证明需要联合考虑多个上下文维度（如字节预算与任务区域）来制定共享策略。

Method: 1) 在UAV3D上利用发布的DiscoNet检查点在评估时控制特征交换，无需重训练即量化上下文盲共享的代价；2) 提出"context plane"概念——一个有界、结构化的运行时上下文接口，每架无人机以10Hz发布至多1KB的描述符；3) 轻量级且可替换的策略模块利用蜂群上下文决定每架无人机应计算、分享和融合的内容；4) 在ROS 2与Jetson AGX Orin上实现原型验证。

Result: 任务感知共享在仅使用5-10%字节的情况下达到与全量共享相同的精度；最佳peer选择策略随字节预算变化，选错策略最高损失7.7 AP；在受限预算下，全场景精度相同的两种策略在任务区域内相差5.9 AP。原型中context plane仅占用数据平面带宽的约0.01%，每次策略决策需0.10ms。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文接口可以在不修改或重训练感知模型的前提下，以极低的开销实现运行时自适应。多种上下文轴必须联合考虑，而context plane为此提供了统一且可替换的解决方案。

Abstract: Cooperative perception allows a drone fleet to combine observations from multiple viewpoints. However, existing systems typically fix their feature-sharing policies at design time or adapt to only one context signal. This is a poor fit for aerial fleets, whose missions, bandwidth, formation geometry, and scene coverage can change during flight. We quantify the cost of context-blind sharing on UAV3D by controlling feature exchange at evaluation time using a released DiscoNet checkpoint, without retraining. Mission-aware sharing matches full-sharing accuracy while using only 5-10% of the bytes. The best tested peer selection policy changes with the byte budget, and choosing the wrong policy loses up to 7.7 AP. Moreover, under a constrained budget, two policies with the same full-scene accuracy differ by 5.9 AP within the mission region, showing that multiple context axes must be considered jointly. We therefore propose the context plane, a bounded, structured interface for runtime context. Each drone publishes a descriptor of at most 1 KB at 10 Hz, and lightweight, replaceable policies use the fleet context to decide what each drone computes, shares, and fuses. Existing sharing schemes become fixed policies within this interface. In our ROS 2 prototype on a Jetson AGX Orin, the context plane uses approximately 0.01% of the data-plane bandwidth, and each policy decision takes 0.10 ms. These results show that an explicit context interface can support low-overhead runtime adaptation without modifying or retraining the perception model.

</details>


### [19] [Behavior--Realization Separation for Constrained Physical Human--Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00669)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"行为-实现分离"的物理人机交互控制框架：行为层提供期望的接触端口加速度，实现层通过滚动时域二次规划将期望转化为满足约束的机器人指令，并显式报告期望-实现加速度误差。在7自由度Franka FR3（MuJoCo仿真）上验证，持续20N推挤下可将工作空间边界保持误差控制在约0.1-0.2mm，远超瞬时裁剪的阻抗/导纳方法的4cm级超调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理人机交互软件将期望行为规格与约束实现耦合在一起（如直接裁剪饱和），导致约束介入引起的误差被隐藏在饱和机制中，无法透明地区分行为设计缺陷与约束施加带来的性能损失。作者主张将二者分离为独立层，以便分别评估和调节行为与约束的影响。

Method: 采用双层架构：(1) 行为层用记忆无记忆仿射函数$f_\theta$产生期望接触端口加速度$a_k^{id}$；(2) 实现层用滚动时域二次规划（receding-horizon QP）将其转为受约束的机器人指令，并报告期望-实现总加速度误差。引入同目标无约束反事实来分离正则化与约束干预的影响，用被控对象数据暴露模型误差。行为修改仅改变QP目标中的$(C_\theta,G_\theta)$系数，机器人命令变量与可行集保持不变。

Result: 平面研究验证了阻抗与导纳行为可在不重建的前提下互换；MuJoCo中7自由度Franka FR3在持续20N推力下保持松弛的工作空间边界误差约0.1-0.2mm，对比阻抗4.4cm和导纳4.7cm的超调。在降额执行器预算下，全时域约束强制执行保持冻结模型计划可行（误差$2.1\times10^{-4}$N·m），而仅第一步强制时超预算最高11.329N·m；实际非线性被控对象上差距缩小但全时域仍占优（0.161 vs 0.380N·m）。

Conclusion: 行为-实现分离架构是可行的且有效的：通过将行为规格与约束实现解耦，不仅能在约束下取得远优于瞬时裁剪的边界保持精度，还能清晰地归因期望-实现误差的来源（约束干预vs行为本身），为物理人机交互控制提供了一种模块化、可重配的范式。

Abstract: Physical human--robot interaction software often couples desired-behavior specification with constrained realization; we treat these as separate layers. A \emph{behavior layer} supplies a desired contact-port acceleration $a_k^{\mathrm{id}}=f_θ(e_k,\dot e_k,F_{h,k})$. A \emph{realization layer} converts it into constrained robot commands and reports total desired-versus-realized acceleration error instead of hiding it in saturation. A same-objective unconstrained counterfactual separates regularization from constraint intervention, while plant data expose model error. This paper implements a receding-horizon quadratic program realizing memoryless affine behaviors. Changing the behavior modifies objective coefficients through $(C_θ,G_θ)$ while the robot-command variable and feasible set remain unchanged. A planar study instantiates impedance and admittance; the same running layer accepts an impedance--admittance--impedance reassignment without reconstruction, under its existing rate limit. On a torque-controlled 7-DOF Franka FR3 in MuJoCo, the runtime freezes task-space dynamics per solve and enforces torque feasibility across its horizon. Under a sustained 20~N push, it holds a slack-relaxed workspace boundary to within approximately 0.1--0.2~mm, versus 4.4~cm (impedance) and 4.7~cm (admittance) overshoot from instantaneous clipping. A derated actuator budget then activates the torque constraint: horizon-wide enforcement keeps its frozen-model plan feasible to $2.1\times10^{-4}$~N$\cdot$m, whereas a first-step-only ablation plans up to 11.329~N$\cdot$m beyond budget; on the executed nonlinear plant, where both share the same local-model error, the gap is smaller but still favors horizon-wide enforcement (0.161 vs.\ 0.380~N$\cdot$m). These results are a focused proof of behavior--realization separation.

</details>


### [20] [ADAPT: Agile Diffusion Action Priors for Robust and Steerable Online Text-Driven Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00677)
*Yan Wu,Chenhao Li,Kaifeng Zhao,Gen Li,Marco Hutter,Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是一个端到端框架，用于交互式、文本条件的人形全身控制，通过基于扩散的动作先验和强化学习的残差策略来实现语言指令驱动的持续控制、自然运动和平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到运动（text-to-motion）流水线通常生成运动学动作，然后需要单独的运动追踪器来执行，不是闭环控制。ADAPT希望解决语言控制问题，使机器人能持续响应变化的指令，同时保持平衡、自然运动和光滑过渡，即实现真正的端到端闭环语言控制。

Method: ADAPT采用以下方法框架：(1) 从文本标注的人形状态-动作轨迹中学习一个基于扩散的动作先验，使多样的运动技能可以直接由语言命令执行；(2) 在冻结的扩散控制器之上训练一个轻量级的残差强化学习策略，以提升长时程鲁棒性和平滑的指令切换能力；(3) 该扩散策略可以作为可引导的文本条件运动先验，复用于下游任务适应。

Result: 实验证明了ADAPT能实现鲁棒的语言接地技能执行、平滑的交互式过渡以及保留风格的下游控制。

Conclusion: ADAPT展示了基于扩散的动作先验结合轻量级残差强化学习可以有效地实现文本条件下的端到端人形全身闭环控制，并且该扩散策略具备作为通用运动先验的复用潜力。

Abstract: We present ADAPT, an end-to-end framework for interactive, text-conditioned humanoid whole-body control. Unlike dominant text-to-motion pipelines that generate kinematic motions for a separate tracker, ADAPT solves language control with an end-to-end closed-loop control framework, where the robot must continuously respond to changing commands while maintaining balance, natural motion, and smooth transitions. ADAPT learns a diffusion-based action prior from text-labeled humanoid state-action trajectories, enabling diverse motion skills to be directly executed from language commands. To improve long-horizon robustness and smooth prompt switching, we train a lightweight residual reinforcement learning policy on top of the frozen diffusion controller. We further show that the same diffusion policy can be reused as a steerable text-conditioned motion prior for downstream task adaptation. Experiments demonstrate robust language-grounded skill execution, smooth interactive transitions, and style-preserving downstream control.

</details>


### [21] [Context-Aware Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00682)
*Liangkai Liu,Shuyao Shi,Mingke Wang,Noah T. Curran,Chuan Li,Fan Bai,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张将上下文情境因素视为下一代智能车辆系统的"一等公民"，统一为软件栈中用于学习、风险评估和闭环控制的共享状态。作者系统综述了环境理解、规划与控制、安全与安全、网联车辆四类SOTA情境感知方法，并通过趋势分析提炼出构建通用情境引擎的四大技术挑战：多模态情境融合、时序情境建模、罕见事件处理与协作式情境共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车辆在资源受限平台上运行于复杂、不确定且快速变化的环境中，同时要满足精度、延迟、成本和可靠性的严苛要求。现有系统缺乏将情境因素作为统一共享状态来处理的设计原则，导致跨层协作和学习效率低下，难以支撑下一代上下文感知应用。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，对情境感知方法按四大类别进行梳理（环境理解、规划与控制、安全与安全、网联车辆），并对情境感知设计模式进行趋势分析，从而归纳出面向未来车辆通用情境引擎的关键技术挑战。

Result: 识别出通用情境引擎面临的四大核心技术挑战：多模态情境融合（整合视觉、雷达、LiDAR等多种传感模态）、时序情境建模（捕捉动态演变的状态信息）、罕见事件处理（对低概率但高风险场景的鲁棒性）、以及协作式情境共享（车车/车路协同中的上下文交换）。

Conclusion: 将情境作为一等公民的统一共享状态，是构建高效、鲁棒的下一代智能车辆系统的关键方向；本文的综述与挑战分析旨在激励更通用的情境感知引擎研究与开发。

Abstract: Intelligent vehicles increasingly support adaptive applications beyond driving themselves, ranging from context-aware ADAS and automated driving to in-cabin monitoring and fleet management, all under tight requirements on accuracy, latency, cost, and reliability. Meeting these requirements is challenging because vehicles operate in complex, uncertain, and rapidly changing environments while running on resource-constrained computing platforms. This paper argues that context-situational factors that give meaning to sensor signals and constrain decisions-should be treated as a first-class principle for next-generation vehicle systems, and operationalized as a unified, shared state for learning, risk assessment, and closed-loop control across the software stack. We systematically review state-of-the- art (SOTA) context-aware methods spanning (i) environment understanding, (ii) planning and control, (iii) safety and security, and (iv) connected vehicles. Based on a trend analysis of context-aware design, we identify four key technical challenges in building a general contextual engine for future intelligent vehicles: multi-modal context fusion, temporal context modeling, handling rare events, and collaborative context sharing. We hope this survey will motivate the development of robust and efficient context-aware vehicle applications.

</details>


### [22] [One Print, Many Moves: Monolithic Origami-inspired Folding Actuator for Composable Soft Multi-DoF Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00751)
*Jaehyung Jang,Zhenish Zhakypov,Jasmin Elena Palmer,Melissa Klein,Jee-Hwan Ryu,Allison Mariko Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MONORIGAMI的单体折纸启发式软体折叠执行器设计策略，通过基于材料厚度的空间编程刚度各向异性，在保持材料柔顺性的同时限制非期望方向的变形，实现精确、可重复且可组合的折叠运动。该执行器可通过单材料单次3D打印制造，无需组装，每个执行器作为可扩展的运动基元，通过链接和定向多个执行器可编程多自由度轨迹。作者展示了三个应用系统：4自由度可穿戴触觉设备、3自由度遥操作操纵杆、以及可水下作业的模块化机器人抓爪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软体机器人执行器虽然具有柔顺性优势，但其不可控的变形导致精度不足，且难以扩展到多自由度系统，这成为软体机器人领域长期存在的关键瓶颈。

Method: MONORIGAMI设计通过折纸启发的几何结构（具有刻面和折痕）中将不同厚度的材料分层排列，构建空间编程的刚度各向异性——沿期望折叠方向保持柔顺性，同时限制非期望方向的变形。该设计采用单材料、单次3D打印工艺，无需额外加固或组装。每个执行器是独立的运动基元，通过机械连接和定向多个基元编程多自由度轨迹。

Result: 作者基于同一基础模块制造了三个覆盖不同应用领域的3D打印软体多自由度机器人系统：(1) 用于VR高保真皮肤反馈的4自由度紧凑可穿戴触觉设备，(2) 用于遥操作动觉反馈的3自由度操纵杆，(3) 可水下作业且具有几何编码抓取轨迹的模块化机器人抓爪。这些系统展示了在多轴集成、受控物理交互和跨环境几何编程操作方面的能力。

Conclusion: MONORIGAMI为高精度软体多自由度机器人提供了一个通用、可组合、易获取、可靠且可扩展的平台，解决了软体执行器在设计和制造方面的长期局限，为软体机器人从单一柔顺运动走向精确多自由度控制铺平了道路。

Abstract: Conventional soft robot actuators excel in compliance, but their uncontrolled deformations compromise accuracy and hinder scaling to multi-degree-of-freedom (DoF) systems. We introduce a MONOlithic ORIGAMI-inspired soft folding actuator design (MONORIGAMI) that establishes a design strategy based on spatially programmed stiffness anisotropy to preserve material compliance along desired folding directions while selectively restricting deformation in unwanted directions. The actuator leverages stiffness tiers based on material thickness, patterned in an origami-inspired geometry with facets and creases, converting unconstrained soft deformation into accurate, repeatable, and composable folding motions without additional reinforcements. The design is fully 3D-printable through a single-material, single-print process that requires no assembly. Each actuator serves as a scalable motion primitive, and linking and orienting multiple actuators mechanically programs multi-DoF trajectories. Using the same fundamental module, we demonstrate three 3D-printed soft multi-DoF robotic systems spanning distinct application domains: (1) a compact 4-DoF wearable haptic device for high-fidelity cutaneous feedback in virtual reality (VR), (2) a 3-DoF joystick for kinesthetic feedback in teleoperation, and (3) a modular robotic gripper capable of underwater operation with geometry-encoded grasp trajectories. These systems demonstrate the module's capabilities for compact multi-axis integration, controlled physical interaction, and geometry-programmed operation across different environments. Together, these results show that MONORIGAMI provides a general, composable, accessible, reliable, and scalable platform for high-precision soft multi-DoF robotics, addressing long-standing limitations in both soft actuator design and fabrication.

</details>


### [23] [A Compact Robotic Finger with 2-DoF MCP Joint Embedding DoF-Selective Passive Continuously Variable Transmission for Wide Force-Speed Operating Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00769)
*JaeHyung Jang,Jee-Hwan Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有屈曲选择性无源无级变速器（CVT）的紧凑型双自由度机器人手指，灵感来源于人类掌指关节的功能分化。该机构通过将无源CVT选择性分配给屈曲-伸展自由度，同时保留外展-内收自由度的直接传动，实现了力-速度工作范围的显著扩展。实验表明最大输出力放大4.19倍，平均放大3.6倍，且保持了外展-内收功能的灵巧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型多自由度机器人手需要在有限的尺寸内实现宽泛的力-速度工作范围，但传统设计难以兼顾大力输出与高速运动。人类掌指关节通过功能分化在单关节内实现不同自由度间的差异化传动特性，这一生物学启示尚未在机械设计中得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种双自由度机器人手指，将力响应型无源CVT嵌入屈曲-伸展传动路径中，而对外展-内收路径保留直接传动。引入基于移动滑轮的线缆布线结构的输出侧无源CVT，利用线张力作用于滑轮产生的反作用力，根据负载被动增加屈曲力臂和传动比，无需额外的执行器、传感器或控制系统。

Result: 实验验证在15°至75°屈曲角度范围内，通过力臂自适应实现了最大4.19倍的输出力放大和平均3.6倍的放大效果，显著扩展了力-速度工作范围。球体操控实验证明在无源传动自适应过程中仍能保持外展-内收功能。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种可扩展的多自由度紧凑型机器人手传动设计策略，利用自由度特定的无源CVT分配实现了功能分化，为多自由度机械手的宽范围力-速度控制和传动系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: This letter presents a compact two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic finger with a flexion-selective passive continuously variable transmission (CVT) to achieve a wide force-speed operating range. Inspired by the functional differentiation of the human metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint, the proposed mechanism realizes DoF-specific transmission differentiation by selectively assigning passive CVT to the flexion-extension DoF while preserving direct transmission for abduction-adduction. For a wide force-speed operating range, a force-responsive passive CVT is embedded in the flexion pathway, while direct transmission is preserved for the abduction-adduction pathway. To selectively realize transmission adaptation within a multi-DoF MCP mechanism, an output-side passive CVT employing a moving-pulley-inspired wire-routing structure is introduced. The resultant force generated by the wire tensions acting on the pulley that passively increases the flexion moment arm and transmission ratio according to the applied load without additional actuators, sensors, or control. Experimental results demonstrate a maximum output-force amplification of 4.19-fold and a mean amplification of 3.6-fold across the tested flexion angles ranging from 15 degrees to 75 degrees through moment-arm adaptation, thereby substantially expanding the achievable force-speed operating range. Furthermore, dexterous ball-rolling experiments verify that passive transmission adaptation can be achieved while preserving abduction-adduction functionality. These results demonstrate a scalable transmission design strategy for compact multi-DoF robotic hands.

</details>

*Abdullah Mustafa,Ryo Hanai,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Floris Erich,Ryoichi Nakajo,Yukiyasu Domae,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的非抓取式机器人抛掷方法，利用滑动和滚动接触模式，直接优化关节空间轨迹，无需解析接触模型。在UR5e上实现零样本部署，仿真成功率99%，真实世界成功率97%，可抛掷790g重物和20×20×28cm大物体至350cm距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非抓取式抛掷方法依赖基于模型的优化，采用简化的接触模型（如动态抓取）和低维轨迹参数化，限制了解决方案质量和可达工作空间。需要一种无需解析接触模型和自定义参数化的方法，以充分利用滑动和滚动接触模式，提升抛掷大/重/变形物体的能力。

Method: 采用强化学习方法：1) 将MDP建模为以抛掷目标、物体模型和初始构型为条件的动力学系统；2) 离线低控制率规划关节加加速度轨迹，上采样为平滑高率速度指令；3) 通过最小加加速度系统辨识缩小sim-to-real中机器人动力学差距；4) 训练不确定感知策略以缓解物体建模误差，尤其对动态摩擦的敏感性。

Result: 仿真中在数千种配置下实现99%成功率并泛化到未见物体；敏感性分析显示对质量不确定性鲁棒但对动态摩擦高度敏感。在UR5e上零样本部署（末端速度5 m/s），可抛掷重型（790g）和大型（20×20×28cm）物体至350cm距离或180cm高度，真实世界成功率97%。

Conclusion: 所提RL方法通过利用滑动/滚动接触模式和直接优化关节空间轨迹，显著扩展了非抓取式抛掷的可达工作空间与物体能力范围，验证了强化学习在该任务中优于传统模型基优化方法的潜力。

Abstract: Robotic throwing enables fast object transport and extends a robot's reachable workspace beyond traditional pick-and-place. While prehensile (grasp-based) throwing works well for graspable items, non-prehensile (grasp-free) throwing is better suited for large, heavy, and/or deformable objects. Existing approaches rely on model-based optimization with simplified contact models (e.g., dynamic grasping) and low-dimensional trajectory parameterizations, which limit solution quality and reachable workspace. We propose a reinforcement learning approach that additionally leverages sliding and rolling contact modes and directly optimizes joint-space trajectories without analytical contact models or custom parameterizations. The Markov Decision Process (MDP) is formulated as a dynamical system that evolves the robot's joint state conditioned on the throwing target, object model, and initial configuration. Joint-jerk trajectories are planned offline at a low control rate and upsampled into smooth, high-rate velocity commands for deployment. For sim-to-real transfer, we minimize the robot-dynamics gap through minimum-jerk system identification and train uncertainty-aware policies to mitigate object-modeling errors, particularly sensitivity to dynamic friction. In simulation, the policy achieves 99% success across thousands of configurations and generalizes to unseen objects. Sensitivity analysis shows robustness to mass uncertainty but high sensitivity to dynamic friction, consistent with the sliding-based release mechanism. Deployed zero-shot on a UR5e operating near its physical limits (5 m/s end-effector velocity), our method throws diverse objects including heavy (790 g) and large (20x20x28 cm) items to targets up to 350 cm distance or 180 cm elevation, achieving a 97% real-world success rate.

</details>

*Béatrice Garcia Cegarra,Elena Vanneaux,Quentin Picard,David Filliat*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机探索未知环境时面临间歇性通信的挑战，分散式操作是基本前提。现有方法在断连和空间布局变化时难以保持区域划分的稳定性和鲁棒性，需要一种新的图扩展策略来提升分散式探索的效率和稳健性。

Method: 提出一种基于前沿连通性的探索图扩展策略（frontier connectivity-based exploration graph extension），在分散式框架下扩展探索计划，将图扩展方法应用于两种最先进的区域划分方法，通过仿真评估其在低通信率下的性能。

Result: 仿真实验表明，与现有图扩展方法相比，所提方法在低通信率下具有更高的探索效率，展现出改进的性能。

Conclusion: 基于前沿连通性的图扩展策略能够有效提升多无人机在间歇通信环境下的探索效率，增强区域划分面对断连和布局变化的鲁棒性，是一种适用于分散式多无人机探索的有效方案。

Abstract: Exploring unknown environments with multiple UAVs requires coordination under intermittent communication, making decentralized operation a baseline assumption. We propose, within a decentralized framework, a novel exploration graph extension strategy based on frontier connectivity to extend exploration plans and maintain area partitioning among agents stable and robust to disconnections and changes in spatial layout. The proposed extension method is applied to two state-of-the-art area partitioning methods and evaluated in simulation. Experiments show improved performance over existing graph extension approaches with higher exploration efficiency under low communication rate.

</details>


### [26] [Peg-in-Bench: A Modular Benchmark for High-Precision Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00906)
*Yosel Delgado,José G. Buenaventura-Carreón,Floris Erich,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可重构的插销-孔（peg-in-hole）基准测试平台，用于评估高精度插入操作中的任务泛化能力。该基准由全3D可打印的模块化组件构成，包含多种销钉几何形状、公差等级和可配置基座结构，能够组合生成大量不同的插入与装配任务，并提供场景生成工具以保证可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度插入是机器人操作中的基础难题，需要严格的对齐要求和处理接触丰富交互。现有peg-in-hole基准测试多依赖固定任务配置，难以评估机器人在不同插入场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，因此需要一个可重配置、可系统评估泛化性能的基准平台。

Method: 该基准包含一整套完全3D可打印的模块化组件，包括多种销钉（peg）几何形状、多个公差等级以及可配置的基座结构，这些组件可自由组合以生成大量多样化的插入和装配任务。通过改变物体布局、朝向和任务结构，同时保持受控的物理条件，实现对新场景适应能力的系统评估。此外，还提供一个场景生成工具，可生成标准化的任务配置和机器可读的任务描述以支持可复现性。

Result: 该基准通过模块化组件的组合能够生成大量不同类型的插入和装配任务，支持在不同物体布局、朝向和任务结构下的系统评估。提供的场景生成工具可生成标准化任务配置和机器可读任务描述，确保实验的可复现性。所有资源（STL文件和工具）已通过项目仓库公开提供。

Conclusion: 该工作为高精度机器人插入任务提供了一个可重配置、可复现且支持系统评估任务泛化能力的基准平台，弥补了现有固定配置基准的不足，有助于推动机器人插入操作泛化能力的研究。

Abstract: High-precision insertion remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation due to the strict alignment requirements and contact-rich interactions involved. Although peg-in-hole tasks are widely used for evaluation, existing bench- marks often rely on fixed task configurations, limiting their ability to assess robustness and generalization across different insertion scenarios. This paper introduces a reconfigurable peg-in-hole benchmark designed to evaluate task generalization in high-precision insertion. The benchmark consists of a set of fully 3D-printable modular components, including multiple peg geometries, tolerance levels, and configurable base structures that can be combined to generate a large variety of insertion and assembly tasks. By varying object layouts, orientations, and task structures while maintaining controlled physical conditions, the benchmark enables systematic evaluation of adaptation to unseen scenarios. To support reproducibility, we additionally provide a scenario generation tool capable of producing standardized task configurations and machine-readable task descriptions. The scenario generation tool and the STL files of the benchmark pieces are available through the project repository: https://github.com/aistairc/peg-in-bench.

</details>


### [27] [Knowing When to Stop: Adaptive Action Chunking via Internal Cross-Attention Dynamics in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00908)
*Runze Xu,Xiaolong Shan,Shuang Dai,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动作专家内部交叉注意力动态的自适应动作分块（adaptive action chunking）方法，通过检测预测过程中交叉注意力熵升高并趋于平台的信号，动态选择执行水平，无需重新训练即可在推理时自适应调整分块长度，在多个基准和真实机器人任务上优于固定分块策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA框架中的固定动作分块（chunking）在执行效率与准确性之间存在权衡：短分块需要频繁推理且可能导致振荡行为，长分块则可能与新观测状态失配。作者希望通过利用策略内部已有的注意力信号实现自适应分块长度选择，避免这一固有矛盾。

Method: 作者观察发现，随着预测水平延伸，动作专家中动作到观测的交叉注意力趋于分散，熵上升并趋于平台化，这一模式与更高的动作预测误差相关联。基于此，提出一种无需训练（training-free）的截断机制，在推理期间检测持续的高熵平台并动态确定执行水平，仅利用策略已有的注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。

Result: 在π_{0.5}和X-VLA上，于RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及三项真实世界操作任务中评估，结果表明该方法相较于固定分块和自适应分块基线在平均任务成功率上均有提升，同时保持了高效的闭环控制。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力动态可以为VLA提供一种实用的内部信号，用于自适应动作执行策略，从而在不牺牲闭环效率的前提下提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Action chunking is a standard execution strategy in modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks, but fixed execution horizons impose a trade-off between efficiency and accuracy. Short chunks require frequent inference and may cause oscillatory behavior, whereas long chunks can become misaligned with newly observed states. We address this limitation with an adaptive action chunking approach based on internal cross-attention dynamics in the action expert. We observe that, as the prediction horizon extends, action-to-observation cross-attention becomes increasingly dispersed and its entropy rises toward a plateau. This pattern is associated with higher action prediction error and provides an online signal that the current observation offers limited grounding for further open-loop execution. Based on this observation, we introduce a training-free truncation mechanism that detects sustained high-entropy plateaus and dynamically selects the execution horizon during inference. The method uses attention weights already computed by the policy and introduces negligible additional overhead. Evaluations on $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and three real-world manipulation tasks show improved average task success over fixed-horizon and adaptive chunking baselines, while preserving efficient closed-loop control. These results show that cross-attention dynamics can provide a practical internal signal for adaptive action execution in VLAs.

</details>


### [28] [VerNav: Verifier-First Low-Latency Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00920)
*Zhixin Wang,Chengzheyi Yao,Leyuan Liu,Xiaosong Zhang,Yongzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VerNav提出了一种验证器优先（verifier-first）的框架，用于低延迟的基于LLM的视觉语言导航（VLN）。它通过批量动作验证替代逐步骤的自回归生成来大幅降低决策阶段延迟，并结合熵自适应的生成器仅在不确定决策时生成紧凑状态证据。通过两阶段对齐方案（VPO静态训练对齐和步骤级强化微调）提升导航性能，在R2R基准上实现了比自回归方法快10倍以上的决策延迟，且导航性能具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN任务需要智能体根据自然语言指令在未知3D环境中导航。虽然显式推理可以增强指令理解和语义接地，但每一步的自回归生成会在多步导航过程中累积大量决策阶段延迟，影响实际应用中的实时性。现有方法缺乏在保持推理能力的同时显著降低延迟的有效方案。

Method: 提出VerNav框架，核心包括：（1）验证器优先的决策路径，用批量动作验证替代逐步骤自回归生成；（2）基于熵的自适应生成器，仅在验证器不确定时触发，生成紧凑的状态证据辅助决策；（3）两阶段对齐方案：第一阶段通过VPO（验证器偏好优化）在静态训练中实现局部动作偏好对齐；第二阶段通过步骤级强化微调在多步导航rollout中提供密集进度奖励，实现动态任务执行时的优化。

Result: 在Room-to-Room (R2R)基准上的实验表明，VerNav的仅验证器决策路径在代表性LLM-based VLN智能体中取得了有竞争力的导航性能，同时将每步的平均决策阶段LLM延迟比自回归方法降低了10倍以上。

Conclusion: VerNav验证了"验证优于生成"的设计范式对于降低LLM-based VLN决策延迟的有效性，即在保证导航性能的同时大幅提升响应速度，为实时导航场景中LLM的应用提供了可行的低延迟解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate through unseen 3D environments according to natural-language instructions. Explicit reasoning can improve instruction understanding and semantic grounding, but autoregressive generation at every step accumulates large decision-stage latency over multi-step navigation. We propose VerNav, a verifier-first framework for low-latency LLM-based VLN. The verifier reduces decision-stage latency by replacing per-step autoregressive generation with batched action verification, while an entropy-based adaptive generator is invoked only for uncertain decisions to produce compact state evidence. To further improve navigation performance with the verifier, we introduce a two-stage alignment scheme: (i) VPO improves local action-preference alignment in static verifier training, and (ii) step-level reinforcement fine-tuning provides dense progress rewards over multi-step navigation rollouts during dynamic task execution. Experiments on the Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmark show that the verifier-only decision path of VerNav achieves competitive navigation performance among representative LLM-based VLN agents while reducing average decision-stage LLM latency per step by more than $10\times$ compared with autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [29] [ProxPI: Proximal Prior Injection for Sampling-Based MPC under Learned-Prior Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00941)
*Euncheol Im,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ProxPI（近端先验注入）方法，解决策略引导的MPPI控制中，当策略处于分布外时，以策略输出为中心的采样分布会限制探索并阻止优化器恢复任务最优点的问题。ProxPI保留以标称（nominal）为中心的MPPI采样，通过软近端代价将策略先验纳入优化，既保持分布内性能又能在先验不匹配时恢复到vanilla MPPI水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将学习到的策略与模型预测控制结合可兼具学习先验与在线自适应能力，但当策略处于分布外时性能会严重退化。现有策略引导MPPI方法将采样分布中心化于策略输出，若先验不匹配，这会限制在次优解周围的探索，无法恢复到任务最优解。

Method: ProxPI采用名义（nominal）为中心的MPPI采样，不对采样分布重新中心化到策略输出，而是通过一个软近端（proximity）代价项将学习策略的输出纳入优化目标。该方法理论上分析了两种机制的差异：重新中心化会丢弃优化器每次更新时的修正量，而名义中心化采样可保留修正并收敛到任务代价与策略先验共同定义的解集。

Result: 理论上证明重新中心化到策略先验会丢弃优化器在每次更新中的修正并无法通过扩大rollout预算来修复，而名义中心化采样能保留修正并收敛。仿真与真实机器人实验表明，ProxPI在分布内任务上与现有先验注入方案性能相当，在分布外任务上能摆脱不准确策略并恢复到vanilla MPPI级别的性能。

Conclusion: ProxPI通过"名义中心化采样+软先验代价"的方式，兼顾了策略引导MPPI在分布内的高效性和分布外场景下的鲁棒性，为策略先验与模型预测控制的融合提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: Combining learned policies with model predictive control can leverage learned task priors while retaining online adaptation to new objectives and constraints, but performance degrades when the policy is out of distribution. In policy-guided model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a policy-centered warm-start approach centers the sampling distribution on the policy output. When the prior is mismatched, centering the sampling distribution on the policy output restricts exploration around an unsuitable solution and prevents recovery toward the task optimum. We propose Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI), which retains nominal-centered MPPI sampling and incorporates the policy through a soft proximity cost. This matches the in-distribution performance of existing prior-injection schemes while enabling the optimizer to escape an inaccurate policy and recover vanilla MPPI-level performance. We theoretically show that re-centering on the prior discards the optimizer's correction at every update, whereas nominal-centered sampling retains it and converges to a solution set by both the task cost and the prior, and that this failure is not removed by a larger rollout budget. Simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate robust performance under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [30] [HitMem: Hierarchical Temporal 3D Memory with Multi-Modal Context-Aware Retrieval for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00950)
*Ruijie Tang,Chenye Zou,Guoquan Wu,Jun Wei,Wei Chen,Jiaxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HitMem是一个层次化时序3D记忆框架，通过轻量级拓扑图统一语义与空间信息，并采用时间衰减机制和多模态上下文感知检索，解决动态环境中物体位移导致的记忆-观测冲突问题，在Dyna-THOR基准上显著提升物体重定位准确率、降低探索成本并改善任务执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D记忆框架大多基于静态世界假设，当物体被人类活动或未观测事件移动时，智能体遭遇记忆-观测冲突，需要进行昂贵的几何重计算或低效的全局重新探索，缺乏对动态场景的适应能力。

Method: HitMem框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 通过连续感知将语义与空间信息统一为捕获支撑关系的轻量级拓扑图；(2) 时间衰减机制动态调节记忆活跃度以减轻陈旧表示的影响；(3) 多模态上下文感知检索机制，默认利用语义、空间和时间特征过滤候选，在检测到物体位移时激活两阶段检索过程——结合外部智能体轨迹推导的空间约束与基于类别亲和力的语义常识，高效定位高概率候选区域。

Result: 在自建的Dyna-THOR基准上，HitMem显著提升了物体重定位的准确率，降低了探索成本，并增强了动态环境中的任务执行性能。

Conclusion: HitMem证明层次化时序记忆与上下文感知检索的结合能够使具身智能体有效应对动态环境中的物体位移，避免低效的全局重探索，为长期任务场景中的3D场景记忆提供了可行的动态化方案。

Abstract: Executing long-term tasks in dynamic environments requires embodied agents to maintain robust and adaptive 3D scene representations. However, most existing 3D memory frameworks rely on static world assumptions. When objects are displaced by human activities or unobserved events, agents encounter memory-observation conflicts and often require costly geometric recomputations or inefficient global re-exploration. To address this, we propose HitMem, a hierarchical temporal 3D memory framework with a multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism. Through continuous perception, HitMem unifies semantic and spatial information into a lightweight topological graph that captures support relationships, while a temporal decay mechanism dynamically regulates memory activeness to mitigate the impact of stale representations. In addition, the multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism defaults to filtering candidates using integrated semantic, spatial, and temporal memory features, and activates a specialized two-stage retrieval process when object displacement is detected. This process combines spatial constraints inferred from external agent trajectories with semantic common sense grounded in class affinities, efficiently identifying high-probability candidate regions. Extensive evaluations on our constructed Dyna-THOR benchmark demonstrate that HitMem significantly improves object relocation accuracy, reduces exploration costs, and enhances task execution performance in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [31] [Accelerating Reinforcement Learning via MPC Solver-Gradient Guidance for Weights-varying MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01061)
*Baha Zarrouki,Arslan Thobani,Jasper Hoffmann,Mattia Piccinini,Rudolf Reiter,Felix Jahncke,Sébastien Gros,Davide Scaramuzza,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-RL（Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning），一种为RL驱动的MPC代价权重在线自适应提供求解器灵敏度引导的框架，在保持采样闭环回报作为目标的同时，利用有界的可微MPC求解器梯度作为辅助指导，提升稳定性和样本效率。在两个具有故意模型失配的全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台上，SG-RL以最高70.6%更少的样本达到PPO的最佳闭环回报，相较GB-PL基线至少提升54%的闭环回报，并能零样本泛化到未见环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC中代价权重影响闭环行为，但固定参数化在变化条件下往往次优，需要上下文相关的在线自适应。然而学习这类策略很困难：行为隐式依赖数值MPC求解结果，产生对策略参数的非线性、潜在非光滑、长时域依赖。这导致偏差-方差权衡：RL从环境采样优化真实闭环回报但样本效率低，而GB-PL利用可微MPC的低方差求解器梯度优化预测轨迹上的替代损失但存在模型失配偏差。

Method: 提出SG-RL框架，其核心思想是：保持采样闭环回报为优化目标，同时使用有界（bounded）的求解器推导梯度作为辅助引导，以提升训练稳定性和样本效率。SG-RL在PPO中实例化了四个模块化算法，分别将求解器梯度引导注入：(1) actor更新缩放，(2) 策略损失，(3) 优势估计，(4) 值函数学习。

Result: 在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台上（带有故意引入的模型失配）：(1) SG-RL以最多70.6%更少的样本达到PPO的最佳闭环回报；(2) 相较GB-PL基线在闭环回报上至少提升54%；(3) 可零样本泛化至未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 求解器梯度引导可以有效缓解RL在MPC代价权重自适应学习中的样本效率问题，同时避免GB-PL在模型失配下的偏差问题。SG-RL通过将低方差的求解器信息注入RL训练的各模块，在保持闭环目标准确性的同时显著提升学习效率和泛化能力，为MPC参数在线自适应提供了一种兼顾偏差与方差的新范式。

Abstract: In Model Predictive Control (MPC), cost-function weights shape closed-loop behavior, yet changing conditions often make fixed parametrizations suboptimal and motivate context-dependent online adaptation. Learning such policies is difficult because behavior depends implicitly on numerical MPC solutions, producing nonlinear, potentially nonsmooth, long-horizon dependencies on policy parameters. This creates a bias-variance tradeoff: Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimizes realized closed-loop return from environment samples but is sample-inefficient, whereas Gradient-Based Policy Learning (GB-PL) uses low-variance solver gradients from differentiable MPC to optimize surrogate losses on predicted trajectories but can be biased under model mismatch. We propose Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning (SG-RL), a solver-sensitivity augmentation for RL-based online MPC cost-weight adaptation. SG-RL keeps sampled closed-loop return as the objective and uses bounded solver-derived gradients as auxiliary guidance to improve stability and sample efficiency. We instantiate SG-RL in Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with four modular algorithms that inject solver-gradient guidance into actor-update scaling, policy loss, advantage estimation, and value-function learning. On two full-scale autonomous racing platforms with intentional model mismatch, SG-RL reaches PPO's best closed-loop return with up to 70.6% fewer samples, outperforms GB-PL baselines by at least 54% in closed-loop return, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen environments.

</details>


### [32] [Adaptive Depth-Map-Guided Bundle Adjustment for Correspondence-Free Multi-View Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01089)
*Yiran Zhou,Yingyu Wang,Shoudong Huang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为自适应分层深度图引导的捆绑调整（ADM-BA）框架，用于无对应关系的多视图点云配准。通过全局2.5-D网格中自适应维护多个深度假设，将原始深度观测直接投影到全局地图形成深度约束，并利用softmax层分配处理多表面冲突，联合优化传感器位姿与分层深度图，在工业钢板废料切割场景中实现鲁棒且计算高效的精确三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险废钢切割工作单元中，机器人处理不规则钢板废料需要密集3D测量来替代人工目视评估。现有基于特征提取和数据关联的多视图配准方法在光滑金属表面、重复结构、遮挡及部分重叠场景下容易产生错误对应关系，导致位姿估计不准确和重建畸变，影响下游尺寸估计、切割区域规划及碰撞感知路径的准确性。

Method: 提出自适应分层深度图引导的捆绑调整框架：(1)将场景表示为全局2.5-D网格，每个单元自适应维护多个深度假设；(2)原始深度观测直接投影至全局地图形成深度约束，无需显式特征对应；(3)对多表面深度冲突的网格单元，采用softmax层分配将观测匹配至兼容深度假设；(4)通过非线性最小二乘公式联合优化传感器位姿与分层深度图，对应关系由深度图表示和投影模型隐式诱导。

Result: 在自采集工业数据集上的实验表明，该方法在具有挑战性的工业场景中实现了一致的竞争力重建精度，同时保持了鲁棒性和低计算成本。开源源代码已发布于GitHub。

Conclusion: 通过深度图表示和投影模型隐式构建对应关系，有效避免了传统特征匹配在光滑金属及重复结构场景下的误匹配问题，为工业废钢切割机器人提供了鲁棒、高效且精度可靠的三维重建方案。

Abstract: Robotic processing of irregular steel scrap requires dense 3-D measurement to replace manual visual assessment in hazardous cutting workcells. The reconstructed map is used to estimate piece dimensions, boundary geometry, feasible preheating and cutting regions, and collision-aware torch paths. The reconstruction errors therefore propagate directly to downstream measurement and planning. Existing multi-view registration methods commonly rely on feature extraction and data association to establish correspondences between views. In workcells with smooth metallic surfaces, repeated structures, occlusions, and partial overlaps, however, wrong correspondences may be established, leading to inaccurate pose estimation and distorted reconstruction. This paper presents an adaptive layered depth-map-guided bundle adjustment framework for correspondence-free multi-view point cloud registration. The scene is represented by a global 2.5-D grid, where each cell can adaptively maintain multiple depth hypotheses. Raw depth observations are directly projected into the global map to form depth constraints without explicit feature correspondences. At grid cells where multiple surfaces produce conflicting depths, a softmax-based layer assignment links each observation to compatible depth hypotheses. The resulting nonlinear least-squares formulation jointly refines sensor poses and the layered depth map, with correspondences implicitly induced by the depth-map representation and projection model. Experiments on self-collected industrial datasets show that the proposed method achieves consistently competitive reconstruction accuracy while maintaining robustness and low computational cost in challenging industrial scenarios. We release the open-source code implementation at: https://github.com/YiranZhou-Robotics/ADM-BA.git

</details>


### [33] [DNC-IMM: Early Lane-Change Intention Recognition via Neural Calibration Based on Driving Context Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01120)
*Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双重神经校准交互多模型（DNC-IMM）方法，通过神经网络同时校准转移概率矩阵和测量似然来编码驾驶情境信息，从而提高车辆换道意图早期识别的准确性，在highD数据集上验证了其在交叉路口前2-3秒预测时段的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换道意图的早期识别对自动驾驶和高级驾驶辅助系统的前瞻性决策至关重要，而传统交互多模型（IMM）方法虽然具有清晰的概率结构和可解释性，但对驾驶情境的适应性有限，难以在不同交通场景下准确、及时地识别换道意图。

Method: 提出DNC-IMM框架，将目标车辆运动、与周围车辆的间距、相对速度等驾驶情境信息编码为神经网络输入，对传统IMM的转移概率矩阵和测量似然进行双重校准；最终换道意图由校准后的IMM模式后验概率决定，而非通过独立的直接分类器输出。

Result: 在highD数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够在车道交叉之前可靠地识别换道意图，并在更早的2-3秒预测时域内表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: DNC-IMM通过神经网络校准机制提升了IMM对驾驶情境的自适应性，同时保留了原方法概率结构的可解释性，为早期换道意图识别提供了一种兼顾性能与透明度的有效方案。

Abstract: Early recognition of lane-change intention is essential for proactive decision-making in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. This paper proposes a Dual Neural-Calibrated Interacting Multiple Model (DNC-IMM) that improves adaptability to driving context while preserving the probabilistic structure and interpretability of a conventional IMM. The proposed method encodes driving-context information, including target-vehicle motion, gaps to surrounding vehicles, and relative velocities, with a neural network that calibrates both the transition-probability matrix and measurement likelihoods. The final intention is determined from the calibrated IMM mode posterior rather than from a separate direct classifier. Experiments on the highD dataset demonstrate that the proposed method reliably recognizes lane-change intentions before lane crossing and provides particularly strong performance at the earlier 2-3 s prediction horizons.

</details>


### [34] [On Global Regulatability of Robot Manipulators by Classical PID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01207)
*Cheng Zhao,Jingru Zhu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究一类不确定MIMO非线性系统，提出了逐通道扩展PID（EPID）控制方法。针对具有变通道相对度的系统，每个通道的控制输入由对应跟踪误差的比例、积分和微分项构成，最高微分阶数依据该通道的相对度选取。在适当的增长条件下，构建了由初始状态界、不确定性和参考信号规定的EPID参数容许集，任何该集合内的参数选择都能保证闭环系统的半全局稳定性和设定点调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PID型控制文献对MIMO系统中各通道相对度可能不同的情况关注不足。作者希望填补这一空白，并理论证明工程实践中常见的逐通道独立调参做法在强耦合不确定MIMO系统（如飞行控制系统）中的合理性。

Method: 提出逐通道EPID控制器。每个通道的控制输入由对应跟踪误差的比例、积分和微分项构造，最高微分阶数依据该通道的相对度选取。在未知非线性满足适当增长条件的假设下，构建由预设初始状态界、不确定性和参考信号共同规定的EPID参数容许集，并证明任意该集合内的参数选择都能保证闭环系统的半全局稳定性与设定点调节。

Result: 证明了从所构建参数容许集中任意选取EPID参数，都能保证闭环系统的半全局稳定性并实现期望的设定点调节。该结果表明EPID参数可以逐通道独立设计，验证了在强耦合且不确定的MIMO系统中逐通道调试这一常见工程实践的正当性。

Conclusion: 本文的EPID控制方法有效解决了具有通道间相对度差异的不确定MIMO非线性系统的控制问题，从理论上支持了通道级独立参数设计的工程实践，对包括飞行控制系统在内的强耦合不确定MIMO系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of uncertain multi-input multi-output (MIMO) nonlinear systems using extended PID (EPID) control. We focus on systems possessing a well-defined vector relative degree whose components may vary across channels, a setting that received limited attention in the existing literature on PID-type control. We develop a channel-wise EPID controller, where each control input is constructed from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of the corresponding tracking error, and the highest derivative order is selected according to the relative degree of that channel. Under suitable growth conditions on the unknown nonlinearities, we construct an admissible set of EPID parameters specified by prescribed initial-state bounds, the uncertainties and the reference signal. We show that any parameter choice from this set guarantees semiglobal stability of the closed-loop system and achieves the desired setpoint regulation. These results indicate that the EPID parameters can be designed independently for each channel, justifying the common engineering practice of channel-by-channel tuning in strongly coupled and uncertain MIMO systems, including flight control systems.

</details>


### [35] [EmbodiedSkills: A Unified Framework for Orchestrating, Training, and Deploying VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01281)
*Wei Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Yutong Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Tianwei Lin,Jinhao Mao,Zhenxuan Fan,Mingjian Gao,Yang Dai,Wentong Li,Zheqi Lv,Zheng Dong,Yingjie Niu,Jiaqi Zhu,Jun Xiao,Chao Li,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EmbodiedSkills框架，将VLA模型的每个技能决策视为一次"执行提案"，在真实环境中执行前检查前提条件、执行后验证结果，通过共享的可执行技能接口连接高层技能选择、低层VLA执行和动作后验证，形成闭环具身智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型能直接映射视觉和语言指令到机器人动作，但长程任务仅靠动作预测远远不够。代理必须协调感知、规划、执行、进度验证和恢复等过程，而单纯的动作预测或技能决策无法保证操作在当前状态下的有效性及结果能被验证。

Method: 将每个技能决策视为执行提案：运行时在执行前检查前提条件，执行后验证结果。通过固定的可执行技能接口连接高层技能选择、受限低层VLA执行和后动作验证，形成统一代理循环。该接口记录规划、执行、验证和恢复事件为结构化轨迹，为各组件提供监督信号，并支持在线自适应。使用Qwen3-VL和OpenPI/pi0.5在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO上实例化。

Result: 任务自适应的低层VLA策略在RoboTwin 2.0的50个任务上平均成功率达86.20%，在LIBERO的四个套件上平均成功率达97.40%。在四个记忆依赖的RMBench任务上，同一方法仅实现12.5%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: EmbodiedSkills提供了一个可训练且可检查的智能体层，能将VLA策略转化为闭环具身系统。但框架也暴露了记忆依赖任务的局限性——纯任务自适应的动作执行在需要长期记忆的任务上表现不佳，说明框架的优势在于闭环验证和恢复机制，而非单纯的记忆能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but long-horizon tasks require more than action prediction. An agent must coordinate perception, planning, execution, progress verification, and recovery as the physical state evolves. An action prediction or a model-generated skill decision does not, by itself, guarantee that the proposed operation is valid in the current state or that its outcome will be verified. We propose EmbodiedSkills, a unified framework that treats each skill decision as an execution proposal: the runtime checks its prerequisites before execution and verifies the outcome afterward. A shared executable-skill interface connects high-level skill selection, bounded low-level VLA execution, and post-action verification within a single agent loop. Because this interface remains fixed, low-level VLA policies can be replaced or adapted without changing the agent loop. The interface also records planning, execution, verification, and recovery events as structured trajectories, which provide supervision for individual components and can support optional online adaptation when interactive feedback is available. We instantiate EmbodiedSkills with Qwen3-VL and OpenPI/pi0.5 on RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO. Task-adapted low-level VLA policies achieve an average success rate of 86.20% across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks and 97.40% across the four LIBERO suites. These results establish the execution performance of the task-adapted low-level VLA policies used in EmbodiedSkills. On four memory-dependent RMBench tasks, the same task-adapted execution approach achieves 12.5% average success. The framework provides a trainable and inspectable agent layer for turning these policies into closed-loop embodied systems.

</details>


### [36] [Integrating Traffic Noise Emission Modelling into Variable Speed Limit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01339)
*Jiawen Meng,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声感知的可变限速（VSL）框架，将聚合交通状态估计与简化CNOSSOS-EU排放指标相结合，通过阶段式控制器动态调整限速等级。24小时评估中，该策略相比自由通行条件将接收点等效声级降低2.9 dB(A)，同时保持比永久低速限速模式高出约11.3 km/h的平均车速，验证了将环境绩效指标纳入运营速度控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路交通噪声是重大环境挑战，但现有速度管理策略大多是静态的，无法响应交通噪声排放的短期变化。尽管VSL系统已广泛部署用于安全和拥堵缓解，但交通噪声很少被明确作为运营控制目标，缺乏将噪声作为动态控制目标的框架。

Method: 构建噪声感知VSL框架：（1）集成聚合交通状态估计；（2）采用基于简化CNOSSOS-EU模型的排放指标；（3）设计带时变参考阈值的阶段式控制器，根据估计的排放状态动态调整离散限速等级；（4）使用经验高速公路数据标定的微观交通仿真进行评估，并跨多个随机实现进行复制。

Result: 24小时评估期内，自适应策略相比无限制交通条件降低接收点等效声级2.9 dB(A)，同时平均车速比永久低速限速模式高约11.3 km/h。周期分析显示速度降低仅在排放水平接近标定阈值时选择性地激活。交通稳定性指标显示车速变异性较无限制运行适度增加，但制动强度显著低于统一低速限速策略。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将环境绩效指标（噪声）整合到运营速度控制中的可行性，为传统基于基础设施的噪声减缓措施提供了实用的补充方案，同时避免了永久低速限速对通行效率的过度损害。

Abstract: Road traffic noise remains a major environmental challenge, yet most speed management strategies are static and do not respond to short-term variations in traffic noise emissions. Although variable speed limit (VSL) systems are widely deployed for safety and congestion mitigation, traffic noise is rarely treated as an explicit operational control objective.
  This paper proposes a noise-aware VSL framework that integrates aggregated traffic-state estimation with a simplified CNOSSOS-EU-based emission indicator. A stage-based controller with time-varying reference thresholds dynamically adjusts discrete speed-limit levels in response to estimated emission conditions. The framework is evaluated using microscopic traffic simulation calibrated with empirical motorway data and replicated across multiple stochastic realisations.
  Over a 24-hour evaluation period, the adaptive strategy reduces the receiver-based equivalent sound level by 2.9 dB(A) relative to unrestricted traffic conditions, while maintaining an average vehicle speed approximately 11.3 km/h higher than a permanently imposed low-speed regime. Period-wise analysis shows that speed reductions are activated selectively when emission levels approach calibrated targets, rather than enforcing a constant intermediate limit. Traffic stability indicators reveal moderate increases in speed variability compared with unrestricted operation, but substantially lower braking intensity than under uniform low-speed enforcement.
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating environmental performance indicators into operational speed control, providing a practical complement to conventional infrastructure-based noise mitigation measures.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
