# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [A Dimension-Reducing Fréchet Simplification Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00393)
*Boris Aronov,Tsuri Farhana,Matthew J. Katz,Indu Ramesh*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究曲线/几何树的维度缩减（投影）问题：在平面中，给定多边形曲线P（n个顶点），构造O(n log n)大小的数据结构，支持"查询线ℓ上至多k个顶点的曲线Q，使其与P的离散Fréchet距离最小"的查询（每次O(k²log³n+klog⁴n)时间）。推广到几何树上的路径查询（O(k²polylog n)时间）。对高维情形（d≥3），针对g-平面上的约简，构造近似算法，保证距离在(1+ε₀)因子内，查询时间O(f(ε₀)k²log²n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线简化与降维是计算几何与数据压缩的核心问题。已有的Fréchet距离相关数据结构研究多集中于查询是否在阈值内，而本文关注更强的"查询中直接构造最优或近似最优的简化曲线"，即从所有落在查询子空间（线/平面）上且至多k个顶点的曲线中，找到与原始曲线离散Fréchet距离最小的那条。这一问题的挑战在于需要同时处理顶点数约束、子空间约束与距离最优性，此前缺乏高效的数据结构支持。

Method: 本文提出用于支持简化查询的数据结构。对于平面情况，利用O(n log n)大小的预处理结构，将查询分解为可在查询线上搜索最优顶点配置的子问题，通过动态规划/几何搜索方法在O(k²log³n+klog⁴n)内求解。对于树上的路径查询，利用树分解与路径结构复用将查询转化为近线性复杂度。对于高维情况，引入ε₀-相关的函数f(ε₀)=(1+1/ε₀)^((d-1)/2)，构造大小O(n log n + f(ε₀)n)的结构，通过近似技术获得(1+ε₀)-近似解，查询时间为O(f(ε₀)k²log²n)。

Result: 对于平面多边形曲线的线查询，实现O(n log n)预处理与O(k²log³n+klog⁴n)查询时间。对几何树上任一路径查询达到O(k²polylog n)时间。对d维空间中的g-平面维度缩减问题，在O(f(ε₀)k²log²n)时间内获得(1+ε₀)-近似最优曲线，数据结构大小为O(n log n + f(ε₀)n)。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了针对Fréchet距离的"查询驱动型"曲线简化问题，给出了平面精确解、树路径扩展与高维近似解的完整方案，展示了在固定k与固定维数下高效的数据结构设计可能性，为该方向后续研究建立了理论框架。

Abstract: Let $P$ be a polygonal curve with $n$ vertices in the plane. We construct a data structure of size $O(n \log n)$ suited for simplification queries of the following kind. Given a query line $\ell$ and an integer $k\ge1$, find a curve $Q$ on $\ell$ with at most $k$ vertices that minimizes the discrete Fréchet distance to $P$, among all such curves. Using our data structure, a query can be handled in $O(k^2 \log^3 n + k\log^4 n)$ time.
  More generally, a geometric tree $T$ on $n$ vertices in the plane can be preprocessed into a near-linear-size structure so that, given a pair $u$, $v$ of its vertices, a line $\ell$, and an integer $k\ge1$, one can find a curve $Q$ on $\ell$ with at most $k$ vertices that minimizes the discrete Fréchet distance to the path from $u$ to $v$ in $T$, in time $O(k^2 \mathop{polylog} n)$.
  For the general dimension-reduction problem, where $P$ is a curve in $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d \ge 3$), $0 < \varepsilon_0 < 1$ is a real parameter, and a query specifies a $g$-flat $h$ ($1 \le g \le d-1$) and an integer $k \ge 1$, we construct a data structure of size $O(n\log n + f(\varepsilon_0) n)$, where $f(\varepsilon_0)=(1+1/\varepsilon_0)^{(d-1)/2}$, that allows us to find a curve $Q$ on $h$ with at most $k$ vertices, whose discrete Fréchet distance to $P$ is at most $1+\varepsilon_0$ times the distance of $Q^*$ to $P$, where $Q^*$ is such a curve that minimizes the distance to $P$. The query handling time is $O(f(\varepsilon_0) k^2 \log^2 n)$.

</details>


### [98] [Efficient K-Visibility Query in Polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01472)
*Yeganeh Bahoo,Roni Sherman*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精细的单元分解框架，用于支持k-可见性多边形的空间预处理查询。通过识别最小拓扑边界集（主顶点水平线和次级互临界铰链线），实现了Θ(n^4)个单元的空间分解，配合组合δ压缩方案达到O(n^4)的存储和O(log n + m)的最优查询时间，并自然扩展到含孔多边形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然从单个查询点计算k-可见性多边形已被充分研究，但现有的空间预处理方法依赖于通过所有顶点对的完整O(n^2)线排列，没有刻画决定k-可见性所需的最小子集拓扑边界。这导致预处理的几何事件不精确，空间与复杂度分析不够紧致。

Method: 提出精炼的单元分解框架，隔离出支配k-可见性的两类精确几何事件：主顶点水平线（primary vertex horizon lines）和次级互临界铰链线（secondary mutually critical hinge lines）。证明该最小划分线集产生Θ(n^4)个单元，在每个单元内k-可见性多边形的组合结构严格不变。进一步采用组合δ压缩方案跨单元边界处理，实现O(n^4)的存储复杂度，支持O(log n + m)的查询时间重建大小为m的显式k-可见性多边形，框架可扩展至含孔多边形。

Result: 该框架的空间分解复杂度为Θ(n^4)个单元，存储复杂度为O(n^4)，查询时间复杂度为最优的O(log n + m)。框架自然扩展到含孔多边形场景。

Conclusion: 通过刻画最小集拓扑事件并结合组合压缩，该工作解决了k-可见性空间预处理中几何边界刻画不精确的问题，在最优查询时间下实现了空间高效的预处理方案，并保持了向含孔多边形的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper investigates $k$-visibility, where a line of sight can penetrate up to $k$ obstacles. While computing the $k$-visibility polygon from a single query point is well-studied, existing spatial preprocessing approaches rely on full $O(n^2)$ line arrangements through all vertex pairs without characterizing the minimal set of topological boundaries. We present a refined cell decomposition framework that isolates the exact geometric events governing $k$-visibility: primary vertex horizon lines and secondary mutually critical hinge lines. We prove that this minimal set of partition lines yields a spatial decomposition of $Θ(n^4)$ cells within which the combinatorial structure of the $k$-visibility polygon remains strictly invariant. By leveraging a combinatorial $δ$-compression scheme across cell boundaries, we achieve an overall storage complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n^4)$ while supporting optimal $\mathcal{O}(\log n + m)$ query time to reconstruct explicit $k$-visibility polygons of size $m$. Our framework naturally extends to polygons containing holes.

</details>


### [99] [Sierpiński--Knopp Wasserstein Distance for Persistence Diagrams and Applications to 2-Wasserstein Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01528)
*Sebastien Tchitchek,Julien Tierny*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sierpiński-Knopp (SK) Wasserstein距离，一种快速计算持久图之间距离的度量。该方法通过Sierpiński-Knopp空间填充曲线将图点映射到单位区间，再通过一维最优分配以O(N log N)复杂度实现对角线感知的点匹配。该距离可控制经典2-Wasserstein距离，支持Hilbert空间等距嵌入和正定高斯核，在12个科学数据集的227个持久图上实现626倍中位加速和2100倍总体加速，其聚类性能与传统W2方法相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久图是拓扑数据分析中的核心对象，但经典2-Wasserstein距离（W2）的计算代价高，难以应用于大规模数据分析任务。研究者希望找到一种既能保持W2几何优良性质（对角线感知、可控性、内核友好）又具有高效计算性能的距离度量，以支持大规模持久图的聚类、分割等机器学习任务。

Method: 该方法将持久图中远离对角线的点及其对角线投影，通过Sierpiński-Knopp空间填充曲线映射到上三角对角线区域上的单位区间，将多维点集编码简化为一维序列。随后利用一维最优分配算法实现显式的对角线感知点分配，以O(N log N)复杂度完成计算。文中还证明了该度量能控制经典W2距离、存在显式Hilbert空间等距嵌入，并诱导正定高斯核。此外，基于该曲线路径上的点分配提出更紧凑的替代不相似度量WΓ。

Result: 在12个科学数据集的227个持久图上，dSK相对于最先进的W2近似方法实现每集合中位626倍加速，总体基准加速达2100倍。平均链接聚类中，dSK和WΓ在12个集合中的8个上精确匹配W2的划分结果。基于dSK的Hilbert k-means和高斯谱聚类分别达到0.756和0.800的平均调整兰德指数（ARI），优于平均链接在W2上取得的0.750。高斯dSK核还成功应用于有序持久图集合的连续分割任务。

Conclusion: SK-Wasserstein距离为持久图分析提供了一种兼具理论保障与计算效率的度量工具。它在保持与经典W2距离几何高度一致性的同时，实现了数量级以上的加速，并天然兼容欧氏和基于核的机器学习方法，为大规模拓扑数据分析提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Sierpiński-Knopp (SK) Wasserstein distance, a fast metric between persistence diagrams. The SK-Wasserstein distance, denoted $d_{\mathrm{SK}}$, maps diagram points and their diagonal projections to the unit interval via the Sierpiński-Knopp space-filling curve on the upper diagonal triangle. The encoded point sets are then efficiently matched via one-dimensional optimal assignment, in \(O(N\log N)\) steps, yielding an explicit diagonal-aware point assignment between the two input persistence diagrams. We show that the SK-Wasserstein distance controls the classical \(2\)-Wasserstein distance between diagrams, admits an explicit isometric embedding into a Hilbert space, and induces a positive-definite Gaussian kernel, making the resulting geometry directly compatible with Euclidean and kernel-based learning methods. A tighter surrogate dissimilarity, noted \(W_Γ\), is also introduced based on the point assignments along the curve. Experiments on 12 scientific collections comprising 227 diagrams show median per-collection speedup of \(d_{\mathrm{SK}}\) over state-of-the-art approximations of \(W_2\) is \(626\times\), while the aggregate speedup over the full benchmark is \(2100\times\). Average-linkage partitions obtained from \(d_{\mathrm{SK}}\) and \(W_Γ\) each exactly match the corresponding \(W_2\) partition on 8 of the 12 collections. Hilbert \(k\)-means and Gaussian spectral clustering, both based on \(d_{\mathrm{SK}}\), achieve mean adjusted Rand indices (ARI) of \(0.756\) and \(0.800\), respectively, with respect to the benchmark reference partitions, compared to \(0.750\) obtained by average linkage on \(W_2\). The Gaussian \(d_{\mathrm{SK}}\) kernel supports other kernel-based analysis tasks, as illustrated by its use for contiguous segmentation of ordered diagram collections in our experiments.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Chern--Simons Fluctuations and Information Geometry in Discrete Electromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00020)
*Jean-Pierre Magnot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在闭合定向三维流形上，针对Whitney离散化的电磁场构造了螺旋度约束的统计态，通过单纯形de Rham复形获得精确的离散规范对称性，并在约化相空间上引入螺旋度偏置高斯系综，推导了配分函数、均值螺旋度、相对熵和Fisher信息等显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立离散电磁场中螺旋度约束统计态的严格数学框架，弥合连续场论中磁螺旋度统计性质与离散数值实现之间的鸿沟，并为相干螺旋度结构的推断提供理论基础。

Method: 利用Whitney离散化结合单纯形de Rham复形实现精确离散规范对称性；通过Whitney内积分离精确、调和与余闭三个扇区；固定调和模式后，在约化电磁相空间上引入螺旋度偏置的Gaussian系综，并推导熵约束下的显式参数公式。

Result: 导出了可容许参数、配分函数、均值螺旋度、相对熵与Fisher信息的闭合表达式；证明该分布在给定均值螺旋度约束下唯一最小化相对熵；螺旋度磁化率等于离散Chern-Simons泛函的方差，控制相邻统计态的局部可区分性。

Conclusion: 该统计态代表了约束推断而非动力学平衡，因为磁螺旋度在无约束Maxwell动力学下一般不守恒；这为理解湍流与等离子体中的螺旋度条件统计提供了离散框架。

Abstract: We construct helicity-conditioned statistical states for a Whitney-discretized electromagnetic field on a closed oriented three-manifold. The simplicial de Rham complex provides exact discrete gauge symmetry, while the Whitney inner product separates exact, harmonic, and coexact sectors. The spatial Abelian Chern--Simons functional is gauge invariant and depends only on the coexact potential. After fixing harmonic modes, we introduce a helicity-biased Gaussian ensemble on the reduced electromagnetic phase space and derive explicit formulas for its admissible parameters, partition function, mean helicity, relative entropy, and Fisher information. The distribution uniquely minimizes relative entropy under a prescribed mean-helicity constraint. Its helicity susceptibility equals the variance of the discrete Chern--Simons functional and controls the local distinguishability of neighboring statistical states. Because magnetic helicity is generally not conserved under unconstrained Maxwell dynamics, these states represent conditioned inference rather than dynamical equilibrium.

</details>


### [101] [Alternative-mean trace divergences: geometry, data processing, and barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00034)
*Trung Dung Vuong,Hiroki Shudo,Hiroyuki Osaka*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究算子单调函数生成的"替代均值迹泛函"altPhi_f，证明了它是Bhatia-Gaubert-Jain意义下的量子散度，其对角的Hessian诱导Bures-Wasserstein黎曼度量的正倍数，并建立了与Bures-Wasserstein距离d_BW之间带最佳常数的双边比较不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息与矩阵分析中，量子散度是刻画正定矩阵/量子态之间"距离"的重要工具，而Bures-Wasserstein度量是正定锥上具有几何意义的黎曼度量。作者希望构造一类新的量子散度（与已有的Bregman型或Amari型散度不同的"替代均值"结构），并精确刻画其与Bures-Wasserstein度量之间的定量关系。

Method: 利用算子单调函数的积分表示（Löwner表示）定义"替代均值"迹泛函，借助Bhatia-Gaubert-Jain确立的量子散度判定框架验证其散度公理；通过计算Hessian在单位点处的显式表达式确定其诱导度量的倍数关系；最后利用算子单调性与算子不等式技巧，推导出上下界的最优（sharp）常数比较不等式。

Result: 1) altPhi_f是量子散度；2) 其对角Hessian等于正倍数乘以Bures-Wasserstein黎曼度量；3) 建立了带最优常数s(1-s)与(1-s+s^2)的双边比较不等式，其中s=f'(1)。

Conclusion: 构造的替代均值迹泛函构成一类新的量子散度，其与Bures-Wasserstein距离的精确关系由正数s=f'(1)刻画，这为连接量子散度理论与正定矩阵几何提供了新途径，并为后续的信息几何应用奠定了定量基础。

Abstract: Let $f:(0,\infty)\to(0,\infty)$ be a nontrivial normalized operator
  monotone function and set $s=f'(1)$. We introduce the alternative-mean
  trace functional $$ \altPhi_f(A,B) :=\Tr(A\nabla_s B) -\Tr\!\left( f(A^{-1}\sharp B)\,A\,f(A^{-1}\sharp B) \right). $$
  on the positive definite cone. We prove that $\altPhi_f$ is a quantum divergence in the sense of
  Bhatia--Gaubert--Jain whose diagonal Hessian induces a positive multiple
  of the Bures--Wasserstein Riemannian metric. We also establish the sharp
  comparison
  $$
  s(1-s)d_{\rm BW}(A,B)^2
  \le \altPhi_f(A,B)
  \le (1-s+s^2)d_{\rm BW}(A,B)^2.
  $$

</details>


### [102] [Fock-Space Formulation of the Boltzmann Collision Operator for Maxwell Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00040)
*Ilya Karlin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了非线性玻尔兹曼碰撞算子（针对麦克斯韦分子）的表示无关的对称Fock表述。作者从标准速度空间算子出发，推导了Bobylev傅里叶恒等式，并证明了一个与碰撞几何无关的规范升腾-融合交织定理，揭示了碰撞算子的内在代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻尔兹曼碰撞算子的内在结构，尤其是麦克斯韦分子情形下其代数性质与几何无关的本质，从而为动力学理论提供不依赖于具体坐标实现的抽象框架。

Method: 采用对称Fock空间框架，将速度空间算子抽象为Fock空间上的双线性映射。通过证明升腾-融合交织定理（incoming Fock腿的线性映射与对称代数的融合），将标准碰撞算子的几何细节剥离，再通过Bobylev碰撞映射选择出麦克斯韦顶点，获得满足精确分次性质的碰撞算子。同时利用Bargmann坐标和Hermite函数、傅里叶实现检验协变性。

Result: 所得双线性映射满足精确分次Q(H_p,H_q)⊆H_{p+q}；其单真空约束恢复完整的Wang Chang-Uhlenbeck谱；矩适配使非平衡层级严格三角化。对非齐次问题，两条路线（Hermite函数与傅里叶实现）给出相同的抽象Fock传播和相同的首阶Chapman-Enskog源，Prandtl数Pr=2/3。

Conclusion: 碰撞算子的Fock表述本质上与坐标实现无关，Bargmann坐标等只是使其透明的工具而非其定义。该工作为麦克斯韦分子动力学提供了内在的代数框架，证明坐标实现的选取纯属计算经济性问题，不影响结构性的Fock表述。

Abstract: We develop a representation-independent symmetric-Fock formulation of the nonlinear Boltzmann collision operator for Maxwell molecules. Starting from the standard velocity-space operator, we give a short derivation of Bobylev's Fourier identity and isolate its characteristic structure: two linearly related Fourier arguments followed by multiplication. We then prove, independently of collision geometry and of any coordinate realization, a canonical lift--fusion intertwining theorem: one-particle linear maps lift to the two incoming Fock legs and symmetric-algebra multiplication fuses them into a single outgoing state. Bargmann coordinates make this intrinsic construction especially transparent, but do not define it; Bobylev's collision maps subsequently select the Maxwell vertex from the already graded lift--fusion class. The resulting bilinear map obeys the exact grading \[ \widehat{\mathcal Q}(\mathscr H_p,\mathscr H_q)\subseteq\mathscr H_{p+q}, \] which gives an intrinsic origin for the triangular structures of Maxwell kinetics. Its one-vacuum restriction yields the complete Wang Chang--Uhlenbeck spectrum, while moment adaptation makes the nonequilibrium hierarchy strictly triangular and every finite compatible Fock jet an exact autonomous factor with global exponential relaxation. For the inhomogeneous problem, we extend realization covariance to the nonlinear two-leg vertex and evaluate the moving-frame connection independently through Hermite-function and Fourier realizations. The two routes give the same abstract Fock propagation and the same first Chapman--Enskog source; its traceless level-$2$ and vector level-$3$ sectors are relaxed by the exact Maxwell rates, giving $\Pr=2/3$. This provides an explicit computational demonstration that coordinate realizations may be chosen for economy without entering the structural Fock formulation.

</details>


### [103] [Szegő-type determinant asymptotics for generalized Hilbert matrices with edge eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00255)
*Martin Gebert,Heinrich Küttler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了广义Hilbert矩阵的I±H的N→∞行列式渐近行为，重点关注δ<-1/2的新区域，揭示了衰退指数的二分性，该二分性源于δ<-1/2时极限算子的±1边缘特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Hilbert矩阵行列式的大N渐近在Anderson正交性灾变等物理问题中有重要应用。此前δ≥-1/2的情形已知，本文旨在填补δ<-1/2这一未知区域的空白，并理解渐近衰退指数为何在不同参数区间呈现不同形式。

Method: 通过分析广义Hilbert矩阵相关算子的谱性质，特别是对δ<-1/2情形，利用极限算子的±1边缘特征值来推导行列式的大N幂律渐近。对δ非半整数情形，证明log det(I_N±H_N^δ)=-(θ_δ²±θ_δ)/2·log N+O(1)，其中θ_δ在δ<-1/2取δ，在δ≥-1/2取arcsin(sin(δπ))/π。

Result: 获得了δ<-1/2时log det(I_N±H_N^δ)的精确大N幂律渐近表达式；特别地，对δ<-1/2证明了log det(I_N-(H_N^δ)²)=-δ²log N+O(1)。

Conclusion: 揭示了行列式渐近衰退指数的二分现象：该二分源于δ<-1/2时极限算子出现的±1边缘特征值，这为理解Anderson正交性灾变中的相关行列式提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: We compute the large-$N$ asymptotics of the determinant of the identity plus or minus a generalized Hilbert matrix. For $N \in \mathbb{N}$ and $δ\in \mathbb{R} \setminus \{-1, -2,
  \ldots\}$, let \[
  H_N^δ
  := \left(
  \frac{\sin((1+δ)π)}{π(j+k+1+δ)}
  \right)_{j,k=0}^{N-1} \] be the generalized Hilbert matrix. For $δ$ not a half-integer, we prove the large-$N$ power-law asymptotics of the determinant \[
  \log \text{det}(I_N \pm H_N^δ) = -\frac{θ_δ^2 \pm θ_δ}{2} \log N + O(1) \] where $θ_δ:= δ$ if $δ< -1/2$, and $θ_δ:= \frac 1 π\arcsin(\sin(δπ))$ if $δ\ge -1/2$ and $\arcsin \colon [-1,1] \to [-\fracπ{2}, \fracπ{2}]$ denotes the principal branch of $\arcsin$. For $δ\geq -\frac 12$ the asymptotics is known. The novelty of the present paper is the regime $δ< - \frac 12$ and understanding the dichotomy in the decay exponent. This stems from the $\pm 1$ edge eigenvalues of the limiting operator appearing for $δ< - \frac 12$. Most notably, we obtain for $δ< -\frac12$ \[
  \log \det\bigl(I_N - (H_N^δ)^2\bigr) = -δ^2 \log
  N + O(1). \] Such determinants arise in the study of Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe.

</details>


### [104] [Robin Laplacians with point interactions on unbounded domains: discrete spectrum and coupling asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00315)
*Diego Noja,Francesco Raso Stoia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维和三维无界及外部C^{1,1}域上Neumann和Robin拉普拉斯算子的有限多点相互作用的离散谱。通过普通边界三元组实现自伴延拓，利用Robin Green核构造gamma场和Weyl矩阵表征背景谱以下的特征值，给出精确的有限维计数公式，并分析单中心和多中心耦合的特征值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在无界和外域上带有有限多点相互作用的Neumann与Robin拉普拉斯算子的离散谱结构，旨在理解点相互作用如何影响背景算子的谱性质，并给出精确的特征值计数公式和定量描述。

Method: 采用普通边界三元组（ordinary boundary triple）方法将算子实现为自伴延拓，gamma场和Weyl矩阵由Robin Green核构造。通过Weyl矩阵刻画背景谱以下的本征值，推导精确的有限维计数公式。针对单中心情形识别临界耦合，运用实解析性分析特征值分支；对多中心标量耦合情形利用通用渐近分析和解耦极限的方法处理。

Result: 建立了离散谱的精确有限维计数公式，不要求Weyl矩阵在零能量处的有限极限假设。单中心情形下识别出临界耦合，证明唯一特征值分支是实解析的、严格递增且严格凹的。对多中心标量耦合Θ=αI_N，足够强的吸引精确产生N个背景谱以下的特征值，且均具有与全域一致的普适首阶渐近。当背景谱底部为孤立特征值时，分支对任意有限耦合均存在，并给出耦合趋向+∞时的首阶解耦渐近。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了二维三维无界与外域上点相互作用算子的离散谱理论，提供了精确的计数公式和特征值分支的定量行为描述，并通过外部球和外部圆盘模型验证了临界耦合及阈值行为的理论结果。

Abstract: We investigate the discrete spectrum of finitely many point interactions for Neumann and Robin Laplacians on special unbounded and exterior $C^{1,1}$ domains in dimensions two and three. The operators are realized as self-adjoint extensions through an ordinary boundary triple whose gamma field and Weyl matrix are constructed from the Robin Green kernel. Eigenvalues below the background spectrum are characterized by the Weyl matrix, yielding an exact finite-dimensional counting formula. If the background operator is non-negative, this also gives the number of negative eigenvalues without assuming a finite zero-energy limit of the Weyl matrix. In the one-centre case we identify the critical coupling and prove that the unique eigenvalue branch is real analytic, strictly increasing, and strictly concave. For scalar multicentre couplings $Θ=αI_N$, sufficiently strong attraction produces exactly $N$ eigenvalues below the background spectrum, and all of them have universal leading asymptotics coinciding with the whole-space laws. If the bottom of the background spectrum is an isolated eigenvalue, the branch exists for every finite coupling and we determine its leading decoupling asymptotics as the coupling tends to $+\infty$. Explicit exterior-sphere and exterior-disk models illustrate the critical couplings and their threshold behaviour.

</details>


### [105] [Topological Recursion on Spectral Curves with Infinite Ramification Loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00517)
*Quinten Weller*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对物理学文献中出现的具有无限基数分支点的谱曲线上的Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归过程，建立了严格的数学框架。作者在足够广泛的谱曲线上定义了拓扑递归，证明了相关无限级数的收敛性及相关子函数满足期望的性质，并发现了两个不同谱曲线产生相同量子曲线的有趣巧合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学文献中已有少量论文将Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归方法应用于具有无限基数分支点轨迹的谱曲线，但数学文献对此存在空白——尚未严格验证所得无限和是否收敛，以及所得相关子是否具备拓扑递归的所有期望性质。本文旨在弥合这一数学与物理之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者在适合涵盖前述物理应用的一类广泛谱曲线上定义拓扑递归，并严格证明该定义满足直观期望的所有性质（包括无限级数的收敛性及相关子的性质）。

Result: 成功建立了涵盖无限基数分支点情形的拓扑递归严格的数学基础，并作为副产品发现了量子曲线中两个不同谱曲线产生同一量子曲线的新颖巧合现象，这在现有文献中此前未曾出现。

Conclusion: 本文填补了物理研究中无限分支点拓扑递归缺乏严格数学基础的空白，同时揭示了量子曲线领域新的数学现象，即谱曲线到量子曲线的映射不再是一一对应的，这一发现为后续研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recently in the physics literature there have been a small, but growing, number of papers that have applied the Eynard-Orantin topological recursion procedure to spectral curves with ramification loci of infinite cardinality. However, there is a corresponding gap in the mathematics literature; indeed, it has not been checked rigorously whether the resulting infinite sums converge, or whether the resulting correlators have all the desired properties of the topological recursion. The present work aims to bridge this gap by defining topological recursion on a suitably broad class of spectral curves to cover the aforementioned physics applications and to rigorously prove that this definition has the properties one would intuitively expect. As a by-product, some interesting coincidences involving quantum curves are discovered, where two different spectral curves yield the same quantum curve, a situation that has, hitherto, not appeared in the literature.

</details>


### [106] [Two-photon quantum Rabi models - Spectral degeneracy and symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00750)
*Cid Reyes-Bustos,Masato Wakayama*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双光子非对称量子Rabi模型的能量简并问题，证明了简并本征值的存在性，揭示了系统简并与超椭圆曲线和数论结构之间的深层联系，并给出了谱简并的完整刻画及关于隐藏对称性与简并关系的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Rabi模型是量子光学中描述光与物质相互作用的基础模型，精确理解其谱性质（尤其是双光子非对称推广形式的能量简并）对于量子光学和量子信息技术等领域至关重要。

Method: 利用Fuchsian ODE的单值性数据证明简并本征值及对称算子的存在性，借助代数曲线（超椭圆曲线）和数论工具对谱简并进行完整刻画，并通过双变量多项式零点对强相互作用下的能量曲线进行近似。

Result: 证明了双光子非对称量子Rabi模型中简并本征值的严格存在性，给出了谱简并的完整特征化，并在强耦合条件下通过双变量多项式的零轨迹获得了能量曲线的高精度近似。

Conclusion: 研究表明双光子量子Rabi模型的简并结构与本征谱整体结构密切相关，并提出了隐藏对称性与能量简并之间关系的显式描述猜想，为理解该模型的深层数学结构开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The quantum Rabi model occupies a distinguished role in the study of quantum light and matter, describing the most fundamental interactions. Elucidation of its spectral properties, along with those of its generalizations, is necessary for quantum optics and applications in areas such as quantum information technologies. In this paper, we explore a precise description of energy degeneracy of the two-photon asymmetric quantum Rabi model and the nature of the system's symmetries; as a result, we demonstrate that these have significant relationships with the structure of certain algebraic curves (hyperelliptic curves) and number theory. Concretely, we first prove the existence of degenerate eigenvalues using the monodromy data of the Fuchsian ODE, which draws the eigenvalue problem of the system, and the existence of associated symmetry operators, previously discovered and studied heuristically in physics. Subsequently, we give a complete characterization of spectral degeneracies and present a series of conjectures showing that the degeneracy may determine the entire energy spectral structure. More precisely, for sufficiently strong interaction, we obtain an excellent approximation of the energy curves by the zero locus of a two-variable polynomial associated to degenerate eigenstates. In addition, we postulate an explicit description of the relation between the missing symmetry at the Hamiltonian level (hidden symmetry) and the degeneracy of the two-photon quantum Rabi model.

</details>


### [107] [Blow-Up, Asymptotics, and Improbability of Collision Singularities in the n-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00793)
*Nathan Duignan,Manuel Quaschner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了n体系统中具有齐次度-α（0<α<2）的两体势函数的有限时间奇异性，系统不一定具有吸引力且包含有界时变外力。作者开发了一种系统方法，用于在系统中其他地方同时存在碰撞或非碰撞奇异性的情况下分析碰撞子系统，将von Zeipel和Painlevé的经典结果推广到此类子系统，在总碰撞附近建立其内能界，并实现了在无能量守恒情况下仍然有效的McGehee爆破。该爆破给出了簇尺寸的渐近行为，在吸引势情况下证明了向中心构型集合的收敛性及相对位置、相对速度和内角动量的渐近性。作为应用，构造了碰撞初始条件构成零测度集的简化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统n体奇异性分析通常假设势能守恒且势能具有特定形式。当系统包含非吸引势、时变外力，或系统中同时存在多个不同位置的奇异性时，经典工具（如von Zeipel定理、Painlevé定理和McGehee爆破）不再直接适用。本文旨在填补这一空白，为更一般的物理系统提供奇点分析的统一框架。

Method: 作者首先将经典von Zeipel和Painlevé结果推广到碰撞子系统（允许系统中其他位置存在同时发生的碰撞或非碰撞奇异性）。然后在无能量守恒假设下建立子系统在总碰撞附近内能的界，并实现一种不依赖能量守恒的McGehee坐标爆破变换来分析簇的动态渐近行为。对于吸引势情形，进一步推导出相对位置、相对速度和内角动量的收敛性质。

Result: 获得了碰撞簇尺寸的渐近展开，在吸引势情况下建立了簇收敛到中心构型集合的结论以及相对位置、相对速度和内角动量的渐近公式。利用这些渐近结果，构造了一个简化的证明表明导致碰撞的初始条件集合的测度为零。

Conclusion: 该工作为包含非标准势函数和外部扰动力的广义n体系统中的有限时间奇异性提供了系统的分析框架，扩展了经典奇异点理论的适用范围，并通过测量零定理给出实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study finite-time singularities in $n$-body systems with pair potentials that are homogeneous of degree $-α$, where $0<α<2$, that are not necessarily attractive and with bounded time-dependent external forces. We develop a systematic method to analyse a colliding subsystem in the presence of simultaneous collision or non-collision singularities elsewhere in the system. We extend classical results of von Zeipel and Painlevé to such subsystems, establish bounds on their internal energy near total collision, and implement a McGehee blow-up that remains effective without conservation of energy. The blow-up yields the asymptotics of the cluster size, and in the case of attractive potentials, the convergence towards the set of central configurations and asymptotics of relative position, relative velocities, and internal angular momentum. As an application of the asymptotic results, we construct a simplified proof that the initial conditions leading to collision form a set of measure zero.

</details>


### [108] [A general improbability result for non-collision singularities as subsystems with at most four particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00876)
*Manuel Quaschner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了n体问题中满足特定条件的非碰撞奇异性初始条件集合是极不可能的（测度为零），推广了此前仅n=4情形的已知结果，为1984年Barry Simon十五个数学物理问题之首提供了更一般的解答方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n体问题中是否存在导致非碰撞奇异的初始条件集合（系统转动惯量发散而非碰撞）是否'不可能'（测度为零），这一问题自1984年被Barry Simon列为数学物理十五大问题之首以来，仅对n=4的情形得到肯定回答。作者希望将此结果推广至更一般的情形。

Method: 利用末态簇分解（final cluster decomposition），将系统划分为内部粒子强相互作用、跨子系统间仅存在微弱力的子系统。通过分析以下两类子系统行为：(1) 任意多个在其质心处发生全碰撞的子系统；(2) 恰好由四个粒子组成且发生非碰撞奇异性的子系统（即子系统的转动惯量发散），并假设这些四粒子子系统的渐近方向互不相同（这一条件较温和），从而证明奇异轨道集合的极不可能性。

Result: 证明了满足上述条件（任意数量的质心全碰撞子系统、恰好四粒子的非碰撞奇异性子系统且渐近方向互异）的所有奇异性初始条件集合是极不可能的。该结果涵盖了此前所有已知结论。

Conclusion: 这一结果不仅统一推广了已有的n=4结果，还指明了解决一般n体问题中非碰撞奇异性不可概率性这一开放问题的发展方向。

Abstract: Non-collision singularities of the $n$-body problem are initial conditions for which no global solution exists and which, however, do not lead to a collision in the limit; i.e., the moment of inertia of the system diverges. The question of whether the set of initial conditions leading to non-collision singularities in the $n$-body problem is improbable is open and is the first on Barry Simon's list of fifteen problems in mathematical physics from the year 1984. So far, this question has only been answered positively in the case $n=4$. Using a final cluster decomposition into subsystems whose particles interact strongly with each other and exert only small forces on particles from different subsystems, we can prove the improbability of the set of all singular orbits with the following conditions: -There may be arbitrarily many subsystems undergoing total collision at their center of mass. - There may be subsystems exhibiting a non-collision singularity, i.e. the respective moment of inertia of the subsystem diverges. These subsystems consist of exactly four particles each and the asymptotic directions of these subsystems have to differ (which is only a mild condition). This result includes all known statements and indicates a way how the general problem could be solved.

</details>


### [109] [$A_1$ - Coulomb branches over finite fields and Selberg Character sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01443)
*Junzhe Lyu,Andrey Smirnov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文受镜像对称启发，研究了A₁库仑分支上的有限域指数和，证明了其纤维结构由多项式高斯和支配，从而为Evans研究的Selberg特征和提供了几何实现，并利用Evans的求值公式获得了经典高斯和乘积形式的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像对称是连接数学与物理的重要桥梁，本文的动机在于通过镜像对称的视角为Selberg特征和（Evans研究的重要数论对象）提供几何实现，从而建立数论与算术几何之间的联系。

Method: 作者研究了A₁库仑分支在有限域F_q上的指数和，分析其纤维结构，将其与多项式高斯和建立对应关系，进而利用Evans给出的Selberg特征和的求值公式，将库仑分支指数和约化为经典高斯和的乘积形式。

Result: 获得了库仑分支指数和的显式公式，该公式以经典高斯和的乘积表达，同时给出了多项式高斯和与Selberg特征和之间的几何对应。

Conclusion: 本文从镜像对称出发，为Selberg特征和提供了新的几何解释，揭示了库仑分支指数和、多项式高斯和与经典高斯和之间的深层联系，取得了显式的解析结果。

Abstract: Motivated by mirror symmetry, we study exponential sums over the $\mathbb{F}_q$-points of the $A_1$ Coulomb branch. We show that their fiberwise structure is governed by polynomial Gauss sums, providing a geometric realization of Selberg character sums studied by Evans. Using Evans's evaluation, we obtain explicit formulas for the Coulomb-branch exponential sums as products of classical Gauss sums.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Shifted poles and chamber cancellation for classical Witten zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00290)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文确定了经典单变量Witten zeta函数在正实轴上的两族无穷极点：类型A_r在r≥5时q_r^A=2(r-4)/(r²+r-4)处有简单极点（除r=12,20为二重极点）；类型D_r在r≥4时q_r^D=(r-3)/(r(r-1)-1)处有简单极点（除D_8为二重极点）。同时证明类型B_r和C_r在q_r^BC处全纯（除秩7和11可能有简单极点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典单变量Witten zeta函数的解析性质，特别是其在正实轴上的极点结构，以加深对这类与李代数相关的zeta函数的理解。

Method: 结合穷举支持的分类（exhaustive support classifications）、显式分部积分恒等式（integration-by-parts identities）以及有限扇区关系（finite chamber relations）进行证明，不使用数值非零估计。

Result: 在根积归一化下，将A_r和D_r的简单留数以及三个二重极点的首项系数表示为gamma函数、三角函数和Riemann zeta值的组合。对于B_r和C_r函数在q_r^BC处的全纯性由二次法向Taylor系数决定；对每个固定的更高偶数法向阶数，确定了相关的连续B/C扇区和非零的精确条件。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了A_r和D_r类型Witten zeta函数在特定正实轴点的极点阶数，并给出了B_r/C_r类型的全纯性判定；辅助的扇区和结论不能单独完全分类完整Witten函数的极点。

Abstract: We determine two infinite families of poles on the positive real axis for classical single-variable Witten zeta functions. In type $A_r$, for $r \geq 5$, the point $q_r^A = 2(r-4)/(r^2+r-4)$ is a simple pole except in ranks $12$ and $20$, where it is a double pole. In type $D_r$, for $r \geq 4$, the point $q_r^D = (r-3)/(r(r-1)-1)$ is a simple pole except at $D_8$, where it is double. In root-product normalization, we express the simple residues and the leading coefficients of these three double poles in terms of gamma, trigonometric, and Riemann zeta values.
  For $r \geq 4$, the functions of types $B_r$ and $C_r$ are holomorphic at $q_r^{BC} = (r-3)/(r^2-1)$ except possibly in ranks $7$ and $11$, where any pole is simple. These pole and holomorphy statements arise from quadratic normal Taylor coefficients. For every fixed higher even normal degree, we also determine exactly when the associated continued $B/C$ chamber sum is nonzero; this auxiliary result does not by itself classify poles of the full Witten function. The proofs combine exhaustive support classifications, explicit integration-by-parts identities, and finite chamber relations. No numerical nonvanishing estimate is used.

</details>


### [111] [Gallagher's Theorem for Real-valued Brauer Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00298)
*Daryl Zane Adriano*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了有限群G中覆盖给定不可约p-Brauer特征θ的实值不可约Brauer特征个数的表达式，该表达式基于G_θ/N的p-正则θ-好共轭类，这是John Murray近期论文（arXiv:2301.01177）主要结果的模表示论类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果已知覆盖θ的G的不可约Brauer特征数等于G_θ/N的p-正则θ-好共轭类数。本文旨在将该结果推广到实值不可约Brauer特征的计数情形，建立其与p-正则θ-好共轭类之间的对应关系，作为John Murray在常表示论（ordinary character theory）中关于实值不可约特征计数结果的模模拟。

Method: 设G为有限群，N为G的正规子群，θ为N的不可约p-Brauer特征。本文利用p-正则θ-好共轭类（p-regular θ-good conjugacy classes）作为工具，构造了G中覆盖θ的实值不可约Brauer特征与该类共轭类之间的计数对应关系。这一方法是对Murray在常特征情形下所采用的类似技术向模表示论框架的直接推广。

Result: 建立了覆盖θ的实值不可约Brauer特征的个数与G_θ/N的p-正则θ-好共轭类个数之间的数学表达式，从而将John Murray的主要结果推广到了模表示论（p-Brauer特征）的语境中。

Conclusion: 本文的结论为实值不可约Brauer特征的计数提供了一种基于共轭类结构的显式刻画，证明了常表示论中Murray结果在模表示论中的对应版本成立，丰富了有限群模表示论中关于迹与实特征的研究工具。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group, let $N$ be a normal subgroup of $G$, and let $θ$ be an irreducible $p$-Brauer character of $N$, for a prime $p$. It is well known that the number of irreducible Brauer characters of $G$ lying over $θ$ equals the number of $p$-regular $θ$-good conjugacy classes of $G_θ/N$.
  In this note we express the number of real-valued irreducible Brauer characters of $G$ lying over $θ$ in terms of the $p$-regular $θ$-good conjugacy classes of $G_θ/N$. This is a modular analogue of one of the main results in a recent paper (arXiv:2301.01177) by John Murray.

</details>


### [112] [Axiomatics for generic categories in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00302)
*Karthik Ganapathy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个公理化框架，用于分析正特征下表示稳定性和等变交换代数中出现的一般类别。核心观察是这些类别虽然通常缺乏足够（甚至任何）有限生成的内射对象，但它们是Serre子范畴的递增并集，每个子范畴具有有限多个单对象和足够的有限长度内射对象。该框架应用于两类具体问题：非描述特征下的VI-模（恢复Nagpal关于一般VI-模简单分类的结果）以及无限多个变量上的截断多项式环和外部代数的GL-等变模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征下表示稳定性和等变交换代数中的一般类别难以分析，因为这些类别很少具有足够的（甚至任何）有限生成内射对象，使得标准的同调代数和分类工具失效。需要一种新的方法来处理这类难以直接分析的对象。

Method: 核心观察是：尽管这些类别缺乏有限生成内射，但它们可以被视为Serre子范畴的递增并集，其中每个子范畴具有有限多个单对象和足够的有限长度内射对象。作者将此观察包装成一个广泛适用的公理化框架，该框架允许在子范畴层面进行有限性论证，再通过递增并集传递到整个类别。

Result: 该框架被成功应用于：(1) 非描述特征下VI-模的分析，恢复了Nagpal关于一般VI-模简单对象的分类结果；(2) 无限多个变量上的截断多项式环和外部代数的GL-等变模的同调分析。

Conclusion: 正特征下这些困难类别尽管全局上缺乏足够的有限生成内射，但通过Serre子范畴递增并集的结构分解，仍可在子范畴层面获得足够的有限长度内射，从而为系统性和广泛适用的同调分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generic categories arising in representation stability and equivariant commutative algebra are difficult to analyze in positive characteristic, as these categories rarely admit enough (or even any) finitely generated injectives. Our main observation is that they are nonetheless an increasing union of Serre subcategories, each with finitely many simples and enough finite length injectives. We package this into an axiomatic framework which is widely applicable. In particular, we use this to analyze (1) VI-modules in non-describing characteristic, recovering Nagpal's classification of simple generic VI-modules, and (2) GL-equivariant modules over truncated polynomial rings and exterior algebras in infinitely many variables.

</details>


### [113] [A right pretriangulated category which is not right triangulated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00602)
*Jing He,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入一种适用于右预三角范畴的一般性扩充构造，证明了该构造保持公理(RTR1)-(RTR3)，并利用遗忘函子检测(RTR4)的失效。将该构造应用于Chen等人在A_5型例子中构造的反例，得到一个非右三角范畴的右预三角范畴，其中悬置函子是忠实的但非本质满的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen, Liu, Lu和Zhang最近构造了一个具有可逆悬置但Verdier八面体公理失效的预三角范畴。本文旨在通过一种通用的扩充构造方法，深入分析右预三角范畴中八面体公理失效的结构性原因，并揭示该反例在更广泛构造下的性质。

Method: 作者为右预三角范畴引入了一个一般的"扩充"(enlargement)构造，并证明该构造保持公理(RTR1)-(RTR3)不变。同时，通过在忘却函子下检测(RTR4)的失效来判定扩充后范畴是否为右三角范畴。将该构造应用于Chen等人构造的类型A_5反例，得到具体的非右三角范畴的例子。

Result: 该扩充构造保持(RTR1)-(RTR3)三个公理，但(RTR4)（八面体公理）可能在忘却函子下失效。应用于类型A_5的例子后，构造出一个右预三角范畴，但它不是右三角范畴。在此例子中，悬置函子是忠实的但不是本质满的。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种系统性的构造方法来生成右预三角范畴而非右三角范畴的例子，深化了对八面体公理在同调代数中独立性的理解，并表明即使悬置函子具有忠实性等良好性质，右三角结构的完备性仍需八面体公理来保证。

Abstract: Chen, Liu, Lu, and Zhang recently constructed a pretriangulated category with invertible suspension in which Verdier's octahedral axiom fails. We introduce a general enlargement construction for right pretriangulated categories and show that it preserves axioms (RTR1)-(RTR3), while failure of (RTR4) is detected by the forgetful functor. Applied to their type $A_5$ example, the construction yields a right pretriangulated category that is not right triangulated. In this example, the suspension is faithful but not essentially surjective.

</details>


### [114] [Scary Wheels (and Super Shrubs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00726)
*Andrei Neguţ,Alexander Tsymbaliuk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了类型B、C、D的量子仿射超代数在loop实现中的shuffle实现，构造了这些代数与适当定义的双shuffle代数之间的同构。文中引入了一类推广Feigin-Odesskii轮条件的消失条件，其中新出现了一种被称为"恐怖轮"的涉及7个变量的二阶消失条件。同时全面发展了超类型A（仿射和环面）中shrubs理论，并提出了广义的quiver带参数框架来表述和证明shuffle实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仿射超代数的shuffle实现是理解这些代数结构的重要途径，但此前在类型B、C、D（仅限loop实现）中缺乏相应的证明。此外，需要推广原有的轮条件（wheel conditions）以适用于超代数情形，并发展相关的组合工具（shrubs）来支撑shuffle代数的研究。

Method: 本文在loop实现中构造类型B、C、D的量子仿射超代数与双shuffle代数之间的同构。首先引入推广的Feigin-Odesskii消失条件（包括新出现的"恐怖轮"条件），用以显式描述shuffle代数；其次全面发展超类型A中shrubs理论作为组合工具；最后建立适用于quiver带参数的一般性框架来统一表述和证明shuffle实现，并利用这些技术定义类型B、C、D的量子环面超代数。

Result: 成功证明了类型B、C、D量子仿射超代数的shuffle实现（loop情形），发现了新的"恐怖轮"（涉及7变量的order 2消失条件），完整发展了超类型A的shrubs理论，定义了类型B、C、D的量子环面超代数，并给出了quiver参数化的一般框架。

Conclusion: 本文不仅完成了类型B、C、D量子仿射超代数的shuffle实现证明，还将这一方法推广到更广泛的量子环面超代数及一般quiver参数框架，为shuffle代数与超代数之间联系的研究提供了统一的方法论基础。

Abstract: We prove the shuffle realization for quantum affine superalgebras of types $B,C,D$ in the loop realization, in that we construct an isomorphism between each of these algebras and a suitably defined double shuffle algebra. We explicitly describe the latter shuffle algebra using certain vanishing conditions that generalize the Feigin-Odesskii wheel conditions; a novel feature is the appearance of a so-called scary wheel, which is a particular order $2$ vanishing condition involving $7$ variables. Along the way, we fully develop the theory of shrubs in super types $A$ (affine and toroidal), which are important combinatorial tools in the study of the shuffle algebras associated to toric Calabi-Yau threefolds. Our techniques also allow us to define quantum toroidal superalgebras of types $B,C,D$. More importantly, we provide a general framework to formulate and prove shuffle realizations in the wide generality of quivers with parameters.

</details>


### [115] [Polynomiality for algebras of invariants associated with some Inönü-Wigner contractions in type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01173)
*Florence Fauquant-Millet*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了A型中某些特定的Inönü-Wigner收缩相关的对称不变量代数的多项式性。当抛物子代数$\mathfrak p$的Levi因子（关于反对角线）分解为三个对称块时，证明了该收缩（对应$\mathfrak p$的规范截断）的对称不变量代数是一个多项式代数，并给出了生成元的个数、权和次数。方法基于构造适配对（adapted pair），并由此推广得到在中心块与两个极端块尺寸不同的条件下，对称半不变量生成代数具有Weierstrass截面，因而是多项式代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称不变量代数的多项式性是表示论和不变式理论中的核心问题。Inönü-Wigner收缩在数学物理中广泛出现（如从相对论到非相对论的极限），但对收缩代数的对称不变量代数结构的理解仍然有限。本文旨在通过对A型中特定抛物子代数的Inönü-Wigner收缩构造适配对，从而证明其对称不变量代数为多项式代数，并给出生成元的具体信息。

Method: 作者选取A型中以Levi因子与其幂零根分解方式进行的Inönü-Wigner收缩（幂零根变为收缩代数的交换理想）。针对Levi因子（关于反对角线）分解为三个对称块的情形，考虑$\mathfrak p$的规范截断的收缩，通过为其构造适配对（adapted pair）来证明对称不变量代数的多项式性，并提供代数独立齐次生成元的个数、权和次数。作为推论，利用Weierstrass截面的理论处理对称半不变量生成代数的情形。

Result: 对Levi因子分解为三个对称块的A型抛物子代数的规范截断的收缩，证明了其对称不变量代数是一个多项式代数，并显式给出了代数独立齐次生成元的数量、权重和次数。进一步地，当中心块与两个极端块尺寸不同时，相应Inönü-Wigner收缩的对称半不变量生成代数具有Weierstrass截面，从而也是多项式代数。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造适配对，证实了一类特定的A型Inönü-Wigner收缩（源于Levi因子含三个反对称对称块的抛物子代数）的对称不变量代数具有多项式结构，丰富了收缩背景下Kostant型定理的适用范围，并为更一般情形的对称不变量研究提供了方法基础。

Abstract: This paper deals with polynomiality of algebras of symmetric invariants (or generated by symmetric semi-invariants) associated with some particular Inönü-Wigner contractions in type A. More precisely we are interested with contractions of some parabolic subalgebras in type A with respect to their decomposition into their Levi factor and their nilpotent radical, the latter becoming an abelian ideal of the contraction. When the parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak p$ has its Levi factor decomposing into three symmetric blocks, with respect to the antidiagonal, we show in this article that the algebra of symmetric invariants associated with the contraction of the canonical truncation of $\mathfrak p$ is a polynomial algebra, for which we can give the number of algebraically independent homogeneous generators, their weight and degree. The method relies on the construction of an adapted pair for such a contraction. As a by-product we obtain that the algebra generated by symmetric semi-invariants associated with the Inönü-Wigner contraction of such a parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak p$ in type A has a Weierstrass section and then is a polynomial algebra, when the central block is not of the same size as the two extremal blocks of the Levi factor.

</details>


### [116] [Total positivity in coordinate algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01321)
*G. Lusztig*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文定义了与单laced型Cartan数据相关联的坐标代数的全正部分，并证明了它与相应包络代数正部分典范基底之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全正性与量子群/包络代数结构之间的深层联系，通过坐标代数的全正部分来理解典范基底的几何意义。

Method: 从Cartan数据的坐标代数出发，定义其全正部分（totally positive part），进而通过代数结构分析建立其与包络代数正部分典范基底的关联。

Result: 证明了单laced型Cartan数据对应的坐标代数全正部分与对应包络代数正部分的典范基底之间存在明确的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了坐标代数全正部分与量子群典范基底理论的内在联系，为全正性理论在表示论中的应用提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We define the totally positive part of the coordinate algebra associated to a Cartan datum of simply laced type. We show its relation to the canonical basis of the corresponding plus part of the enveloping algebra.

</details>


### [117] [Diagrammatic Okada monoid and cellularity of the Okada algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01440)
*Florent Hivert,Jeanne Scott*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用带标记的非交叉弧图实现Okada代数O_N(X,Y)及其幺半群O_N，证明其维数为N!（推广到一般参数），建立了置换与弧图间的自然双射作为Young-Fibonacci格的Robinson-Schensted对应的体现，并利用弧图形式化研究Okada幺半群的结构，构造了其细胞基，证明该代数族构成Goodman-Graber意义下的相干细胞代数塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Okada代数作为Young-Fibonacci格的Bratelli图对应的代数族（类似于对称群与Young格的关系），其结构尚未被充分理解。作者希望通过弧图的形式化方法深入探究Okada幺半群和代数的结构，并建立其与Robinson-Schensted对应及细胞代数理论的联系。

Method: 利用Temperley-Lieb代数与Jones幺半群描述中出现的非交叉弧图的一种带标记版本，构造Okada代数和幺半群的显式实现；利用弧图与置换之间的自然双射解释Fomin关于Young-Fibonacci格的Robinson-Schensted对应；通过弧图形式研究幺半群的Green关系与序结构；利用这些结构构造细胞基，并证明该代数族构成相干细胞代数塔。

Result: 证明了对于一般参数(X,Y)，Okada代数O_N(X,Y)的维数为N!（Okada仅在半单情形证明了此结果）；证明了Okada幺半群是正则、无周期的*-幺半群，并描述了其Green关系与序；构造了Okada代数的细胞基；证明了{O_N(X,Y)}_{N≥0}构成Goodman-Graber意义下的相干细胞代数塔；并给出了关于Gram行列式的猜想。

Conclusion: 通过弧图形式化，作者成功深入理解了Okada代数与幺半群的结构，将其纳入细胞代数与相干塔的框架，进一步强化了Okada代数与对称群之间的类比。文中还提出了后续研究计划与开放问题。

Abstract: It is well known that the Young lattice is the Bratelli diagram of the symmetric groups, expressing how irreducible representations restrict from $\mathfrak{S}_{N}$ to $\mathfrak{S}_{N-1}$. In 1975, Stanley discovered a similar lattice called the Young-Fibonacci lattice which was identified as the Bratelli diagram of a family of algebras $\{\mathbf{O}_N(X,Y)\}_{N \geq 0}$ by Okada in 1994.
  In this paper, we first realize the Okada algebra $\mathbf{O}_N(X,Y)$ and the associated monoid $\mathbf{O}_N$ using a labelled version of non-crossing arc-diagrams appearing in the description of the Temperley-Lieb algebra and Jones monoid. We establish, for general parameters $(X,Y)$, that the dimension of the Okada algebra $\mathbf{O}_N(X,Y)$ is $N!$, noting that Okada proved this result only in the semisimple case. We interpret a natural bijection between permutations and labelled arc-diagrams as an incarnation of Fomin's version of the Robinson-Schensted correspondence associated to the Young-Fibonacci lattice. The arc-diagram formalism allow us to probe the structure of the Okada monoid and algebra. In particular we prove that the Okada monoid is a regular, aperiodic $*$-monoid and we describe its Green relations and order.
  These results allow us to construct a cellular basis of the Okada algebra and to show that $\{\mathbf{O}_N(X,Y)\}_{N \geq 0}$ forms a coherent tower of cellular algebras in the sense of Goodman and Graber. We present some conjectures expressing the Gram determinant of the invariant bilinear form attached to each cell module in terms of Okada's clone Schur functions. We conclude the paper by presenting two follow-up, ongoing projects along with a series of questions pushing further the analogy between the symmetric groups and the Okada algebras.

</details>

### [118] [Causal self-consistency of the Blandford--McKee self-similar solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00116)
*Gilad Sadeh,Tamar Faran,Doron Kushnir,Eli Waxman*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析了Blandford-McKee（BM）解在强球面爆炸波后方超相对论自相似流中的有效性问题。研究发现，对于浅层密度剖面（k<kg≈2.062），BM解的特征结构本身就阻止了非BM内部区域的声学信息在超相对论阶段到达激波；对于较陡的剖面（kg<k<4），热状态方程在流仍处于相对论且与激波相连时失效，此时需要推导新的自相似冷却解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM解只适用于激波邻近的热壳层，在足够深的后方流体会离开BM区域。关键疑问是：既然相似剖面仅由激波条件决定，内部（非BM区域）的流动是否会影响靠近激波的热壳层的有效性？这需要研究BM解的特征结构与因果关系。

Method: 通过特征结构分析来确定声学信息能否从内部传播到激波。对于陡峭剖面（kg<k<4），推导了冷却相对论流的新的自相似解，该解具有自身的相似标度、方程和特征结构，与热BM解在激波侧重叠，并在重叠区之外达到声速点。特别地，在k=7/2时获得了冷却解的解析形式。

Result: 对于k<kg≈2.062，BM解的特征结构天然地阻止了内部声学信息到达激波；对于kg<k<4，冷却自相似解在激波侧与热BM解重叠，关键点（声速点）将激波相连的BM加冷却复合体与更深的下游区域分开，使得超出该点产生的声学信号无法向激波传播。

Conclusion: 这种因果结构使得BM解即使在不能全局描述整个下游流动的情况下，仍能在激波附近保持自洽有效。这解释了BM解为何在物理上成立——因为它被因果禁区保护，其有效性由激波附近的局部条件决定。

Abstract: The Blandford--McKee (BM) solution describes the ultra-relativistic self-similar flow behind a strong spherical blast wave propagating into an external density profile $\propto r^{-k}$, where $k<4$ and $r$ is the distance from the center, but it applies only to the hot shell adjacent to the shock, sufficiently deep behind it, the fluid leaves the BM regime. Since the similarity profiles are determined solely by the shock conditions, with no conditions imposed at the inner end, the validity of the solution near the shock depends on whether the flow beyond the BM regime can imprint on this hot shell. For shallow density profiles, $k<k_g\simeq 2.062$, the characteristic structure of the BM solution already prevents forward-going acoustic information from the non-BM interior from reaching the shock during the ultra-relativistic stage. For steeper profiles, $k_g<k<4$, the hot equation of state fails while the flow is still relativistic and shock-connected. There, we derive a new self-similar solution for the cooling relativistic flow, which has its own similarity scale, equations, and characteristic structure. It overlaps with the hot BM solution toward the shock, and reaches a sonic point beyond this overlap. This critical point separates the shock-connected BM-plus-cooling composite from the deeper downstream region, so acoustic signals generated beyond it cannot propagate toward the shock. The mechanism is explicit at $k=7/2$, for which the cooling solution is obtained analytically. This causal structure is therefore what enables the BM solution, which remains self-consistent near the shock even though it does not globally describe the full downstream flow.

</details>


### [119] [Redshift Identifiability from Gamma-Ray Burst Prompt Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00166)
*Shu-Xu Yi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究在现实结构化喷流场景下，伽马射线暴（GRB）的哪些瞬时辐射观测量包含可识别的红移信息。通过光子级的洛伦兹变换和宇宙学变换分析，发现反映主导斑块辐射的光谱观测量、反映共动系物理的时间观测量以及绝对流量/计数率观测量都受z-θ_v简并性影响；但中心引擎印记的时间尺度和主导斑块之外的辐射这两类观测量具有打破该简并性的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB红移测量通常依赖宿主星系光谱，但并非所有暴都有光谱红移。若能从GRB自身瞬时辐射的观测量推断红移，将极大地扩展可用的宇宙学探针。然而在结构化喷流场景下，红移z与观测角度θ_v之间存在简并性，需要系统考察哪些观测量类别能够打破这种简并，从而为寻找红移指示器提供理论指导。

Method: 本文通过对光子层面的洛伦兹变换与宇宙学变换进行解析推导，系统分类并检验五类观测量的红移信息含量：(1)反映主导斑块发射的光谱观测量；(2)反映喷流共动系物理的时间观测量；(3)反映绝对流量或计数率的观测量；(4)中心引擎印记的时间尺度；(5)来自主导斑块之外的辐射。逐类分析其是否受z-θ_v简并性影响及可否打破简并。考虑固有亮度的巨大变化，检验观测量组合能否消除简并。

Result: 研究发现前三类观测量（主导斑块光谱观测量、共动系时间观测量、绝对流量/计数率观测量）均受z-θ_v简并性影响——前两类以完全相同的形式简并，第三类以不同形式简并。即使将这三类观测量组合使用，也无法打破简并，原因在于固有亮度的巨大内在弥散。然而，中心引擎印记的时间尺度（如活动时间、特定脉冲时间结构）和来自主导斑块之外的辐射这两类观测量，具有打破z-θ_v简并性的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作系统厘清了GRB瞬时辐射各类观测量与红移的关系，明确了传统观测量无法单独或组合提供可靠红移信息，并指明了未来可能用于红移估计的两类有前景的观测量方向——中心引擎唤醒时间尺度和主导斑块外辐射。这为利用GRB自身观测量估计红移提供了重要的理论约束和指引。

Abstract: We examine which prompt-emission observables of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) carry identifiable redshift information in realistic structured-jet scenarios. By examining the photon-level Lorentz and cosmological transformations, we demonstrate any spectral observable reflecting the dominant-patch emission and any temporal observable reflecting the co-moving frame physics in the jet suffers from the same $z$-$θ_v$ degeneracy. The observable reflecting the absolute flux or count rate will also suffer from this $z$-$θ_v$ degeneracy in a different form. Due to the large variation of the intrinsic luminosity, combining the observables in the above-mentioned three classes cannot break this degeneracy. However, we demonstrate that two other classes of observables contain the potential to break this degeneracy: (i)~central engine-imprinted time scales, (ii)~emission from out of the dominant patch.

</details>


### [120] [A NICER view of PSR J1614$-$2230: a massive and compact millisecond pulsar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00172)
*Lucien Mauviard,Sebastien Guillot,Lami Suleiman,Yves Kini,Denis González-Caniulef,Christine Kazantsev,Pierre Stammler,Devarshi Choudhury,Bas Dorsman,Mariska Hoogkamer,Daniela Huppenkothen,Tuomo Salmi,Anna L. Watts,Jérôme Novak,Natalie A. Webb,Jean-Francois Olive,Matthew Kerr,Lucas Guillemot,Ismaël Cognard,Gilles Theureau*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用脉冲轮廓建模，基于NICER、XMM-Newton和Chandra的数据，首次测量了毫秒脉冲星PSR J1614-2230的质量-半径关系，得出赤道半径约10.06 km、引力质量约1.937太阳质量的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星的质量-半径测量是约束致密物质状态方程（即中子星内部组成）的关键手段。PSR J1614-2230是射电计时确认的第二大质量毫秒脉冲星，对其进行精确的X射线脉冲轮廓建模可以为极端密度下的核物质性质提供重要约束。

Method: 作者使用来自三个X射线望远镜（NICER、XMM-Newton和Chandra）的数据，通过脉冲轮廓建模技术进行分析。模型将表面辐射描述为两个圆形热斑，并结合射电计时的质量先验约束，同时利用针对该脉冲星自转频率定制的紧致度-扁率/表面重力新关系来推断几何构型。此外还进行了相位平均和相位分辨光谱分析。

Result: 模型成功描述了三组数据，得到赤道半径R_eq=10.06(+1.25/-0.87) km，引力质量M=1.937(+0.012/-0.013)太阳质量（68%可信区间）。未在相位平均或相位分辨光谱中发现此前报道的高能非热成分。几何推断得出一个热斑靠近极区、另一个靠近赤道的构型。质量后验主要由射电计时决定，半径约束受限于低信噪信号（8.5σ X射线脉冲显著性）。在所有几何和大气模型下，半径后验均倾向于低值（所有情况下第90百分位不超过12.08 km）。

Conclusion: PSR J1614-2230的半径约束系统地指向较低值（≲12 km），这一结果对致密物质状态方程具有重要排除意义，可能限制软的状态方程或排除较刚硬的状态方程。同时，该工作展示了多波段X射线联合脉冲轮廓建模与大质量毫秒脉冲星研究的潜力。

Abstract: Using pulse profile modeling, we obtain the mass-radius measurement of a millisecond pulsar (MSP) with data from the Neutron Star Interior Composition ExploreR, XMM-Newton and the Chandra X-ray Observatory. We report here the radius of PSR J1614$-$2230, the second most massive MSP confirmed by radio timing. All of the data sets are well described by a simple model composed of two circular hot spots. The final result yields an equatorial radius of $R_{\rm eq}=10.06 ^{+1.25}_{-0.87}\,$km, and a gravitational mass of $M=1.937^{+0.012}_{-0.013}\,M_{\odot}$ (equally tailed 68% credible intervals). Although a non-thermal component was previously reported at higher energies, we find no sign of it in either our phase-averaged or phase-resolved spectral analyses. Using new relations linking the compactness to oblateness or surface gravity, and tailored to the spin frequency of PSR J1614$-$2230, we infer a configuration with one hot spot near the pole, and another near the equator. The tight mass posterior is essentially informed by radio timing, while the radius constraint is not as tight due to the low source signal (8.5$σ$ X-ray pulse significance). However, over all geometries and atmosphere models tested, the radius posterior tends toward low values ($\lesssim12.08\,$km, 90th percentile in all cases).

</details>


### [121] [Equation of state and neutron star properties with new mass-radius constraints from PSR~J1614--2230, PSR~J2124--3358, and 47~Tuc~X7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00173)
*Melissa Mendes,Isak Svensson,Hannah Gottling,Kai Hebeler,Achim Schwenk,Nathan Rutherford,Lucien Mauviard,Christine Kazantsev,Yves Kini,Denis Gonzalez-Caniulef,Sebastien Guillot,Anna Watts*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了来自PSR J1614-2230、PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7的新质量-半径信息对中子星状态方程（EoS）推定的影响。研究结合了手征有效场论在核密度下的约束以及不同的高密度扩展（包括微扰QCD约束），结果表明：重质量的PSR J1614-2230影响最大，使重中子星半径缩小约0.4 km；结合NICER、LIGO/Virgo和X射线信息后，半径后验分布更具数据驱动性，对高密度扩展的敏感性降低；新的质量-半径信息显著收紧了约三倍饱和密度处的压力和声速后验分布；最后对PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7的质量进行了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过整合最新的中子星质量-半径观测数据（包括PSR J1614-2230、PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7）与手征有效场论及微扰QCD约束，进行更精确的联合状态方程推断，以改善冷致密核物质性质（如压力、声速）的约束精度。

Method: 采用基于手征有效场论（chiral EFT）在核密度区域的约束，并结合多种高密度扩展方案（含微扰QCD约束），构建贝叶斯联合推断框架。将多个天体的质量-半径测量数据（PSR J1614-2230、PSR J2124-3358、47 Tuc X7，以及NICER、LIGO/Virgo、X射线源）纳入统一的EoS参数化推断，分析其后验分布变化。

Result: PSR J1614-2230的重质量约束是主要驱动因素，使重中子星的半径后验缩小约0.4 km。综合多种天体物理数据后，半径后验分布更偏向数据驱动，对高密度扩展模型的依赖减弱。新数据显著收紧了三倍饱和密度附近的状态方程压力与声速后验分布，并给出了PSR J2124-3358与47 Tuc X7质量的预测值。

Conclusion: 新增的中子星质量-半径观测数据能有效提高状态方程推断的精度，尤其是缩小了高密度区域的不确定性范围，并使得结果对理论模型（高密度扩展）的依赖有所降低，验证了结合多源天体物理数据与微观核物理约束在EoS推断中的价值。

Abstract: We study the impact of new mass-radius information from PSR J1614-2230, PSR J2124-3358, and 47 Tuc X7 in a combined equation of state inference based on chiral effective field theory constraints at nuclear densities and using different high-density extensions, including perturbative QCD constraints. The largest impact stems from the heavy-mass PSR J1614-2230 star, which shifts heavy neutron stars to smaller radii by around 0.4 km. Moreover, the combined astrophysical NICER, LIGO/Virgo, and X-ray information drives the radius posterior to a more data-driven distribution, which is less sensitive to the high-density extension. For the equation of state, the new mass-radius information significantly tightens the pressure and speed-of-sound posterior distributions, especially around three times saturation density. Finally, we make predictions for the poorly constrained masses of PSR J2124-3358 and 47 Tuc X7 based on the combined equation of state analysis and the other astrophysical sources.

</details>


### [122] [GRMHD Simulations of Accreting Proto-Magnetars I. Implications for Gamma-Ray Burst Jets and Energetic Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00174)
*Tejas Prasanna,Kyle Parfrey,Brian D. Metzger,Ore Gottlieb,Andrei Beloborodov,Koushik Chatterjee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过轴对称GRMHD模拟研究了周围存在吸积盘的快速旋转强磁化中子星（毫秒级原磁星）的吸积动力学。研究发现，吸积会压缩磁层并打开额外磁通，产生比孤立偶极自转减速估计高出数倍到约10倍的相对论性喷流功率。在通道化吸积模型中表现出强烈的喷流变率（由等离子体团喷发和间歇性磁层吸积驱动），而螺旋桨模型则产生更稳定、更强大的喷流和快速自转减速。盘-磁层相互作用调控着中子星的增质效率和自转演化，接近自转平衡时低效吸积可延迟坍缩为黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速旋转的新生强磁化中子星（毫秒级原磁星）被认为是伽马射线暴和明亮超新星的重要中心引擎，但以往模型通常孤立处理这些天体，忽视了它们在恒星坍缩、中子星并合或吸积致坍缩中可能周围环绕着吸积盘的事实。理解盘-磁层相互作用对这些天体的喷流功率、吸积效率和演化命运至关重要。

Method: 采用轴对称广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，研究对原磁星的超吸积过程。模拟包含物理状态方程和带电电流弱相互作用。固定弱磁化的吸积环，改变恒星偶极磁场强度以涵盖压碎磁层、磁通道化吸积和离心螺旋桨三种状态，并与相似条件下的吸积黑洞进行比较。

Result: 吸积压缩恒星磁层并打开额外磁通，产生超过孤立偶极自转减速估计值数倍到约10倍的相对论性喷流功率。更强磁场在磁层仍被压缩时反而愈发阻碍吸积并增强外流。通道化吸积模型呈现由等离子体团喷发驱动的强烈喷流变率，螺旋桨模型则产生更稳定且更强大的喷流和快速自转减速。盘-磁层相互作用调控中子星增质效率和自转方向，近自转平衡时低效吸积可延迟黑洞坍缩。

Conclusion: 吸积的原磁星能够提供与长伽马射线暴及伽马射线暴-超新星推断能量相当的相对论性喷流和重子丰富外流。这意味着在建模伽马射线暴中心引擎时，必须考虑周围吸积盘与磁层的相互作用而不能仅做孤立旋转模型的简化处理。姊妹论文进一步探讨了对富中子抛射物和r过程核合成的意义。

Abstract: Newly formed, rapidly rotating, strongly magnetized neutron stars ("millisecond proto-magnetars") are promising central engines for gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) and luminous supernovae. Although often modeled in isolation, they can be born surrounded by accretion disks in stellar collapse, neutron-star mergers, or accretion-induced collapse. We present axisymmetric GRMHD simulations of hyperaccretion onto such objects, including a physical equation of state and charged-current weak interactions. Holding the weakly magnetized accretion torus fixed, we vary the stellar dipole field strength to span crushed-magnetosphere, magnetically channeled accretion, and centrifugal-propeller regimes, and compare with an otherwise similar accreting black hole.
  Accretion compresses the stellar magnetosphere and opens additional magnetic flux, producing relativistic jet powers that exceed isolated-dipole spin-down estimates by factors of a few to ~10. Even while the magnetosphere remains compressed against the stellar surface, stronger fields increasingly impede accretion and enhance outflows. Channeled-accretion models show strong jet variability driven by plasmoid eruptions and intermittent magnetospheric accretion, whereas the propeller model produces a steadier, more powerful jet and rapid spin-down. The disk-magnetosphere interaction also regulates how efficiently the neutron star grows and whether it spins up or down; near spin equilibrium, inefficient accretion can delay collapse to a black hole relative to estimates based on the external mass-supply rate. Accreting proto-magnetars can therefore power relativistic jets and baryon-rich outflows with energetics comparable to those inferred for long GRBs and GRB-supernovae. A companion paper explores implications for neutron-rich ejecta and r-process nucleosynthesis.

</details>


### [123] [Evidence for a luminosity-class dichotomy in the supergiant donors of persistent high-mass X-ray binaries and supergiant fast X-ray transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00175)
*Lara Sidoli,Vito Sguera*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对文献的系统收集，比较了两类高能X射线双星（超巨星快速X射线暂现源SFXTs与经典X射线源）中超巨星伴星的恒星分类，发现SFXTs中最极端暂现源仅包含O型或Ib/Iab光度级的B型超巨星，而经典源主要是Ia光度级伴星，表明恒星光度级与恒星风性质之间存在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献提出SFXTs中O型超巨星伴星多于B型，可能表明SFXTs的恒星风比经典持续源更快。作者旨在验证恒星光度级是否存在二分性，因为B型超巨星也存在于SFXTs中。

Method: 收集可用文献中所有两类高能X射线双星（SFXTs和经典X射线源）的信息，构建具有可靠恒星分类的稳健样本，并对伴星的恒星光谱型（O型/B型）和光度级（Ib、Iab、Ia）进行系统比对分析。

Result: 发现展示最极端暂现X射线发射的SFXTs宿主仅为O型或Ib/Iab光度级的B型超巨星，而经典源大多数为Ia光度级伴星。恒星光度级与恒星风性质（终端速度、质量损失率和风强度Q参数）相关，表明SFXTs中恒星风更快且密度更低。

Conclusion: 恒星光度级的差异是解释两类高能X射线双星在X射线波段不同行为的稳健证据，SFXTs与经典源的区分可能源于超巨星伴星光度级所决定的恒星风物理性质差异。

Abstract: We have investigated the properties of the supergiant donors in two types of high-mass X-ray binaries (HMXBs, i.e. the so-called supergiant fast X-ray transients (SFXTs) and the classical X-ray sources), by collecting all available information from the literature. A prevalence of O-type versus B-type supergiant donors in SFXTs was previously suggested in the literature, possibly indicative of faster winds in SFXTs than in classical, persistent sources. Our aim is to investigate whether there is evidence for a dichotomy in the stellar luminosity class, since B-type supergiants are also known to be hosted in SFXTs. Our collection of the relevant literature allowed us to build a robust sample of both types of HMXBs with a secure stellar classification. We have found that the SFXTs showing the most extreme, transient X-ray emission host only O-type or B-type stars with a Ib and Iab luminosity class, whereas classical sources have mostly Ia donors. This is robust evidence that might explain the two different behaviours of HMXBs at X-rays since the stellar luminosity class appears to be linked to the wind properties (the supergiant wind terminal velocity, the mass-loss rate, and the wind strength Q parameter), indicative of faster and less dense winds in SFXTs than in classical sources.

</details>


### [124] [Portraits of the young and old in X-ray: New millisecond pulsar detections and updated characterisation of young neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00176)
*Wynn C. G. Ho,Patrick H. Wang,Sebastien Guillot*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于新的Chandra和NuSTAR观测数据以及档案数据，对四颗中子星天体进行了分析：两颗老毫秒脉冲星（PSR J1810-0623和PSR J1933-6211）均为首次在X射线波段探测到；一颗年轻的极高自转能耗脉冲星PSR J1813-1749测量出其44.7毫秒的自转周期并首次展示3-79 keV的脉冲轮廓；以及超新星遗迹G18.95-1.1中的年轻中子星CXOU J182913.1-125113，其谱通过2009与2024年的观测数据进行精细测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对不同年龄和演化阶段的中子星/脉冲星进行多波段X射线观测分析，以推进对毫秒脉冲星X射线辐射性质、年轻高能耗脉冲星自转演化与脉冲风星云能谱变化、以及超新星遗迹内中子星谱特性的理解。

Method: 利用新的Chandra HRC-I/ACIS-S观测和档案Chandra HRC-S/ACIS-I数据，结合NuSTAR（2025年）数据，对四颗目标天体进行X射线探测、源定位、光变与谱分析，测量脉冲星自转周期，构建脉冲轮廓并更新自转演化模型，同时对脉冲星与脉冲风星云的能谱进行时间比对。

Result: PSR J1810-0623首次在X射线波段的试探性探测表明其距离更接近色散测量推断的~1 kpc（较远值）；PSR J1933-6211首次X射线探测，其光度与1.9倍太阳质量脉冲星PSR J1614-2230相似；PSR J1813-1749测得44.7 ms自转周期并首次展示3-79 keV的脉冲轮廓，2018至2025年间脉冲星与脉冲风星云能谱无明显变化；CXOU J182913.1-125113谱通过联合2009和2024年ACIS-I数据得到改进测量。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了毫秒脉冲星X射线探测样本，为PSR J1810-0623的距离约束提供了新依据；对PSR J1813-1749的高能脉冲轮廓与谱稳定性的展示加深了对年轻高自转能耗脉冲星的认知；并通过多年档案数据联合分析提升了年轻中子星谱参数的测量精度。

Abstract: We present analyses of new and archival Chandra and NuSTAR data on two old millisecond pulsars, a young highly-energetic pulsar, and a young neutron star. PSR J1810-0623 is a recently-discovered 4.55 ms radio pulsar in a binary with a possible carbon-oxygen white dwarf companion. We tentatively detect the pulsar for the first time in X-ray using a new short Chandra HRC-I observation. Its faintness suggests a distance more in accord with the further (~1 kpc) of its dispersion measure-inferred distances. PSR J1933-6211 is a 3.54 ms radio pulsar also with a carbon-oxygen white dwarf companion. Our first-time X-ray detection in an archival HRC-S image indicates its X-ray luminosity is similar to that of the 1.9 Msun pulsar PSR J1614-2230. Next, PSR J1813-1749 is a 1-2 kyr pulsar with one of the highest known spin-down luminosities. We measure its spin period of 44.7 ms using 2025 NuSTAR and 2026 Chandra ACIS-S data and update its spin evolution model. We show for the first time the pulse profile of PSR J1813-1749 at 3-79 keV, and we find that the spectra of the pulsar and pulsar wind nebula at these energies did not change significantly between observations in 2018 and 2025. Finally, for the 5-10 kyr neutron star CXOU J182913.1-125113 in the supernova remnant G18.95-1.1, we combine ACIS-I observations from 2009 and 2024 to better measure the neutron star's spectrum.

</details>


### [125] [GRMHD Simulations of Accreting Proto-Magnetars II. Implications for r-process Nucleosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00190)
*Tejas Prasanna,Kyle Parfrey,Brian D. Metzger,Ore Gottlieb,Andrei Beloborodov,Koushik Chatterjee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过GRMHD模拟研究吸积到毫秒级原生磁星上的圆盘-磁层相互作用如何调控重子抛射物质的质量、组成和速度，发现磁化中子星相比黑洞吸积会定性改变抛射物的量和成分，磁驱动的外流可以合成完整范围的r过程核素，但在适度中微子辐照下第三峰产量会降低，磁加速的中子星外流比黑洞盘风更能保留重元素成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解毫秒级原生磁星周围致密吸积盘中圆盘-磁层相互作用如何调控重子富集的抛射物特性，从而评估这类系统作为重r过程元素合成源的潜力，并比较其与黑洞吸积的区别。

Method: 使用轴对称广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，模拟吸积到毫秒级原生磁星的过程，分析磁层压缩、磁通道化和离心加速对抛射物质质量/组成/速度的影响，并结合反应网络计算中微子辐照下的r过程核素合成。

Result: 更强的中子星磁场能抑制吸积并将更多流入物质重定向为未束缚外流；磁驱动外流可合成包括最重元素在内的完整r过程核素范围；中度中微子辐照显著降低第三峰产量，但磁加速的中子星外流比黑洞盘风更能保留重成分；当原生磁星的中微子发射足够衰减后，可作为重要的重r过程核素来源。

Conclusion: 吸积原生磁星可能成为重要的重r过程核素产生源，前提是其早期强中微子发射已充分衰减；磁化中子星与黑洞吸积在抛射物的量和成分上存在定性差异，磁层-圆盘相互作用在调控重元素合成方面起关键作用。

Abstract: Newly formed, rapidly spinning, strongly magnetized neutron stars ("millisecond proto-magnetars") can arise in collapsars, neutron star mergers, or white-dwarf accretion-induced collapse, and are often surrounded by compact accretion disks. At accretion rates of ~0.1 Msun/s, these disks can become neutron rich and power outflows capable of rapid neutron-capture (r-process) nucleosynthesis. In Paper I, we presented axisymmetric GRMHD simulations of accretion onto such proto-magnetars and showed how the disk-magnetosphere interaction regulates jet power, variability, and neutron star torques. Here we use the same simulations to study how this interaction regulates the mass, composition, and velocity of the baryon-rich ejecta, comparing proto-magnetar models to otherwise similar black hole accretion.
  A magnetized neutron star qualitatively changes both the amount and composition of the ejecta. Stronger neutron star magnetic fields suppress accretion and redirect more inflowing material into unbound outflows, even when the magnetosphere remains strongly compressed by the disk. Once the field produces magnetic channeling or centrifugal acceleration, mass loss is enhanced further. Reaction-network calculations show that these magnetically driven outflows can synthesize the full range of r-process nuclei, including the heaviest elements. Moderate neutrino irradiation substantially reduces the third-peak yield, but magnetically accelerated neutron star outflows retain a heavy component more effectively than black hole disk winds; sufficiently strong early-time irradiation suppresses it altogether. Accreting proto-magnetars may therefore be important heavy r-process sources once their neutrino emission has sufficiently declined.

</details>


### [126] [Nanohertz Gravitational-Wave Constraints on Supermassive Binary Black Holes at Cosmic Dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00613)
*Yiqin Chen,Shi-Yi Zhao,Xingjiang Zhu,N. D. Ramesh Bhat,Jacob Cardinal Tremblay,Małgorzata Curyło,Valentina Di Marco,George Hobbs,Wenhua Ling,Rami F. Mandow,Richard N. Manchester,Saurav Mishra,Daniel J. Reardon,Sparrow Roch,Christopher J. Russell,Ryan M. Shannon,Jingbo Wang,Shuangqiang Wang,Andrew Zic*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑宇宙学红移的脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）引力波搜索框架，并将其应用于Parkes PTA第三次数据发布（DR3），获得了宇宙黎明时期超大质量双黑洞（SMBBHs）的首个直接约束。研究在广泛红移范围内评估限制，观测上建立了由"红移偏置"理论驱动的非单调质量-红移排除边界，并对高红移目标（包括z=6.327的类星体J0100+2802和z=14.32的JWST发现星系JADES-GS-z14-0）进行了定向搜索，排除了啁啾质量≥10^10太阳质量以上的SMBBHs，同时证明了高红移双星系统的可探测性与定位能力，为探索早期宇宙黑洞并合提供了迄今为止z>6最严格的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PTA连续引力波搜索忽略宇宙学红移效应，将适用范围限制在近邻宇宙，无法有效探测极早期宇宙中的超大质量双黑洞。本文旨在突破这一局限，引入红移感知的PTA框架，从而能够利用现有脉冲星计时数据直接探索宇宙黎明时期及更早期的黑洞并合历史。

Method: 引入包含宇宙学红移的PTA信号模型框架，使引力波频率和振幅的推算正确考虑红移拉伸效应；将该框架应用于Parkes PTA DR3数据集，在宽红移范围内建立质量-红移排除边界；对高红移目标系统（类星体J0100+2802和JWST星系JADES-GS-z14-0）执行红移感知的定向连续波搜索，并对高红移双星的可探测性与参数测量能力进行评估。

Result: 观测上确立了非单调的质量-红移排除边界，证实了理论预测的"红移偏置"效应；对J0100+2802和JADES-GS-z14-0的定向搜索以严格的置信度排除了啁啾质量≳10^10 M⊙的SMBBHs在整个纳赫兹频带的存在；展示了高红移双星可以被稳健探测和定位，并能准确测量其内禀参数。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了目前对红移z>6处SMBBHs最严格的直接观测约束，证明了PTA作为宇宙学探针的有效性——不仅能探测邻近宇宙，还能深入探测到宇宙极早期的黑洞形成与并合过程，为研究早期宇宙黑洞聚集提供了实用的理论观测框架。

Abstract: Standard continuous gravitational-wave searches with pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) neglect cosmological redshift, restricting their applicability to the local Universe. We introduce a redshift-aware PTA framework and apply it to the Parkes PTA Data Release 3, deriving the first direct constraints on supermassive binary black holes (SMBBHs) at the cosmic dawn. Evaluating our limits across a broad redshift range, we observationally establish the non-monotonic mass-redshift exclusion boundary driven by the theoretical ``redshift bias", demonstrating how PTAs can effectively probe sources at extreme distances. Redshift-aware targeted searches toward high-redshift systems, including the ultraluminous quasar J0100+2802 ($z=6.327$) and the JWST-discovered galaxy JADES-GS-z14-0 ($z=14.32$), rigorously exclude SMBBHs with chirp masses $\mathcal{M}_c \gtrsim 10^{10} M_\odot$ across the nanohertz frequency band. Finally, we demonstrate that high-redshift binaries can be robustly detected and localized, allowing for the accurate measurement of their intrinsic properties. Our results provide the tightest constraints to date on SMBBHs at $z > 6$ and establish a practical framework for probing early-Universe black hole assembly.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究利用时间相关似然分析方法，对89个具有光学/紫外和X射线观测的潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）进行了中微子对应搜索，包括单源搜索和喷流/非喷流TDE亚群的堆叠搜索，未发现显著的中微子超出，并对TDE对弥散天体物理中微子通量的贡献设置了上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐瓦解事件理论上可通过质子与吸积盘及外流中多波段光子的光强子相互作用产生高能中微子，探测到这些中微子将有助于揭示TDE的物理动力学过程。鉴于广视场望远镜观测到TDE数量近期显著增加，作者开展了针对性的中微子搜索研究。

Method: 该研究选取了89个基于X射线和光学/紫外观测的TDE样本，采用时间相关似然分析方法分两部分进行搜索：第一部分对单个源搜索，拟合预期中微子发射的时间窗口；第二部分将喷流和非喷流TDE划分为亚群，采用固定一年时间窗口的堆叠搜索。

Result: 两类搜索均未观测到显著的中微子超出。在假设TDE为标准烛光的前提下，作者对在不同波段（光学/紫外和X射线）探测到的喷流和非喷流TDE对弥散天体物理中微子通量的贡献设置了上限。

Conclusion: 该研究排除了所选取的TDE样本产生显著中微子发射的可能性，并为TDE对弥散中微子天体物理通量的贡献提供了观测约束，有助于理解TDE中高能粒子加速和宇宙线产生机制。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) are theorized to produce high-energy neutrinos through photohadronic interactions between accelerated protons and multi-wavelength photons in the accretion disk and outflows. Detecting these neutrinos would provide insight into the dynamics of TDEs. Taking advantage of the recent increase in observed TDEs from wide field-of-view telescopes, we conduct a dedicated search for neutrinos coincident in optical/UV and X-ray wavelengths. We searched for neutrino emission from 89 TDEs selected based on X-ray and optical/UV observations using time-dependent likelihood analysis methods in two parts. First, we searched for emission from individual sources, where we fit the time window of expected neutrino emission. Second, we performed a study of jetted and non-jetted TDE subpopulations using a stacking search with a fixed one year time window. No significant neutrino excess was observed in either search. We set upper limits to the contribution of jetted and non-jetted TDEs detected in optical/UV and X-ray wavelengths to the diffuse astrophysical neutrino flux assuming TDEs are standard candles.

</details>


### [128] [X-ray Flares in Gamma-Ray Bursts at High and Very-High-Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00684)
*Pawan Tiwari,Biswajit Banerjee,Gor Oganesyan,Ansh Chopra,Annarita Ierardi,Marica Branchesi,Stefano Ascenzi,Maria Edvige Ravasio*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对Swift X射线望远镜17年间观测到的47个GRB的66个X射线耀发进行了系统性的多波段研究，利用Fermi-LAT数据寻找GeV对应体，发现仅5个耀发有显著高能辐射。通过同步自康普顿模型约束了辐射区的物理条件（磁场、洛伦兹因子、辐射半径），发现最严格的GeV上限意味着磁化发射区（磁-电子光度比≥1），并评估了X射线耀发在CTA等切伦科夫望远镜下的甚高能可探测性，发现晚期耀发更具观测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB早期余辉中的快速明亮X射线耀发（Swift XRT发现）具有快速时变性和谱演化特征，暗示其起源于内激波，但其物理起源和辐射机制仍存在争议。高能(>100 MeV)和甚高能(>30 GeV)伽马射线观测可为耀发耗散区提供强有力约束（尺寸、磁场强度、粒子加速等），因此需要对大量X射线耀发进行系统性的高能波段研究以阐明其物理本质。

Method: 作者选取Swift XRT 17年观测到的47个GRB中的66个X射线耀发（均在Fermi-LAT视场内），进行系统性多波段研究：1) 搜索GeV能段对应的耀发辐射，使用>3σ显著性判据；2) 对有GeV辐射的个体进行宽带谱建模，检验其是否符合标准前向激波余辉；3) 研究耀发光谱性质与1 keV、10 keV、1 GeV能量通量的相关性；4) 采用同步自康普顿(SSC)模型约束发射区的磁场强度、体洛伦兹因子和发射半径；5) 对早期(~500s)和晚期(~5000s)的X射线耀发预测甚高能伽马射线辐射，并评估其在切伦科夫望远镜（如CTAO）下的可探测性。

Result: 研究发现：66个X射线耀发中仅5个表现出显著的(>3σ)高能(>100 MeV)电磁辐射，且其GeV发射与标准前向激波余辉一致，而非耀发自身辐射；SSC模型约束表明，最严格的GeV上限对应磁-电子光度比≥1，意味着发射区高度磁化；甚高能探测性评估显示，晚期(~5000s)的X射线耀发因观测可达性、灵敏度和更宽松的响应时间要求，是CTAO等设施最有前景的后续观测目标。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，大多数X射线耀发在GeV波段没有显著的伴随辐射，少数探测到的GeV发射可归因于标准前向激波余辉而非耀发的内激波辐射。SSC模型约束强烈暗示X射线耀发发射区处于高度磁化状态。未来的甚高能伽马射线观测（尤其是针对晚期耀发的CTAO后续观测）将为揭示耀发的辐射机制和物理起源提供关键诊断。

Abstract: A significant fraction of GRB early afterglows exhibit fast and bright X-ray flares, discovered by Swift X-ray Telescope. Their rapid temporal variability and spectral evolution have suggested an internal shock origin, analogous to prompt MeV emission. However, their physical origin and radiation mechanism remain debated. High-energy (> 100 MeV) and very-high-energy (> 30 GeV) gamma-ray observations provide a powerful probe of flare dissipation region, constraining its size, magnetic field strength, and particle acceleration under the synchrotron self-Compton scenario in optically thin relativistic jets. We present a systematic multi-wavelength study of 66 X-ray flares from 47 GRBs observed by X-ray Telescope over 17 years, all within field of view of Fermi Large Area Telescope. We investigate their GeV counterparts and find that only five flares exhibit significant high-energy emission (> 3 sigma). Broadband spectral modeling indicates that this GeV emission is consistent with standard forward shock afterglow. We further investigate correlations between flare spectral properties and energy fluxes at 1 keV, 10 keV, and 1 GeV. Using a synchrotron self-Compton model, we constrain the physical conditions of the emitting region, including magnetic field strength, bulk Lorentz factor, and emission radius. For the most stringent GeV upper limits, we find a magnetic-to-electron luminosity ratio greater than or equal to 1, implying a highly magnetized emitting region. We predict very-high-energy gamma-ray emission from X-ray flares at early (~500s) and late (~5000s) times and assess their detectability with Cherenkov Telescopes. We find that later X-ray flares provide the most promising targets for follow-up observations owing to improved observational accessibility, sensitivity, and reduced response-time constraints of facilities such as Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory.

</details>


### [129] [Connecting radio pulsars, magnetars, and XDINSs in a unified evolutionary framework using simulation-based inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00962)
*Michele Ronchi,Celsa Pardo-Araujo,Vanessa Graber,Nanda Rea,Clara Dehman,Davide De Grandis,Alessio Marino,Francesco Coti Zelati,Joeri van Leeuwen,José A. Pons*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个统一的孤立中子星群体合成框架，结合动力学、旋转、磁热演化及射电/X射线发射模型与观测选择效应，并利用截断序贯神经后验估计（SNPE）方法重建中子星的诞生性质。发现银河系中子星诞生磁场服从双分量对数正态分布（峰值分别在5×10^12 G和10^14 G），诞生率约为每世纪3-4颗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一框架下理解银河系孤立中子星群体，对于揭示其诞生性质、演化路径以及不同中子星类别（射电脉冲星、磁星、XDINSs）之间的联系至关重要，但目前缺乏一个能够同时再现射电和X射线观测的综合性群体合成模型。

Method: 构建了一个全面的群体合成框架，涵盖中子星诞生性质、动力学/旋转/磁热演化、射电与X射线发射及相应巡天的选择效应。为模拟真实X射线谱，考虑了磁层共振逆康普顿散射与星际吸收；为建模磁星暴发引起的观测偏差，将暴发率与星壳磁应力关联。最后采用基于模拟的推断方法——截断序贯神经后验估计（truncated SNPE）——来重建如初始磁场分布等诞生性质。

Result: 发现银河系中子星群体可由双分量对数正态分布描述，诞生磁场峰值分别位于5×10^12 G和10^14 G；银河系中子星诞生率约为每世纪3-4颗。

Conclusion: 该结果有助于确立中子星作为快速射电暴、超亮超新星及伽马射线暴等强天体暂现源中心引擎的贡献。

Abstract: Understanding the Galactic population of isolated neutron stars within a unified framework provides key insights into their birth properties, evolutionary pathways, and the connections between different neutron star classes. In this work, we aim to reproduce both the observed radio and quiescent X-ray emission from isolated neutron stars, including radio pulsars, magnetars and X-ray dim isolated neutron stars (XDINSs). We develop a comprehensive population synthesis framework that models neutron star birth properties, their dynamical, rotational, and magneto-thermal evolution, as well as radio and X-ray emission, and the selection effects of corresponding surveys. To simulate realistic X-ray spectra, we account for magnetospheric resonant cyclotron scattering and interstellar absorption. Additionally, we model the observational bias introduced by magnetar outbursts, by linking the outburst rate to magnetic stresses in the stellar crust. We then employ a simulation-based inference method, namely truncated sequential neural posterior estimation, to reconstruct the birth properties, such as the initial magnetic field distribution. We find that the Galactic neutron star population can be described by a two-component log-normal distribution of birth magnetic fields with peaks at $5 \times 10^{12}$ G and $10^{14}$ G, respectively. We further find that the Galactic neutron star birth rate is around 3-4 per century. Our results help establish the contribution of neutron stars as central engines of powerful astrophysical transient phenomena, such as fast radio bursts, super-luminous supernovae and gamma ray bursts.

</details>


### [130] [The Radius of the Neutron Star PSR J0614-3329 from NICER Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00965)
*M. C. Miller,A. J. Dittmann,I. M. Holt,F. K. Lamb,C. Chirenti,Z. Arzoumanian,J. Berteaud,S. Bogdanov,K. C. Gendreau,A. K. Harding,W. C. G. Ho,C. Kalapotharakos,S. M. Morsink,P. S. Ray,R. A. Remillard,Z. Wadiasingh,M. T. Wolff*
