TL;DR: 该论文利用NICER望远镜对318-Hz脉冲星PSR J0614-3329的X射线数据进行建模分析，该脉冲星的质量约为1.4-1.5倍太阳质量。最佳拟合模型包含三个均匀温度圆形热斑，给出赤道圆周半径的对称68%置信区间为9.88-12.77 km。研究还探讨了NICER与XMM-Newton数据的联合拟合，但发现联合拟合模型会系统性低估XMM-Newton数据。最后讨论了这些结果对其他中子星数据对致密物质性质的约束意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星半径测量（特别是利用NICER获取的X射线数据）为核饱和密度以上冷催化物态方程模型提供了宝贵约束，本研究的动机在于通过分析新的中子星（PSR J0614-3329）的NICER数据来进一步限制中子星核心致密物质的性质。

Method: 利用NICER数据对318-Hz脉冲星PSR J0614-3329进行X射线脉冲轮廓建模，探索含三个均匀温度圆形热斑的模型。同时进行NICER与XMM-Newton数据的联合拟合分析，并假定XMM-Newton背景可从周围天区观测中获知，评估模型对两组数据的兼容性。

Result: 最佳拟合模型为具有三个均匀温度圆形热斑的方案，给出赤道圆周半径的对称68%可信区间为9.88-12.77 km。联合拟合分析发现，能同时较好匹配两组数据的模型会在标准背景假设下系统性低估XMM-Newton观测数据。

Conclusion: PSR J0614-3329的半径测量为致密物质状态方程提供了额外的观测约束，但NICER与XMM-Newton数据之间的系统性不一致表明在联合分析中需要谨慎处理，后续结合其他中子星数据可进一步限制中子星核心致密物质的性质。

Abstract: Neutron star radius measurements, particularly using X-ray data collected with the Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER), have provided invaluable information for models of cold, catalyzed matter at densities above that of nuclear saturation. Here we present an analysis of NICER data on the 318-Hz pulsar PSR J0614-3329, which has a mass ~1.4-1.5 solar masses determined from radio observations. The best-fitting model we explore has three uniform-temperature circular hot spots and yields a symmetric 68% credible range for the equatorial circumferential radius of 9.88-12.77 km. We also explore joint fits to the NICER data and the X-ray Multi-Mirror (XMM-Newton) data on this pulsar, but find that the models that best fit both data sets systematically underpredict the XMM-Newton data when, as is standard, it is assumed that the XMM-Newton background is known from observations of surrounding fields. Finally, we discuss the implications of our results, combined with data on other neutron stars, for the properties of the dense matter in neutron star cores.

</details>


### [131] [The Missing MeV Window for Identifying Galactic PeVatrons: the need for a dedicated MeV Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00989)
*Martina Cardillo,Riccardo Campana,Yuri Evangelista,Fabio Muleri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文论述了利用MeV能段伽马射线观测（特别是中性π衰变产生的"π峰"特征）来鉴定银河系PeVatron候选体的粒子加速性质（强子vs轻子），并提出发展一个紧凑、聚焦、可快速实施的MeV伽马射线任务以填补当前观测空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHAASO等VHE设施已发现越来越多的银河系PeVatron候选体，但TeV-PeV伽马射线观测本身无法唯一确定辐射粒子的性质（强子还是轻子）。MeV能段（10-100 MeV）的中性π衰变"π峰"是鉴定强子加速的最可靠电磁特征，然而该能段是目前电磁频谱中观测最薄弱的区域，成为PeVatron源鉴定的主要瓶颈。

Method: 文章系统讨论了MeV伽马射线观测的科学驱动因素和观测需求，论证了探测中性π衰变"π峰"对区分强子与轻子辐射模型的关键作用，并探索了一个紧凑、聚焦、可快速上线的专用MeV伽马射线任务概念，作为更大规模下一代MeV设施的补充或先行方案。

Result: 提出了一个专门用于π峰研究的MeV伽马射线任务概念，该任务可在更大的下一代MeV设施开发期间填补即将到来的MeV观测空白，为鉴定银河系宇宙线加速器提供缺失的诊断手段。

Conclusion: MeV能段观测是区分强子与轻子起源、确认银河系PeVatron加速质子与原子核的不可或缺手段。一个快速部署的紧凑MeV任务将补充当前和未来的TeV天文台，最大化其科学回报，并加深对银河系宇宙线起源的理解。

Abstract: The identification of the sources responsible for accelerating Galactic cosmic-rays up to PeV energies remains a major open question in high-energy astrophysics. Recent observations by the Large High Altitude Air Shower Observatory (LHAASO) have revealed a growing population of Galactic PeVatron candidates. However, the detection of TeV-PeV gamma-rays alone does not uniquely identify the nature of the emitting particles. In many cases, both hadronic and leptonic scenarios can reproduce the observed very-high-energy emission, leaving the origin of the radiation unresolved. The most robust and least model-dependent electromagnetic tracer of hadronic acceleration is provided by neutral-pion decay, which produces the characteristic pion bump in the 10-100 MeV energy range. Observations in this energy band are therefore essential to establish whether candidate PeVatrons are accelerating protons and nuclei and, consequently, to assess their role in the origin of Galactic cosmic-rays. More broadly, the MeV domain addresses key astrophysical questions, but remains one of the least explored regions of the electromagnetic spectrum. As current and future VHE facilities expand the Galactic PeVatron census, the lack of MeV observations is becoming a major limitation for source identification. We discuss the astrophysical science drivers and observational requirements for a dedicated MeV gamma-ray capability optimized for pion-bump studies. We explore the concept of a compact, focused, and potentially fast-track mission capable of filling the impending MeV observational gap while more ambitious next-generation MeV facilities are developed and evaluated. Such a mission would provide the missing diagnostic needed to distinguish hadronic and leptonic emission, identify Galactic cosmic-ray accelerators, and fully exploit the scientific return of the emerging generation of TeV observatories.

</details>


### [132] [A Galactic microblazar as a potential accelerator of ultra-high-energy particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00990)
*Josep Martí,Pedro L. Luque-Escamilla,Benito Marcote,Leandro Abaroa,Arnau Aguasca-Cabot,Jorge A. Combi,Gustavo E. Romero,Josep M. Paredes,Federico García,Federico Fogantini,Enzo A. Saavedra,Daniel del Ser,Jakob van den Eijnden*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出IRAS 18293-0941是一个被忽视的"微耀变体"(microblazar)候选体——即喷流几乎对准视线方向的银河系X射线双星。通过多波段观测与理论研究，确认其呈现单侧准直射电喷流（符合相对论聚束效应），并可能关联附近的超高能伽马射线源LHAASO J1831-1007u*。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中缺少喷流方向几乎对准视线方向（从而产生类似blazar观测特征）的X射线双星/喷流系统实例，即"微耀变体"类天体。此类天体能解释银河系中的伽马射线源，填补河内喷流源分类学中的空白。

Method: 对候选源IRAS 18293-0941开展了密集的多波段观测与理论研究，包括光度测量和光谱分析以确定其双星性质，以及射电观测以检查喷流形态和相对论聚束效应表现；同时从物理上构建场景模型并校验其与附近超高能源LHAASO J1831-1007u*的可能联系。

Result: 该系统表现出典型双星的光度与光谱特征，同时呈现清晰的准直、单侧射电发射，与相对论运动沿小出射角方向投影的预期一致。仅缺少快速光变，可能被致密星周包层平滑掩盖。物理模型的一致性也为其与LHAASO J1831-1007u*的关联提供了可信度。

Conclusion: 该识别填补了银河系喷流源分类学上的空白，并强化了微耀变体与微类星体整个大家族在理解银河系内最高能天文现象中所扮演的角色。

Abstract: Context. Persistent jets from X-ray binaries which are aligned very close to the line of sight could be considered to be Galactic equivalents of blazars, or 'microblazars'. They are also expected to power gamma-ray sources. Aims. We intend to assess a serious candidate apparently fulfilling many of the requirements to be considered a genuine member of this class: IRAS 18293-0941. Methods. An intense multi-wavelength observational and theoretical study has been carried out on our proposed candidate source. Results. With photometric and spectroscopic properties typical of a binary star, this system exhibits clear collimated and one-sided radio emission matching the effects of relativistic motion along a reduced ejection angle. Only fast variability is not observed possibly smoothed by a dense circumstellar envelope. A physical scenario is consistently modeled that also gives credibility to its likely connection with LHAASO J1831-1007u*, an ultra-high-energy source in its immediate vicinity. Conclusions. Our reported identification not only helps to fill a gap in Galactic taxonomy, but also potentially strengthens the role of the microblazar and microquasar families in our understanding of the most energetic Milky Way phenomena.

</details>


### [133] [The thermal emission for knot A of radio galaxy M87 by Chandra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01083)
*S. Osone*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过Chandra X射线观测分析M87喷流中knot A的X射线能谱，通过专注于北侧无软X射线凹陷的背景区域，以高显著性确认了knot A中存在热发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷流可能压缩星际介质，导致喷流外部软X射线被压缩的星际介质吸收，而激波加热产生的热发射预期出现在喷流内部。此前已在HST-1和knot A中发现热发射，本研究旨在进一步确认knot A的热发射特征并处理背景干扰问题。

Method: 利用Chandra对M87喷流周围区域进行X射线成像研究，发现knot A外南侧区域存在软X射线凹陷。为排除该背景干扰，仅选取北侧无软X射线凹陷的区域作为背景，对knot A的X射线能谱进行分析。

Result: 由于南侧背景存在软X射线凹陷，若将其纳入背景分析会干扰结果。通过仅使用北侧区域作为背景，成功以高显著性确认了knot A中的热发射。

Conclusion: 研究证实了M87喷流knot A中激波加热产生的热X射线发射，并强调了正确选择背景区域（避开软X射线吸收凹陷区）对于确认喷流热发射的重要性。

Abstract: The jet may compress an interstellar medium and soft X ray outside the jet may be absorbed by a compressed interstellar medium and thermal emission heated by a shock is expected inside the jet. Thermal emission was found for HST-1 and knot A of M87 in X ray energy spectra analysis by Chandra (Osone, 2017). The X ray image of the surrounding of the jet of M87 was studied by Chandra (Osone 2024). I find a soft X ray dip in a south region outside knot A. Therefore, by taking only a north region outside knot A as a background, where there is no soft X ray dip, I analyze X ray energy spectra for knot A by Chandra and confirm thermal emission with a high significance.

</details>


### [134] [Complex Lags from Simple Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01109)
*Benjamin Ricketts,Gregoire Marcel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟简单的QPO（准周期振荡）玩具模型，研究了黑洞X射线双星中X射线定时分析产生的相位延迟和相干性等信号特征，探讨了不同物理设置（单一驱动信号、多重振荡、时变调制）如何影响定时产物，指出相干性比相位延迟更能区分不同物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞X射线双星中通过傅里叶分析得到的X射线定时信息（特别是QPO信号）已使用数十年，但QPO的产生机制仍是重大争议。作者希望通过简单的玩具模型模拟，提供更直观的物理直觉，帮助研究者理解信号中的现象（尤其是非线性相位延迟）如何在不同的物理设置下产生，避免对复杂数据过度解读或引入不当的奇异滞后机制。

Method: 作者对QPO类信号构建简单的物理玩具模型并进行模拟，主要考察三种场景：(1)单一驱动信号假设下的QPO特性；(2)信号中包含多重振荡的情况；(3)QPO由周期信号的时变调制产生时定时产物（相位延迟和相干性）的变化。

Result: 许多常见的驱动信号搭配简单物理设置能够重现与观测数据相似的复杂非线性相位延迟，但功率谱等特征仍无法完全匹配实际数据。信号中多个非相干过程难以复现数据中的行为。相干性在区分不同物理设置方面比相位延迟更有用。

Conclusion: 当对X射线双星这类复杂数据进行傅里叶分析时，研究者容易被诱导去引入奇异的滞后解释机制。本文为理解信号中不同现象（尤其是QPO）如何产生具有明确结构的非线性相位延迟提供了分析工具和物理直觉框架。

Abstract: X-ray timing information produced through Fourier analysis from the variable emission of black hole X-ray binaries has been used for several decades to provide key insights into the physical setup of these systems not measurable with spectroscopy. In particular, quasi-periodic oscillations within these systems have been of particular interest and remain the source of great debate on how they come about. We investigate the timing products of simple toy models of QPO variability to provide more intuition when thinking about signals produced by these sources. We simulate simple physical setups and show how phase lags and coherence of the signals change in these different setups. We first focus on properties of QPO like signals under a single driving signal assumption. We then investigate the case of multiple oscillations in a signal. Finally, we investigate how timing products change when QPOs are produced by time dependent modulation of periodic signals. Many simple physical setups with common driving signals are able to reproduce complex non-linear phase lags that resemble those present in the data. The changes in physical setup aligns with experience in the data, such as differing power spectra but fall short of reproducing the data as expected. Multiple incoherent processes present in the signal struggle to reproduce behaviour present in the data. Coherence seems to be a more useful tool for differentiating between setups. Fourier analysis with complicated data like that produced by X-ray binaries can lead one to be tempted to invoke exotic lag mechanisms without the appropriate framing. This paper attempts to help provide tools and intuition as to how different phenomena in signals (particularly relating to QPOs) can result in non-linear phase lags with explicit structure.

</details>


### [135] [Constraints on Buchdahl-Inspired Gravity from Future Pulsar Timing near Sgr A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01153)
*Jian-Ming Yan,Tao Zhu,Zong-Kuan Guo,Zhao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对一颗代表性脉冲星进行Fisher矩阵预报，评估了利用Sgr A*附近脉冲星计时观测约束Buchdahl-inspired R²引力理论（由单一偏差参数ε刻画）的能力。研究发现短轨道周期和高轨道偏心率能显著提高对ε的测量灵敏度，而S2-like轨道的基准情形可达到σε~10⁻⁴的统计灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的脉冲星计时观测有望成为探测超大质量黑洞附近引力物理的独特探针。作者希望评估这一观测手段对广义相对论替代理论（特别是Buchdahl-inspired R²引力）的约束能力，为未来的银河系中心脉冲星搜寻提供目标选择和观测设计方面的定量指导。

Method: 采用了一个自洽的计时框架，将轨道动力学与光传播延迟相综合，并在整个观测跨度上保持完整的计时解。通过Fisher矩阵预报方法，针对代表性脉冲星系统性地分离并研究了轨道几何参数（轨道周期、偏心率等）对ε精度的影响。同时以S2-like轨道（P_b=16年，e=0.88的假设脉冲星）作为基准进行了比较分析。

Result: 短轨道周期和高偏心率能显著增强对ε的灵敏度，这与近心点附近的大量观测贡献一致。对于S2-like轨道的假设脉冲星，在弱场、静态、球对称的计时模型下获得σε~10⁻⁴的统计灵敏度，该值与1PN展开的自然截断量级相当。该统计尺度比当前S2的95%置信区间半宽度（|ε|≈0.56）低数个数量级，但这一比较是启发性的，因为两者置信水平不同。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来银河系中心脉冲星搜寻的目标选择和观测活动设计提供了定量指导，表明脉冲星计时观测对Buchdahl-inspired R²引力的约束潜力巨大，但受限于当前模型中弱场、静态、球对称等理想化假设。

Abstract: Future pulsar timing observations near Sgr~A* offer a unique probe of gravitational physics in the vicinity of a supermassive black hole. We forecast the ability of such measurements to constrain a Buchdahl-inspired $R^2$ gravity, parameterized by a single deviation parameter $ε$, using a timing framework that self-consistently integrates orbital dynamics with light-propagation delays and preserves the full timing solution across the observing span. Through Fisher-matrix forecasts for a representative pulsar, we systematically isolate how the precision on $ε$ depends on orbital geometry. We find that shorter orbital periods and higher eccentricities significantly enhance sensitivity, consistent with a substantial contribution from observations near periastron. As a benchmark comparison, we further consider a hypothetical pulsar on an S2-like orbit ($P_b=16~{\rm yr}$, $e=0.88$) and obtain a statistical sensitivity of $σ_ε\sim 10^{-4}$ within the adopted weak-field, static, and spherically symmetric timing model. This sensitivity is comparable to the natural order-of-magnitude truncation scale of the 1PN expansion and should not be interpreted as a complete forecast for the real Sgr~A* system. Under the adopted idealized assumptions, the characteristic statistical scale is several orders of magnitude below the current S2 95\% confidence interval half-width ($|ε|_{\rm S2}^{\rm 95\%}\approx 0.56$), though this comparison is heuristic given the differing confidence levels. These trends provide quantitative guidance for target selection and campaign design in future Galactic-center pulsar searches.

</details>


### [136] [AGN X-ray coronae at high luminosity: A broadband study of RBS 229 and PG 1407+265](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01265)
*Alessandro Leonardo Lai,Stefano Bianchi,Andrea Marinucci,Enrico Piconcelli,Francesco Ursini,Elena Bertola,Giorgio Lanzuisi,Pierre-Olivier Petrucci,Daniel Stern,Cristian Vignali,Luca Zappacosta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过XMM-Newton、NuSTAR和Swift多波段观测，对两个高光度射电宁静类星体PG 1407+265和RBS 229的X射线发射冕进行了分析，发现其能谱可由"暖冕（软X射线超额）+热冕（硬X射线连续谱）"的双层准直散射模型自洽描述。两个源都探测到约70-80 keV的高能截断，且低于产生电子对失控的极限；RBS 229的暖冕参数随时间稳定，而PG 1407+265表现出强烈变异性，包括极端软超额事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核（AGN）的X射线发射通常被解释为吸积盘上方热冕的康普顿化过程，许多源还表现出显著的软X射线超额，常归因于温暖康普顿层。本研究旨在探索高光度射电宁静类星体中X射线发射冕的性质，检验"双层冕"（暖冕+热冕）情景能否一致地描述其宽波段能谱，以填补高光度区间的AGN冕研究空白。

Method: 将XMM-Newton EPIC与光学监测（Optical Monitor）数据、同步NuSTAR观测以及Swift长期监测数据相结合，对PG 1407+265（z=0.94）和RBS 229（z=0.334）进行多波段能谱分析，并使用康普顿化模型拟合其光谱。

Result: 两个类星体相对于标准α_OX-L_UV关系均表现出X射线亮于预期的特征，其中PG 1407+265的偏离最为显著。两者的能谱可由一个光学厚暖康普顿化成分（负责软X射线超额）和一个热冕（产生硬X射线连续谱）良好再现。两个源均探测到约70-80 keV的高能截断。RBS 229表现出中等反射和弱中性Fe K-alpha线，而PG 1407+265未探测到显著反射特征。RBS 229的暖冕参数在不同历元间保持一致，而PG 1407+265表现出强烈变异性，包括一次静止系中延伸至约3 keV的极端软超额事件。

Conclusion: 这些结果约束了在以往探索不足的高光度区间的AGN冕性质。高能截断与AGN光度和吸积率趋势大致一致，且低于失控成对产生的极限。两个源暖冕行为的不同表明，暖冕会响应吸积流的改变，但其物理状态保持在一个较窄的参数范围内。

Abstract: Context. The X-ray emission of active galactic nuclei (AGNs) is commonly interpreted as arising from Comptonisation in a hot corona above the accretion disc. A prominent soft X-ray excess is also observed in many sources and is often explained by a warm Comptonising layer. Aims. We investigate the X-ray-emitting coronae of the luminous radio-quiet quasars PG 1407+265 ($z=0.94$) and RBS 229 ($z=0.334$), testing whether a two-corona scenario consistently describes their broadband spectra. Methods. We combine XMM-Newton EPIC and Optical Monitor data with simultaneous NuSTAR observations and long-term Swift monitoring, and model the spectra using Comptonisation models. Results. Both quasars are X-ray loud relative to the standard $α_{\rm OX}$-$L_{\rm UV}$ relation, with PG 1407+265 showing the strongest deviation. Their spectra are well reproduced by a warm, optically thick Comptonising component accounting for the soft excess and a hot corona producing the hard X-ray continuum. Both sources show high-energy cut-offs at approximately 70-80 keV. RBS 229 exhibits moderate reflection and a weak neutral Fe K-alpha line, while no significant reflection features are detected in PG 1407+265. The warm-corona parameters of RBS 229 remain consistent across epochs, whereas PG 1407+265 shows strong variability, including an extreme soft-excess episode extending to approximately 3 keV in the rest frame. Conclusions. These results constrain AGN coronae in the poorly explored high-luminosity regime. The cut-offs are broadly consistent with trends in AGN luminosity and accretion rate and remain below the runaway pair-production limit. The contrasting variability suggests that the warm corona responds to changes in the accretion flow while remaining within a narrow range of physical conditions.

</details>


### [137] [Reconstruction of anomalous air showers with SKA-Low](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01347)
*Vital De Henau,Sjoerd Bouma,Justin Bray,Stijn Buitink,Arthur Corstanje,Edwin Dickinson,Tjibbe Gottmer,Brian Hare,Haoning He,Jörg Hörandel,Tim Huege,Clancy James,Mrinal Jetti,Philipp Laub,Xingyu Li,Marten Lourens,Hermann-Josef Mathes,Katie Mulrey,Anna Nelles,Subhadip Saha,Felix Schlüter,Olaf Scholten,Ralph Spencer,Christopher Sterpka,Sander ter Veen,Karen Terveer,Gia Trinh,Paulina Turekova,Darko Veberič,Keito Watanabe,Marc Waterson,Chao Zhang,Pengfei Zhang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究双峰广延大气簇射（double-bump showers）的射电信号特征。作者指出双峰簇射是蒙特卡洛模拟预测但尚未被实验直接探测到的一类事件，其独特的多脉冲射电足迹和频谱干涉图样可用于重建纵向剖面参数。SKA-Low凭借其密集天线阵列和宽频段将成为首个能探测该特征的观测设施。分析目标是：首先对比双峰簇射与普通簇射的射电信号差异，然后建立简化的两点发射模型解释频谱干涉图样并反演两个峰的高度，最后采用暴力搜索方法重建双峰的多个参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双峰簇射目前仅在蒙特卡洛模拟中被预测，尚无实验直接探测到。这类事件为探测强子相互作用和测量宇宙线质量组成提供了新机会。SKA-Low首次具备探测该特征的能力，因此需要开发从射电观测中重建双峰簇射纵向剖面参数的方法。

Method: 首先对比双峰簇射与平均簇射的射电信号；然后构建一个简化的两点发射模型来解释频率谱中的干涉图样，并对该模型进行反演以获得两个峰的粗略大气深度估计；最后采用暴力搜索（brute-force）方法重建双峰的多个参数。

Result: 论文展示了利用双峰簇射的射电频谱干涉图样重建纵向剖面参数的可行性——通过两点发射模型可获得峰位置的粗略估计，暴力搜索方法可进一步重建多个双峰参数。

Conclusion: 这是利用射电信号重建双峰簇射纵向剖面参数的第一步工作。该方法为SKA-Low探测双峰簇射并利用其独特元素特征进行质量组成测量铺平了道路，也为研究强子相互作用提供了新的探针。

Abstract: Double-bump showers are a surprising class of extensive air showers (EAS) predicted by Monte Carlo simulations, which, so far, no experiment has been able to directly detect. They occur when a high-energy secondary particle, the leading particle, travels significantly farther than the rest, creating a distinct double-peaked longitudinal profile. The unique radio footprint of double-bump showers, characterized by multiple pulses in the signals and interference patterns in the frequency spectra, enables reconstruction of longitudinal profiles from radio observations. With its dense antenna array and broad frequency range, SKA-Low will be the first observatory capable of detecting these features, offering a new opportunity to probe hadronic interactions and use the distinctive signatures of elements to provide new mass composition measurements.
  The goal of this analysis is to take the first steps toward using these radio signatures to reconstruct the relevant parameters of the longitudinal profile of a double-bump shower. We will start by explaining the radio signal of double-bump}showers compared to that of average showers. Then we will create a simple 2-point emission model to explain the interference patterns in the frequency spectra, which can be inverted to obtain rudimentary estimates of atmospheric depth of both peaks. Lastly, we implement a brute-force approach to reconstruct multiple parameters of the double bump.

</details>


### [138] [Extreme AGN Variability in WISE: Powerful Flares and Candidate Tidal Disruption Events in AGN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01421)
*Satya Teja Anuraag Upadhyayula,Daniel Stern,Kishalay De,Christos Panagiotou,Matthew J. Graham,K. E. Saavik Ford,Barry McKernan,Murray Brightman,J. A. Acevedo Barroso,Daniel H. McIntosh*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用WISE卫星超过10年的中红外巡天数据，系统研究活跃星系核（AGN）中的极端中红外变异性。通过对两个AGN中的剧烈耀发事件进行识别和分析，结合光学观测和光谱学证据，提出这两个事件可能是在AGN中发生的潮汐瓦解事件（TDE），其中光学耀发衰减远快于预期的t^{-5/3}衰减速率，可能暗示部分瓦解或中间质量黑洞对恒星的潮汐瓦解事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）主要通过光学或X射线巡天在非活跃星系中发现，但这类方法无法探测到尘埃遮蔽AGN中发生的耀发事件。由于AGN中也被预期会发生耀发事件，需要利用不受尘埃遮蔽影响的中红外波段进行系统搜索，以获取完整的TDE普查。

Method: 利用WISE任务超过10年、六个月全天空重复观测的中红外数据，通过极端中红外颜色变异性识别候选耀发事件；对两个AGN中的耀发事件进行后续光学观测和光谱学跟踪，分析耀发的多波段特性、谱线演化及光变曲线衰减行为。

Result: 在飞行员研究中识别出两个AGN中的极端耀发事件，其中一个事件同时被光学波段探测到。两个事件在耀发峰值后数年都表现出展宽的Hα发射线，但仅光学耀发源在更新的光谱中仍存在展宽发射。光学耀发的衰减远快于TDE预期的t^{-5/3}回退速率，暗示可能为部分瓦解事件或AGN吸积盘内嵌入的中间质量黑洞引起的潮汐瓦解。

Conclusion: 两个事件都很可能是AGN中的潮汐瓦解事件。该工作展示了WISE中红外数据在识别尘埃遮蔽AGN中TDE的独特能力，中红外数据能够探测事件的本质特征并在测光测量中提供耀发能量的热力学总量，从而构建更完整、更全面的TDE普查。

Abstract: We present first results from a systematic study of extreme mid-infrared variability of active galaxies across the $>10$-year baseline of the Wide-field Infrared Survey Explorer (WISE) mission. WISE, which observed the full sky with a six-month cadence, is sensitive to transient events in both unobscured and obscured galaxies. This paper focuses on extreme flares in active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Previous studies identified flares such as tidal disruption events (TDEs) in inactive galaxies, typically from optical or X-ray surveys. However, flares are also expected in AGN and optical/X-ray searches are insensitive to events in dust-enshrouded AGN. In this pilot study, we present flares in two AGN identified from extreme mid-infrared color variability. One flare was also detected at optical wavelengths. While both events showed broadened H$α$ emission several years after the flare peak, more recent spectroscopy reveals continued broadened emission only from the source with the optical flare. After considering several potential physical causes of the flares, including supernovae and micro-lensing, we suggest both events are TDEs in AGN. Intriguingly, the optical flare decays much faster than the expected $t^{-5/3}$ fallback rate expected for TDEs in quiescent galaxies, suggesting either a partial disruption or the tidal disruption of a star by an intermediate mass black hole embedded in the accretion disk of an AGN. This work demonstrates the power of WISE to identify the full census of TDEs, including events in dust-obscured AGN. Mid-infrared data probes the nature of the event and provides a bolometric measure of the flare energetics.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Reducing Every Set of 36 Consecutive Integers to Zero by Differences of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00098)
*Zhao Shen,Youran Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了一个由Hickerson和Kleber提出的组合数论问题：对于任意整数n，集合{n,n+1,...,n+35}能否通过反复操作（选取两个数a,b替换为|a²-b²|）归约为零。作者给出了显式的归约构造，并结合他们关于长度12和24的已有结果，完整刻画了所有满足条件的长为L的连续整数集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hickerson和Kleber提出了一个开放问题：是否对于每个整数n，36个连续整数构成的集合{n,n+1,...,n+35}都可以通过特定的二次差操作归约到零。这个问题关乎操作|a²-b²|下集合可归约性的完整刻画，此前仅知道长度为12和24的情况。

Method: 作者给出了一种显式的构造性归约方案，证明集合{n,n+1,...,n+35}可以归约到零。关键在于构造出足够的操作序列，使得整个集合最终通过归约过程变为单一元素0。

Result: 证明了对于每个整数n，36个连续整数{n,n+1,...,n+35}可以归约到零。结合已有的长度为12和24的结果，得出完整分类：长度为L的连续整数集合对任意n∈Z都可归约到零，当且仅当12整除L且L≥24。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了操作|a²-b²|下有限多重集可归约性的一个完整刻画：对于任意起始整数n，连续整数块可以归约到零的充分必要条件是其长度L为12的倍数且至少为24。这彻底回答了Hickerson和Kleber的问题及其推广。

Abstract: For a finite multiset of integers, repeatedly choose two entries $a,b$ and replace them with $|a^{2}-b^{2}|$. Hickerson and Kleber asked whether, for every integer $n$, the set ${n,n+1,\ldots,n+35}$ can be reduced to zero. We give an explicit reduction. Together with their results for lengths 12 and 24, this gives, for every positive integer $L$, [
{n,n+1,\ldots,n+L-1}\text{ reduces to zero for every }n\in\mathbb Z \Longleftrightarrow 12\mid L\text{ and }L\ge 24. ]

</details>


### [140] [Colombo's Determinant Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00101)
*Qianli Ma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整解决了Colombo于1928年提出的行列式问题：对互异的实数x_1,...,x_N（N≥2）和整数D≥1，证明了det[(x_j-x_i)^D]≠0当且仅当D≥N-1，且N为偶数或D为偶数。偶数幂情形由Dyn-Goodman-Micchelli（1986）覆盖，奇数幂情形通过严格的Pfaffian符号定理证明，并在Lean 4中完成了形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colombo于1928年提出了这个行列式非零性判定问题，历经近一个世纪尚未被完全解决。此前偶数幂情形已有结果，但奇数幂的情形一直悬而未决，本文的目标就是彻底关闭这个经典问题。

Method: 将问题分解为两个部分：偶数指数部分直接引用Dyn-Goodman-Micchelli（1986）的已知结论；奇数指数部分则需要证明一个严格的Pfaffian符号定理，通过Pfaffian（而不是行列式）的符号分析来处理奇数情形。此外，作者将完整的证明链在Lean 4证明助手中进行了形式化验证。

Result: 给出了Colombo 1928年行列式问题的完全刻画：det[(x_j-x_i)^D]≠0的充要条件是D≥N-1且（N为偶数或D为偶数）。这一条件覆盖了所有可能的(N,D)组合，是必要且充分的。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了这个跨越近一个世纪的历史数学问题。更重要的是，通过Lean 4的形式化验证，确保了证明的正确性并且为今后类似问题的机械化证明提供了可复用的基础。

Abstract: We completely solve Colombo's 1928 determinant problem. For distinct real $x_1,\ldots,x_N$, $N\geq 2$, and an integer $D\geq 1$, we prove that $\det[(x_j-x_i)^D]\neq 0$ if and only if $D\geq N-1$ and either $N$ is even or $D$ is even. The even-exponent case follows from Dyn--Goodman--Micchelli (1986); the remaining odd case is proved by a strict Pfaffian sign theorem. The new odd-exponent theorem and its complete proof chain have also been formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [141] [The shortest harmonic sums with decreasing denominator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00104)
*Wouter van Doorn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个数论问题：对正整数a，定义b(a)为使调和和从a到b的分母首次减小（相对于a到b-1）的最小整数b。先前研究已证明(b(a)-a)/log a的下极限存在且为正，本文确定了其精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这是一个关于调和数列和的分母行为的数论问题。先前工作仅证明了相关极限的下极限存在且为正数，但未能确定其具体数值。精确值的确定需要对问题有更深入的理解，可能涉及素数分布、分数分母性质等更深层的数论结构。

Method: 从摘要本身无法获得具体的技术方法细节，但可以推断该工作通过精细分析调和和的分母变化规律、结合素数分布的相关结果或更精细的数论工具，来精确计算所研究的极限下确界。

Result: 本文成功确定了极限inf (b(a)-a)/log a (当a趋于无穷时的下极限)的精确数值。

Conclusion: 通过对调和和分母行为的深入分析，作者最终给出了该数论问题中极限下确界的精确值，解决了一个先前仅知其存在性与正性而未精确计算的问题。

Abstract: For a positive integer $a$, let $b(a)$ be the smallest integer $b > a$ such that the denominator of $\frac{1}{a} + \frac{1}{a+1} + \cdots + \frac{1}{b}$ is smaller than the denominator of $\frac{1}{a} + \frac{1}{a+1} + \cdots + \frac{1}{b-1}$. Recently it was shown that the limit inferior $$\liminf_{a \to \infty} \left(\frac{b(a) - a}{\log a}\right)$$ exists and is positive. Here we find its exact value.

</details>


### [142] [From Common-Slot Chains to Heisenberg Central Products over Global Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00244)
*Marina Palaisti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在全局域上（p为不等于域特征之奇素数）统一描述了等度p次符号类的长度四公共槽链，给出其带符号归一化形式，使其能构造性地提供四个相容的范数方程。这些方程在特定的独立性假设下给出中央扩张H_{p^3}*H_{p^3}在C_p^4-库默尔扩张上的显式因式化根式实现，其中央嵌入障碍为两个符号类之差。当基域不含μ_p时，通过限制-余限制论证表明该障碍在环扩张F(μ_p)上被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对等价的p次符号类(a,b)_p=(c,d)_p，需要一种既能唯一记录公共槽链、又能具构造性用途（范数方程）的统一全局域表述。已有Gille-Szamuely的长度四链引理依赖数域上的唯一定理，但未强调带符号归一化形式，故难以直接用于构造中央扩张的根式实现。

Method: 将长度四公共槽链整理为统一全局域形式，引入带符号（signed）归一化，从而从链中读出四个相容的范数方程；对H_{p^3}*H_{p^3}的中央嵌入问题，利用这些范数方程与一个关于辅助库默尔类的自然独立性假设构造显式因式化根式；当μ_p不在基域时，采用限制-余限制论证，将嵌入障碍的探测归结到环扩张F(μ_p)上。

Result: 在μ_p⊂F时，符号类相等给出四个相容范数方程；在独立性假设下，这些方程将extraspecial中央积H_{p^3}*H_{p^3}实现为显式的因式化根式扩张，其中央嵌入阻塞恰为(a,b)_p-(c,d)_p。对一般情形（不含μ_p），限制-余限制论证表明该中央嵌入障碍在经过F(μ_p)后被检测，且其上问题为库默尔型，障碍仍为两类符号之差。

Conclusion: 全文建立了符号类链的带符号全局域统一形式，赋予其构造性范数方程语义，并完成了中央嵌入障碍在一般（非含μ_p）域上的探测归约，表明障碍本质上是Br(F)[p]中两个符号类的外差。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a global field and let $p$ be an odd prime with $p\neq\operatorname{char}F$. Assuming first that $μ_p\subset F$, we record in a uniform global-field form the length-four common-slot chain for equal degree-$p$ symbol classes and emphasize the signed normalization adapted to explicit norm constructions. Thus, from \[(a,b)_p=(c,d)_p\in\textrm{Br}(F)[p]\] one obtains $x,y\in F^\times$ such that \[(a,b)_p=(x^{-1},b)_p=(x,y)_p=(c^{-1},y)_p=(c,d)_p.\] For number fields, the underlying chain is the length-four chain lemma of Gille--Szamuely, based on Tate's simultaneous local--global theorem. The point developed here is that its signed form yields four compatible norm equations that can be used constructively. For the extraspecial central product $H_{p^3}*H_{p^3}$ over a $C_p^4$-Kummer extension, the central-embedding obstruction is $(a,b)_p-(c,d)_p$, while the four norm equations supplied by the chain assemble, under a natural independence hypothesis on the auxiliary Kummer classes, into an explicit factorized radical realization of the central product. Finally, when the ground field does not contain $μ_p$, we show by a restriction--corestriction argument that the central-embedding obstruction is detected after passage to the cyclotomic extension $F(μ_p)$. Over that field the problem is Kummer, and the obstruction is again the difference of the two symbol classes.

</details>


### [143] [Smooth Diffeomorphisms and Mahler's Problem on Liouville Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00376)
*Diego Marques*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了存在任意接近恒等映射的、保持定向的C∞微分同胚f（关于ℝ(x)超越），使得其所有迭代的任意阶导数均将任何实数域K和Liouville数集𝓛映回自身，从而回答了Mahler关于超越整函数是否具有类似性质的问題的强光滑对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maillet经典定理断言有理函数将Liouville数映到Liouville数；Mahler（1984）提出超越整函数是否具有同样性质的问题。本文回应这一开放问题，构造其光滑（而非全纯）强对应物。

Method: 证明结合三部分：(1) Maillet定理；(2) Körner光滑多项式缝合技术的算术精化；(3) 有理芽构造。非解析性位点可预先指定为任何与实代数和Liouville数不相交的非空紧致完全无处稠密集。

Result: 构造出任意接近恒等映射的C∞保向微分同胚f，对任意实数域K⊂ℝ、任意n≥1、任意m≥0都有D^m(f^∘n)(K)⊆K且D^m(f^∘n)(𝓛)⊆𝓛，其中f关于ℝ(x)是超越的。

Conclusion: 在C∞范畴给出了Mahler问题的强对等：存在超越的C∞自同胚不仅保持Liouville数集，还保持一切实数数域（及其任意迭代与导数），从而将代数的与超越的、光滑的与解析的情形在Liouville数结构下建立了新的联系。

Abstract: A classical theorem of Maillet asserts that every nonconstant rational function over $\mathbb{Q}$ maps Liouville numbers to Liouville numbers. In 1984, Mahler asked whether a transcendental entire function can have the same property. We prove a strong smooth counterpart: writing $\mathscr{L}$ for the set of Liouville numbers, there exist orientation-preserving $C^\infty$ diffeomorphisms $f:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}$, arbitrarily close to the identity and transcendental over $\mathbb{R}(x)$, such that for every real number field $K\subset\mathbb{R}$, every $n\geq 1$, and every $m\geq 0$, \[ D^m(f^{\circ n})(K)\subseteq K, \qquad D^m(f^{\circ n})(\mathscr{L})\subseteq\mathscr{L}. \] In fact, the non-analyticity locus may be prescribed as any nonempty compact perfect nowhere-dense set disjoint from the real algebraic and Liouville numbers. The proof combines Maillet's theorem with an arithmetic refinement of Körner's smooth polynomial sewing method and a rational-germ construction.

</details>


### [144] [Almost $k$-th powers in short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00386)
*Wenguang Zhai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究固定整数 k≥3 与大的实数 x，在区间 [x, x + x^{δ_k(θ)+ε}] 中寻找形如 n₁n₂⋯n_k 的整数，使得每个因子 n_j 与 n^{1/k} 的距离满足 |n_j − n^{1/k}| ≪ n^{θ/k}。作者证明了存在常数 1/2 ≤ δ_k(θ) < 1，并特别得到 δ₃(1) = δ₄(1) = 1/2，改进了 Chan 此前的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，研究短区间内整数的因子分解结构是一个经典问题。作者旨在改进 Chan 在短区间内寻找具有近似相等因子分解形式的整数（k 元乘积）的结果，将指数 δ_k(θ) 进一步缩小到 1/2，从而在更短的区间内保证此类整数的存在性。

Method: 采用解析数论中的方法（可能涉及圆法、指数和估计或筛法），通过精细地处理与 n₁n₂⋯n_k 形式相关的计数问题，结合对 θ 参数的设定条件 0 < c₀ < θ ≤ 1，建立 δ_k(θ) 的存在性，并对 k=3,4 且 θ=1 的情形进行特殊处理以得到 δ(1)=1/2 的最优结果。

Result: 证明了存在常数 1/2 ≤ δ_k(θ) < 1，使得上述区间含有满足条件的 k 元乘积形式整数；特别地，δ₃(1) = δ₄(1) = 1/2，这优于 Chan 之前获得的相应结果。

Conclusion: 本文在短区间内 k 因子乘积整数的存在性问题上取得了实质进展，将指数改进至 δ=1/2 的临界值（对 k=3,4 且 θ=1 的情形），为后续进一步优化和推广提供了基础。

Abstract: Let $k\geq 3$ be a fixed integer and $x$ be a large real number. Let $0<c_0<θ\leq 1$ be a fixed real number for some $c_0>0.$ In this paper we show that there exists a constant $1/2\leq δ_k(θ)<1$ such that the interval $[x, x + x^{δ_k(θ) +\varepsilon} ]$ contains an integer of the form $n_1n_2 \cdots n_k$ such that $|n_j-n^{1/k}|\ll n^{θ/k} \ (j=1,2, \cdots, k)$. Especially we have $δ_3(1) = δ_4(1) = 1/2,$ which improves previous results of Chan.

</details>


### [145] [Exceptional points on Atkin--Lehner quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00516)
*Eran Assaf,Sachi Hashimoto,Ari Shnidman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究星曲线 X_0^*(N)（经典模曲线 X_0(N) 模去全部 Atkin-Lehner 对合）上的有理点。作者在亏格 3 和亏格 4 的情形提供了新的例外点例子，同时给出亏格 g ≥ 5 时不存在例外点的证据，并探寻这些例外点背后的几何原因，特别对 Galbraith 在 X_0^*(137) 和 X_0^*(311) 上发现的例外点提出了几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Elkies 猜想：对充分大的 N，X_0^*(N) 只含有 CM 点或尖点有理点。该猜想与 Q-曲线（对全体 Galois 共轭均同源的椭圆曲线）的分类密切相关。找到并理解任何超出此猜想的"例外"有理点，是验证或反驳该猜想并深化对模曲线算术几何理解的关键。

Method: 论文在平方自由级别 N、亏格 3 和 4 的情形系统地构造并验证新的例外有理点；对亏格 g ≥ 5 的情形给出不存在例外点的数值与理论证据；并沿 Ogg 和 Mazur 的思路，研究可能"解释"这些例外点存在的底层几何机制（如特殊子簇、模解释或几何结构上的必然性）。

Result: 在亏格 3 和亏格 4 中发现了新的例外点实例；为亏格 g ≥ 5 无例外点提供了支持性证据；为 Galbraith 在 X_0^*(137) 和 X_0^*(311) 上发现的例外点提出了几何层面的解释框架。

Conclusion: 例外有理点在低亏格模曲线中确实存在且有迹可循的几何成因，但高亏格情形可能验证 Elkies 猜想。这一研究深化了对 Q-曲线参数化和模曲线上有理点分布的理解。

Abstract: We study the rational points on the star curve $X_0^*(N) := X_0(N)/W(N)$, the quotient of the classical modular curve $X_0(N)$ by the full group of Atkin--Lehner involutions, for squarefree levels $N$. Rational points on $X_0^*(N)$ parameterize $\mathbb{Q}$-curves, i.e.\ elliptic curves $E/\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ that are isogenous to all of their Galois conjugates. Elkies conjectures that $X_0^*(N)$ has only CM or cuspidal rational points for all large enough $N$. We call any other rational points "exceptional". In this article, we provide new examples of exceptional points in genus 3 and 4, and we give evidence that no exceptional points exist in genus $g \geq 5$. Moreover, we investigate the underlying geometric reasons that might "explain" why these exceptional points arise in the first place, in the vein of Ogg and Mazur. In particular, we propose geometric explanations for Galbraith's exceptional points on $X_0^*(137)$ and $X_0^*(311)$.

</details>


### [146] [Explicit Exponential Sum Estimates and Approximate Functional Equations for the Zeta Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00537)
*Natasha Dhiman,Habiba Kadiri,Emily Quesada-Herrera*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了Van der Corput截断Poisson求和公式(B-过程)的显式版本，通过改进的显式指数和估计，改善了Patel-Yang (2024)和Arias de Reyna (2024)的误差项。作为应用，作者获得了Riemann zeta函数近似函数方程误差项的精细显式估计，在特定范围内改进了Simonič (2020)的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，显式误差估计对于控制近似函数方程和zeta函数的数值计算至关重要。此前的显式结果如Patel-Yang和Arias de Reyna的Vander Corput截断Poisson求和公式仍有改进空间，Simoníč关于zeta函数近似函数方程的误差界也有可优化的余地，因此需要更精细的显式指数和估计来提升这些结果。

Method: 作者建立Van der Corput截断Poisson求和公式(B-过程)的显式版本，利用经过精化的显式指数和估计（如对指数和长度及曲率的精细控制）来压缩截断求和公式中的误差项，并将该结果应用于Riemann zeta函数近似函数方程的误差估计中。

Result: 得到了比Patel-Yang (2024)和Arias de Reyna (2024)更优的截断Poisson求和误差项；在Riemann zeta函数近似函数方程中，所获误差项的显式估计在特定参数范围内优于Simonič (2020)的结果。

Conclusion: 该项工作为解析数论中的显式估计提供了更精确的工具，改进了若干已知显式界，对zeta函数数值计算和相关问题的研究有实际价值。

Abstract: We show an explicit version of the Van der Corput truncated Poisson summation formula (B-process). By using refined explicit exponential sum estimates, this improves the error term of previous explicit results by Patel and Yang (2024) and Arias de Reyna (2024). As an application, we obtain fine explicit estimates for the error terms in approximate functional equations for the Riemann zeta function, improving on previous explicit results of Simonič (2020) in certain ranges.

</details>


### [147] [Regularized spectral expansion of a Rankin--Selberg period and moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01358)
*Jakub Dobrowolski,Subhajit Jana,Ramon Nunes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对GL_n上极大退化Eisenstein级数的模长的平方建立了正则化谱分解，将其视为GL_n上的Schwartz分布。尽管原问题在GL_n上，其谱分量完全由GL_2的自守谱所描述。作为应用，证明了GL_n×GL_n的Rankin–Selberg中心L值的二阶矩与GL_2 L值的混合矩之间的部分互反公式；并在广义Ramanujan猜想下，证明了上述Rankin–Selberg L函数在导子族上的二阶矩渐近公式，误差项具有平方根抵消强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高秩群GL_n上退化Eisenstein级数的谱分解及其在Rankin–Selberg L函数矩估计中的应用，旨在建立高维自守谱与低维（GL_2）自守谱之间的联系，并改进矩渐近公式的误差项。

Method: 利用正则化谱分解技术，将GL_n上极大退化Eisenstein级数模长平方作为Schwartz分布进行谱展开；结合Rankin–Selberg积分和自守谱理论，通过GL_2的自守谱描述GL_n问题的谱分量，并在广义Ramanujan猜想假设下分析导子族上的二阶矩。

Result: 建立了GL_n上退化Eisenstein级数的正则化谱分解定理；证明了GL_n×GL_n Rankin–Selberg中心L值二阶矩与GL_2 L值混合矩之间的部分互反公式；在广义Ramanujan猜想下获得了带平方根抵消强度误差项的导子族二阶矩渐近公式。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了高秩群GL_n上的谱分解问题可归结为GL_2的自守谱信息，并据此给出了Rankin–Selberg L值矩的新结果，同时展示了广义Ramanujan猜想对L函数矩分布中平方根抵消现象的作用。

Abstract: We establish a regularized spectral decomposition of the squared magnitude of a maximal degenerate Eisenstein series as a Schwartz distribution on $\mathrm{GL}_n$. Although the original problem is on $\mathrm{GL}_n$, its spectral components are described entirely by the automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{GL}_2$. As an application, we prove a partial reciprocity formula relating the second moment of $\mathrm{GL}_n\times\mathrm{GL}_n$ Rankin--Selberg central $L$-values and a mixed moment of $L$-values of $\mathrm{GL}_2$. We also prove, assuming the generalized Ramanujan conjecture, an asymptotic formula for the second moment of the above Rankin--Selberg $L$-functions over a conductor-aspect family with an error term of square-root-cancellation strength.

</details>


### [148] [Distribution of Kaneko's val function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00560)
*Toshiki Matsusaka*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明Kaneko的val函数（椭圆模j函数沿闭测地线的规范化周期积分）在按测地线长度排序的本原双曲共轭类上，其值集中于单点720；更一般地，对任意权为0的弱全纯模函数f，相应值集中于Atkin内积(f,1)_At给出的点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kaneko val函数在模曲面闭测地线上的分布行为，揭示其值的集中现象，并推广到更一般的弱全纯模函数情形。

Method: 结合Pollicott的均匀分布定理与连分数悬链流的遍历性，利用Bengoechea-Imamoglu的分解公式构造有界连续可观测函数来完成证明。

Result: 当本原双曲共轭类按测地线长度排序时，Kaneko val函数值集中于720；对任意权为0的弱全纯模函数f，值集中于Atkin内积(f,1)_At。

Conclusion: Kaneko val函数沿闭测地线具有强烈的集中现象，这一现象可通过均匀分布定理与连分数悬链流的遍历性加以严格证明，并自然推广到更一般的模函数类。

Abstract: Kaneko's val function is defined as the normalized cycle integral of the elliptic modular $j$-function along closed geodesics on the modular surface. We prove that, when primitive hyperbolic conjugacy classes are ordered by geodesic length, its values concentrate at the single point 720. More generally, an analogous concentration result holds for every weakly holomorphic modular function $f$ of weight 0, with the concentration point given by Atkin's inner product $(f, 1)_{\mathrm{At}}$. The proof combines an equidistribution theorem following Pollicott with the ergodicity of a continued-fraction suspension flow and uses the decomposition formula of Bengoechea-Imamoglu to construct a bounded continuous observable.

</details>


### [149] [A unification of Euler-Maclaurin and Euler-Boole summation formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00687)
*Yuan He*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个与广义Apostol-Bernoulli函数相关的求和公式，该公式统一了Euler-Maclaurin求和公式、Euler-Boole求和公式及其特征类比版本，并应用于推导一般幂和公式以及Berndt广义L函数在非正整数处的特殊值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Euler-Maclaurin求和公式和Euler-Boole求和公式及其特征模拟是数论和解析数论中的重要工具，但它们是分开处理的。作者试图通过广义Apostol-Bernoulli函数建立一个统一的框架，将这些看似不同的求和公式纳入一个统一的理论之下，从而简化推导过程并揭示内在联系。

Method: 本文以广义Apostol-Bernoulli函数为工具，推导出一个通用的求和公式。该公式作为统一框架，涵盖了Euler-Maclaurin求和公式、Euler-Boole求和公式以及它们的Dirichlet特征模拟版本。随后，作者将该统一求和公式应用于两个具体问题：推导一般幂和公式，以及计算Berndt广义L函数在非正整数处的特殊值。

Result: 成功建立了统一求和公式，并通过该公式获得了更一般化的幂和公式以及Berndt广义L函数在非正整数点的显式值表达式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个涵盖多个经典求和公式的统一求和公式，不仅深化了对Apostol-Bernoulli函数理论的理解，还为幂和与L函数特殊值等经典问题提供了统一且高效的处理途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a summation formula associated with the generalized Apostol-Bernoulli functions. This formula unifies the Euler-Maclaurin summation formula, the Euler-Boole summation formula, and the character analogues of these two formulas. We also apply it to obtain the general power sum formula and the special values of Berndt's generalized $L$-function at non-positive integers.

</details>


### [150] [Improved bounds in Birch's theorem for forms in many variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00757)
*Yijie Diao,Lena Wurzinger*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了齐次形式（次数d≥5）光滑Hasse原理所需变量数的最佳已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进齐次形式光滑Hasse原理在变量数上的最佳已知下界，减少所需变量数量，使定理适用范围更广。

Method: 针对次数d≥5的齐次形式，通过数学分析（推测涉及解析数论或代数数论技术）改进光滑Hasse原理成立的充分条件下所需的变量数上界。

Result: 获得了对次数d≥5齐次形式光滑Hasse原理所需变量数的改进，优于此前已知结果。

Conclusion: 光滑Hasse原理在更少的变量数条件下即可成立，推动了该方向最优定量结果的发展。

Abstract: We improve the best known result on the number of variables needed for the smooth Hasse principle for homogeneous forms of degree \(d\geq5\).

</details>


### [151] [Universal murmuration and Hecke augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00777)
*Shenghao Hua,Chung Pang Mok*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆曲线素数次幂系数的murmuration现象，提出根数加权平均的素数次幂系数与素数系数共享同一前导分布的猜想，并在水平方向的权2方形水平新形式下证明了一般固定次幂k的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数系数的椭圆曲线murmuration模式已被发现有助于预测算术标签（如rank）。自然的问题是：素数次幂系数是否也呈现类似的murmuration模式，并能用于改进根数与秩的预测？该文旨在将Zubrilina的素数情形和Kundu-Müller的平方情形推广到所有固定次幂k。

Method: 论文提出猜想：根数加权平均的单位归一化素数及素数次幂系数，在有效位置p^k/X（X为导子尺度）处具有相同的前导分布。对于权2固定方形水平的新形式，在合适的短窗和增长条件下，对所有固定k证明该原则，使用解析数论方法推广先前的素数情形与平方情形分析。

Result: 在水平方面对所有固定正整数k证明了根数加权素数幂系数的murmuration前导分布与素数情形一致。实验表明，包含素数幂特征的模型改善了根数预测，并在区分秩0与秩1方面获得较小增益。

Conclusion: 素数次幂系数同样表现出murmuration现象并遵循统一的前导分布原则，且在实际预测中辅助根数和秩的判定，为理解椭圆曲线算术数据的统计结构提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: Prime coefficients of elliptic curves exhibit murmurations, statistical patterns that support the prediction of arithmetic labels. We conjecture that root number weighted averages of unitary normalized coefficients at primes and prime powers sample the same leading profile when placed at the effective position $p^k/X$, where $X$ is the conductor scale. For weight $2$ newforms of squarefree level in the level aspect, we prove this principle for every fixed $k$ under suitable short-window and growth conditions, extending Zubrilina's prime case and the square-case analysis of Kundu and Müller. Experiments with elliptic curve isogeny class representatives show that the resulting prime power features improve root number prediction and give a smaller gain in distinguishing ranks $0$ and $1$.

</details>


### [152] [Covering Projective Height Balls by Subspaces in Rigid Adelic Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00988)
*Ruida Di,Runjie Hu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究刚性adelic空间上射影高度球的覆盖问题与一般线性位置子集的最大基数，证明了当半径足够大时，两者量级均为Ψ_E(R)^{[K:Q]}，其中Ψ_E(R)是Roy–Thunder最小值的显式表达式，比较常数可有效计算且对空间一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的覆盖问题和一般位置问题（如覆盖单位球所需超平面数、Singer–Cameron–Erdős问题等）在数论与几何中具有重要地位。本文将其推广到刚性adelic空间这一统一框架下，探索Roy–Thunder最小值如何刻画射影高度球的覆盖与独立集结构的渐近行为。

Method: 通过将覆盖数g_E(R)与一般位置最大基数h_E(R)联系起来，利用Roy–Thunder极小值理论分析高度球的几何结构。当R相对于最后一个Roy–Thunder最小值足够大时，建立这些量的精确量级估计，并给出显式且对E一致的比较常数。作为特例，标准adelic空间K^n的情形推导出R^{[K:Q]n/(n-1)}的阶。

Result: 证明了对足够大的R，g_E(R)和h_E(R)均具有量级Ψ_E(R)^{[K:Q]}，比较常数是有效可计算的且关于E一致。对标准adelic空间K^n，给出了阶R^{[K:Q]n/(n-1)}的结果。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了刚性adelic空间中覆盖数、一般位置基数的精确渐近阶，统一并推广了此前关于标准高度球的已知结论，展示了Roy–Thunder最小值在刻画高度几何中的核心作用。

Abstract: Let $E$ be an $n$-dimensional rigid adelic space over a number field $K$. We study the minimum number $g_E(R)$ of proper $K$-subspaces needed to cover the projective height ball of radius $R$, together with the maximum cardinality $h_E(R)$ of a subset in linear general position. We show that, once $R$ is sufficiently large compared with the last Roy--Thunder minimum of $E$, both quantities have order $Ψ_E(R)^{[K:\mathbb Q]}$, where $Ψ_E(R)$ is an explicit expression in the Roy--Thunder minima. The comparison constants are effectively computable and uniform in $E$. For the standard adelic space $K^n$, this gives order $R^{[K:\mathbb Q]n/(n-1)}$.

</details>


### [153] [Sharp lower bounds for shifted moments of Dedekind zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01101)
*Benjamin Durkan,Nilmoni Karak,Kamalakshya Mahatab*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在假设GRH成立的前提下，证明了临界线上移位Dedekind ζ函数乘积的锐利下界，其相关因子由Galois群中双陪集稳定子的固定域上的Dedekind ζ函数乘积表示，并结合作者早先的上界结果，确定了Galois与非Galois数场移位矩的数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多个数域K_1,...,K_r的Dedekind ζ函数在临界线上的移位矩。此前作者已证明相应的上界，亟需匹配的下界以确定这些移位矩的精确数量级，且需同时涵盖Galois与非Galois数域的情形。关键挑战在于下界的证明需要处理多个数域Galois闭包的复合域L，并刻画相关因子的精确结构。

Method: 假设ζ_L的广义黎曼假设(GRH)成立，对任意固定的正实数指数和至多T/2的均匀移位，证明临界线上移位Dedekind ζ函数乘积的锐利下界。相关因子通过对Gal(L/Q)中双陪集稳定子的固定域构造Dedekind ζ函数乘积来显式表达。该下界与作者此前的上界结果相结合。

Result: 在GRH假设下获得了任意固定正实数指数、移位大小不超过T/2的均匀下界，相关因子具有双陪集稳定子固定域上Dedekind ζ函数乘积的精确结构；与已有的上界结合，完全确定了Galois与非Galois数场的移位矩数量级。

Conclusion: 结合上界与下界结果，彻底解决了多数域Dedekind ζ函数临界线上移位矩的数量级问题，涵盖了Galois与非Galois情形，相关因子由Galois群的双陪集稳定子所对应固定域的ζ函数精确刻画。

Abstract: Let $K_1,\cdots,K_r$ be fixed number fields, and let $L$ be the compositum of their Galois closures. Assuming GRH for $ζ_L$, we prove a sharp lower bound for products of shifted Dedekind zeta functions on the critical line, for arbitrary fixed positive real exponents and uniformly for shifts of size at most $T/2$. The correlation factor is expressed as a product of Dedekind zeta functions of the fixed fields of double-coset stabilisers in $\textrm{Gal}(L/\mathbb{Q})$. Combined with the corresponding upper bound by the authors, determines the order of magnitude of these shifted moments for both Galois and non-Galois fields.

</details>


### [154] [Nonabelian Chabauty for the Thrice-punctured Line over Cyclotomic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01128)
*Minhyong Kim,Xiang Li,Martin Lüdtke*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究环分圆域上动机Chabauty–Kim方法，具体针对K=Q(ζ₈)、S={(1-ζ₈)}情形，构造了显式的对数Kim函数（深度至4），验证了多个素数上的Kim猜想，并发现Chabauty–Kim轨迹中包含由Q_p中单位根产生的例外点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机Chabauty–Kim方法旨在确定射影直线去掉三个点后的S-整点集合，但该方法的实际可行性取决于能否显式构造Kim函数。此前相关工作多限于有理数域，本文希望将这一方法推广到环分圆域，并在具体实例中验证Kim猜想。

Method: 以K=Q(ζ₈)、S={(1-ζ₈)}为具体目标，构造多对数（polylogarithmic）商上的Kim函数至深度4，利用p进多对数理论计算Chabauty–Kim轨迹，并对多个素数检验Kim猜想。同时分析轨迹中额外点与Q_p中单位根的关联。

Result: 获得了深度4以内的显式多对数Kim函数，在若干素数上证实了Kim猜想（即S-整点集合与Chabauty–Kim轨迹一致）；同时发现该轨迹除S-整点外还包含某些来自p进单位根的例外点，并对此给出了说明。

Conclusion: 工作表明Motivic Chabauty–Kim方法可以扩展到环分圆域并产生显式结果，但例外点的存在提示在多对数商下的Chabauty–Kim轨迹可能比S-整点集合更大，需要在更高商或更精细的结构中加以排除。

Abstract: In this paper we study the motivic Chabauty--Kim method, which aims to determine the set of $S$-integral points of $\mathbb{P}^1\smallsetminus \{0,1,\infty\}$, over cyclotomic fields. We focus on the case $K=\mathbb{Q}(ζ_8)$ and $S=\left\{(1-ζ_8)\right\}$, where we obtain explicit polylogarithmic Kim functions up to depth $4$ and verify Kim's Conjecture for several primes. We also observe and explain that the Chabauty--Kim locus for the polylogarithmic quotient contains, in addition to the $S$-integral points, certain exceptional points arising from roots of unity in $\mathbb{Q}_p$.

</details>


### [155] [An improvement on the largest prime factor of $n^2+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01327)
*Hector Pasten*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式序列 $n^2+1$ 的最大素因子下界。Mahler (1933) 和 Chowla (1934) 证明了最大素因子至少以 $\log_2 n$ 的速度增长；作者在 2023 年将其改进为 $(\log_2 n)^2/\log_3 n$；本文进一步将下界提升至 $(\log_2 n)^2/\log_4 n$，并证明了当该下界接近最优时，$n^2+1$ 的多个素因子也同时满足该下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于对数论中经典问题——多项式序列最大素因子增长下界的探索。该问题可追溯至19世纪末Störmer的工作，Mahler和Chowla在1930年代给出了初步结果。作者旨在不断改进该下界，深入理解$n^2+1$型整数的素因子分布结构，并探究下界逼近最优值时其素因子的精确行为。

Method: 本文采用解析数论方法，在前人工作基础上通过对数化迭代技术与素数理论工具，将最大素因子的下界从$(\log_2 n)^2/\log_3 n$改进为$(\log_2 n)^2/\log_4 n$。证明中涉及对几乎所有接近下界的$n$的素因子分布进行精细分析，从而得出多个素因子同时满足下界的结果。

Result: 主要结果两方面：(1) 将$n^2+1$最大素因子的下界改进为$(\log_2 n)^2/\log_4 n$，比2023年的结果进一步迭代了对数阶；(2) 当该下界几乎达到最优（即几乎取等）时，证明了不仅最大素因子、而且$n^2+1$的许多素因子都满足相同的下界。

Conclusion: 本文在最大素因子下界问题上取得了新的进展，将指数改进推进到$\log_4$阶，并揭示了下界接近最优时素因子分布的刚性结构——即多个而非仅一个素因子具有较大阶。这一结果丰富了人们对$n^2+1$型整数算术性质的理解，也为未来进一步优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: The study of the largest prime factor in polynomial sequences can be traced back at least to the late 19th century in the work of Störmer. Mahler (1933) and Chowla (1934) proved that the largest prime factor of $n^2+1$ grows at least as fast as $\log_2 n$. In 2023 we improved this to $(\log_2 n)^2/\log_3 n$. In this note we show the lower bound $(\log_2 n)^2/\log_4 n$, and that when this bound is nearly sharp it also holds for many prime factors of $n^2+1$.

</details>


### [156] [New classes of trace-form permutation polynomials and their compositional inverses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01350)
*Kirpa Garg*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文构建了有限域F_{q^2}上形如x + γTr^{q^2}_q(h(x))的置换多项式，提出了系数覆盖整个域而非限制于真子域的两族置换多项式，并给出了它们的合成逆，方法涉及Kloosterman和的求值及有限域上方程的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造置换多项式是有限域理论中的重要问题，现有构造往往将系数限制在真子域（即trace函数核的补空间），这限制了置换多项式的多样性和应用范围。作者旨在突破这一限制，构造系数覆盖整个域的置换多项式，并同时解决其合成逆的计算问题。

Method: 论文采用x + γTr^{q^2}_q(h(x))的形式构造置换多项式，其中系数γ取遍整个F_{q^2}。证明技术包括两个方面：一是利用Kloosterman和来评估某些和式，二是对有限域上一类特定方程进行深入分析。基于这些工具，给出了两族新的置换多项式，并进一步推导了它们的合成逆的显式表达式。

Result: 成功构造了两族置换多项式，其系数范围覆盖F_{q^2}中所有元素而非限于子域，并计算得到了这两族置换多项式的合成逆。所发展的Kloosterman和求值技巧及有限域方程分析方法被作者认为具有独立的研究价值。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了已有构造范式，使得置换多项式的系数更具一般性，同时提供了合成逆的显式计算，为置换多项式在编码理论、密码学等领域的应用提供了更丰富的数学工具。

Abstract: We construct permutation polynomials of the form x + γTr^{q^2}_q(h(x)) over the finite field F_{q^2}. More precisely, we present two families ofpermutation polynomials whose coefficients range over all elements of the field rather than being restricted to a proper subfield. We also compute the compositional inverses of both families. Our techniques involve the evaluation of certain Kloosterman sums together with the analysis of some equations over finite fields, and we believe these can be of independent interest.

</details>


### [157] [Confluence relations for $q$-analogues of multiple zeta values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01458)
*Minoru Hirose*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过将经典confluence关系构造适配到q-设置中，为q-模拟的多重zeta值构建了confluence关系，并证明所得关系族包含stuffle积关系以及（在某个猜想成立时）Bradley-Zhao和Schlesinger-Zudilin模型的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta值的q-模拟是数论和量子代数中的重要研究对象，但有关其代数关系（特别是confluence关系）的系统性构造尚不完善。本文旨在将经典多重zeta值的confluence关系构造推广到q-设置，以建立统一的代数关系框架。

Method: 构造依赖于辅助变量z的q-模拟多重对数函数，利用其q-微分方程产生函数关系，并通过（正则化）极限z→1获得q-多重zeta值之间的confluence关系。

Result: 证明了所构造的confluence关系族包含stuffle积关系；在某个猜想性恒等式成立的假设下，还包含Bradley-Zhao和Schlesinger-Zudilin两种模型的q-多重zeta值之间的对偶关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为q-模拟多重zeta值的代数结构提供了统一的confluence关系框架，将两类已知模型（Bradley-Zhao与Schlesinger-Zudilin）纳入同一关系体系中，但部分关键对偶关系的建立依赖于一个尚未证明的猜想。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct confluence relations for $q$-analogues of multiple zeta values by adapting the classical construction to the $q$-setting. Our construction uses $q$-analogues of multiple polylogarithms depending on an auxiliary variable $z$, functional relations arising from their $q$-differential equations, and the (regularized) limit as $z$ tends to $1$. We prove that the resulting family of relations contains the stuffle product relations and, assuming a certain conjectural identity, the duality relations for both the Bradley--Zhao and Schlesinger--Zudilin models.

</details>


### [158] [A General Construction of Codes from Drinfeld Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01484)
*Alessandro Giannoni,Giacomo Micheli,Mihran Papikian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过限制Drinfeld模的有界次数态射到素特征扭转，构造了加性秩度量码与和秩度量码，并给出了其参数、Singleton缺陷分析及解码算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索代数几何/数论对象（Drinfeld模）与编码理论（秩度量码、和秩度量码）之间的深层联系，旨在构造参数良好的新码族，并发展相应的解码理论。

Method: 利用超奇异Drinfeld模的态射空间稳定化公式，通过将态射限制到素特征扭转（及多个degree-m扭转模）构造加性秩度量码/和秩度量码；对特征(T)中的φ_T=τ^r情形，将其识别为由中心斜多项式支撑的斜中国剩余定理码，并推导其poly-skew重量与和秩重量的精确关系；对一般超奇异情形导出Welch-Berlekamp型滤波方程用于解码。

Result: 构造出F_q-维数为mrt-c、最小距离r-t+1的秩度量码（c=r(r-1)(d-1)/2），以及相同维数、最小距离≥ℓr-t+1的ℓ-fold和秩度量码；证明了其归一化Singleton缺陷趋于零，且在特征(T)下获得显式MSRD族；建立了poly-skew重量= m×和秩重量的精确恒等式，给出专门化的Singleton型界及多项式时间唯一解码器（可达完全唯一解码半径），同时为一般情形提供了滤波方程解码方案。

Conclusion: Drinfeld模提供了一种系统构造秩度量/和秩度量码的框架，既能产生MSRD码族（从而在Singleton界上达到最优），又具备实用的解码算法，展示了算术几何在编码理论中的新应用。

Abstract: We construct additive rank-metric and sum-rank-metric codes from Drinfeld modules by restricting bounded-degree morphisms to prime-to-characteristic torsion. For supersingular Drinfeld modules of rank $r$ in characteristic $\mathfrak{p}$ of degree $d$, the stabilization formula for morphism spaces yields rank-metric codes of $\mathbb{F}_q$-dimension $mrt-c$ and minimum distance $r-t+1$, where $c=r(r-1)(d-1)/2$. Simultaneous restriction to $\ell$ distinct degree-$m$ torsion modules gives additive sum-rank codes of the same dimension and minimum distance at least $\ell r-t+1$. Their normalized Singleton defects tend to zero, while in characteristic $(T)$ the module $φ_T=τ^r$ makes the defect vanish and produces an explicit MSRD family. We identify this family with a skew Chinese remainder theorem code supported on central skew polynomials and prove that its poly-skew weight is exactly $m$ times its sum-rank weight. This gives a specialized Singleton-type bound and a polynomial-time unique decoder up to the full sum-rank unique-decoding radius. We also derive a Welch-Berlekamp-type filter equation for the general supersingular sum-rank construction; it becomes an effective decoder whenever bases of the relevant morphism spaces and the restriction maps are computable.

</details>


### [159] [On the Ramification of Quadratic Fields where there is a torsion growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01485)
*Miguel Pineda-Martín*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有理椭圆曲线在二次域上扭群增长时，添加素数阶挠点后域的歧化情况，利用曲线的导子、素数ℓ和坏约化处的Kodaira符号等不变量来描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已建立了椭圆曲线导子素因子与二次域判别式素因子之间的关系，本文希望进一步深入，精确刻画添加素数阶挠点后扩张域的歧化结构。

Method: 利用椭圆曲线的导子、挠点的素数阶ℓ以及坏约化处的Kodaira符号等曲线不变量，计算添加挠点后域的歧化信息。

Result: 给出了添加素数阶挠点后域K的歧化性质与椭圆曲线不变量之间的精确关系。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了先前关于椭圆曲线扭群在二次域增长时的研究，用曲线的不变量完整描述了扩张域的歧化。

Abstract: Let $E/\mathbb{Q}$ be a rational elliptic curve whose torsion group grows over a quadratic field $K$. In a previous paper, a relation between the primes of the conductor of $E$ and the primes that divide the discriminant of $K$ was shown. In the present paper, we go further in this study and we compute the ramification of the field $K$, when a torsion point of prime order $\ell$ is added, in terms of invariants of the curve. Concretely, the conductor, the prime $\ell$ and the Kodaira symbol at the primes of bad reduction.

</details>


### [160] [A Proof of Fraenkel's Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01570)
*Hu Tan,Ying Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Fraenkel猜想：任何将整数划分为至少三条具有不同模数的Beatty序列的分割，其二元密度必为1,2,4,...,2^{m-1}（以2^m-1归一化）。证明通过一个无维数的中间命题（每个此类分割都包含至少1/3密度的分量），结合Fourier消元、除数集中恒等式、一致解析估计及三个精确有限验证完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fraenkel猜想是数论中关于Beatty序列（即齐次线性形式打点序列）划分整数集的一个长期未解猜想。它刻画了具有不同模数的Beatty序列划分整数时密度必须遵循的二进制结构，该猜想在组合数论、离散几何及相关领域中具有重要地位。

Method: 证明策略分为几步：（1）将分割约化为原始共同周期数据；（2）通过Fourier消元得到一个有限的反正弦系统（inverse-sine system）；（3）证明当所有密度均低于1/3时该系统不可能存在，利用除数集中恒等式并结合一致解析估计和三个精确有限验证（全程使用整数或有理数运算）；（4）由密度界得到平均间距至多为3的分量，删除该分量保持平衡性，且所有存活的周期平衡集仍是理性Beatty集；（5）用归纳确定存活的二元尺度，再用两条序列不相交判别准则迫使被删密度恰好为下一个二元尺度，最终导出所需密度模式。

Result: 成功证明了Fraenkel猜想，即具有不同模数的至少三条Beatty序列对整数的划分，其归一化密度必然是1,2,4,...,2^{m-1}（除以2^m-1）的二进制序列模式。

Conclusion: 本文通过将问题转化为中间的无维数密度下界命题并综合运用Fourier分析、除数恒等式、精确有限验证和归纳论证，完整解决了Fraenkel猜想。

Abstract: Fraenkel's conjecture asserts that a partition of the integers into at least three Beatty sequences with distinct moduli has the binary densities $1,2,4,\ldots,2^{m-1}$, normalized by $2^m-1$. We prove the conjecture through a dimension-free intermediate statement: every such partition contains a component of density at least 1/3. After reducing the partition to primitive common-period data, Fourier cancellation produces a finite inverse-sine system. We prove that no such system can exist when every density is below 1/3. The proof combines a divisor-concentration identity with uniform analytic estimates and three exact finite verifications, all carried out with integer or rational arithmetic. The component supplied by the density bound has mean spacing at most three. Deleting it preserves balance, and every surviving periodic balanced set is again a rational Beatty set. Induction determines the surviving binary scales, while a two-sequence disjointness criterion forces the deleted density to be the next binary scale. This yields the asserted density pattern.

</details>


### [161] [Ten unknowns for Hilbert's tenth problem over the integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01594)
*Zhi-Wei Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将希尔伯特第十问题中无解算法所需的不定元数量从11个改进到10个，证明整数上的多项式方程（含10个未知数）是否存在整数解是不可判定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特第十问题（整数域上）由Matiyasevich于1970年给出否定解答，但此前已知结果仅将不可判定性限制在不小于11个未知数的任意多项式方程上。本文的目标是进一步压缩未知数数量，将结果从"11个未知数"推进到"10个未知数"，使该问题更接近最优限度。

Method: 本文通过数学构造和归约技术，将任意具有较多未知数的丢番图方程归约到仅含10个未知数的等价方程，从而证明了即使是10个未知数的整数多项式方程，其整数解的存在性也是不可判定的。该方法改进并超越了此前的11个未知数定理。

Result: 证明了不存在算法能够判定任意含整数系数且含10个未知数的多项式方程P(z1,...,z10)=0在整数域上是否有解，将此前11个未知数的结果改进为10个未知数。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特第十问题在10个未知数的情况下仍然不可判定，这进一步收紧了该问题的不可判定性边界，是对Matiyasevich否定解答的重要改进。

Abstract: Hilbert's Tenth Problem over the integers was solved negatively by Y. Matiyasevich in 1970. In this paper, we prove that there is no algorithm to determine for any polynomial equation $P(z_1,\ldots,z_{10})=0$ (with integer coefficients and ten unknowns) whether it has integer solutions. This improves the previous 11 unknowns theorem.

</details>
